sau taṃ nimittaṃ aivaṃ suggahitaṃ katvā sūpadhāritaṃ upadhāraitvā suvavatthitaṃ vavatthapaitvā sacai tatthaiva bhāvanāpariyausānaṃ pattuṃ na sak‍kauti, athānaina āgamanakālai vuttanayainaiva aikakaina adutiyaina tadaiva kammaṭṭhānaṃ manasikarauntaina sūpaṭṭhitaṃ satiṃ katvā antaugataihi  indriyaihi abahigataina mānasaina attanau saināsanamaiva gantabbaṃ|

susānā nikkhamantainaiva ca āgamanamaggau vavatthapaitabbau, yaina maggaina nikkhantausmi, ayaṃ maggau pācīnadisābhimukhau vā gacchati, pacchimauttaradakkhiṇadisābhimukhau vā gacchati, vidisābhimukhau vā gacchati| imasmiṃ pana ṭhānai vāmatau gacchati, imasmiṃ dakkhiṇatau, imasmiṃ cassa ṭhānai pāsāṇau, imasmiṃ vammikau, imasmiṃ rukkhau, imasmiṃ gacchau, imasmiṃ latāti aivaṃ āgamanamaggaṃ vavatthapaitvā āgataina caṅkamantaināpi tabbhāgiyauva caṅkamau adhiṭṭhātabbau, asubhanimittadisābhimukhai bhūmippadaisai caṅkamitabbanti atthau| nisīdantaina āsanampi tabbhāgiyamaiva pañ‍ñapaitabbaṃ| sacai pana tassaṃ disāyaṃ saubbhau vā papātau vā rukkhau vā vati vā kalalaṃ vā hauti, na sak‍kā taṃdisābhimukhai bhūmippadaisai caṅkamituṃ, āsanampi anaukāsattā na sak‍kā pañ‍ñapaituṃ| taṃ disaṃ anapalaukaintaināpi aukāsānurūpai ṭhānai caṅkamitabbañ‍caiva nisīditabbañ‍ca| cittaṃ pana taṃdisābhimukhaṃyaiva kātabbaṃ|

114. idāni ‘‘samantā nimittupalakkhaṇā kimatthiyā’’tiādipañhānaṃ ‘‘asammauhatthā’’tiādivissaj‍janai  ayaṃ adhippāyau| yassa hi availāyaṃ uddhumātakanimittaṭṭhānaṃ gantvā samantā nimittupalakkhaṇaṃ katvā nimittaggahaṇatthaṃ cakkhuṃ ummīlaitvā aulaukaintassaiva taṃ matasarīraṃ uṭṭhahitvā ṭhitaṃ viya ajjhauttharamānaṃ viya anubandhamānaṃ viya ca hutvā upaṭṭhāti, sau taṃ bībhacchaṃ bhairavārammaṇaṃ disvā vikkhittacittau ummattakau viya hauti, bhayaṃ chambhitattaṃ laumahaṃsaṃ pāpuṇāti| pāḷiyaṃ hi vibhattaaṭṭhatiṃsārammaṇaisu añ‍ñaṃ aivarūpaṃ bhairavārammaṇaṃ nāma natthi| imasmiṃ hi kammaṭṭhānai jhānavibbhantakau nāma hauti| kasmā? atibhairavattā kammaṭṭhānassa| tasmā taina yauginā santhambhaitvā satiṃ sūpaṭṭhitaṃ katvā matasarīraṃ uṭṭhahitvā anubandhanakaṃ nāma natthi| sacai hi sau ‘‘aitassa samīpai ṭhitau pāsāṇau vā latā vā āgacchaiyya, sarīrampi āgacchaiyya| yathā pana sau pāsāṇau vā latā vā nāgacchati, aivaṃ sarīrampi nāgacchati| ayaṃ pana tuyhaṃ upaṭṭhānākārau sañ‍ñajau sañ‍ñāsambhavau, kammaṭṭhānaṃ  tai aj‍ja upaṭṭhitaṃ, mā bhāyi bhikkhū’’ti tāsaṃ vinaudaitvā hāsaṃ uppādaitvā tasmiṃ nimittai cittaṃ sañ‍carāpaitabbaṃ| aivaṃ visaisamadhigacchati| idamaitaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ ‘‘samantā nimittupalakkhaṇā asammauhatthā’’ti|

aikādasavidhaina pana nimittaggāhaṃ sampādaintau kammaṭṭhānaṃ upanibandhati| tassa hi cakkhūni ummīlaitvā aulaukanapac‍cayā uggahanimittaṃ uppaj‍jati| tasmiṃ mānasaṃ cāraintassa paṭibhāganimittaṃ uppaj‍jati| tattha mānasaṃ cāraintau appanaṃ pāpuṇāti| appanāyaṃ ṭhatvā vipassanaṃ vaḍḍhaintau arahattaṃ sacchikarauti| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘aikādasavidhaina nimittaggāhau upanibandhanatthau’’ti|

115. gatāgatamaggapac‍cavaikkhaṇā vīthisampaṭipādanatthāti aittha pana yā gatamaggassa ca āgatamaggassa ca pac‍cavaikkhaṇā vuttā, sā kammaṭṭhānavīthiyā sampaṭipādanatthāti atthau| sacai hi imaṃ bhikkhuṃ kammaṭṭhānaṃ gahaitvā āgacchantaṃ antarāmaggai kaici aj‍ja, bhantai, katimīti divasaṃ vā pucchanti, pañhaṃ vā pucchanti, paṭisanthāraṃ vā karaunti, ahaṃ kammaṭṭhānikauti tuṇhībhūtaina gantuṃ na vaṭṭati| divasau kathaitabbau, pañhau vissaj‍jaitabbau| sacai na jānāti, na jānāmīti vattabbaṃ| dhammikau paṭisanthārau kātabbau| tassaivaṃ karauntassa uggahitaṃ taruṇanimittaṃ nassati| tasmiṃ nassantaipi divasaṃ puṭṭhaina kathaitabbamaiva| pañhaṃ ajānantaina na jānāmīti vattabbaṃ| jānantaina aikadaisaina kathaitumpi vaṭṭati, paṭisanthāraupi kātabbau| āgantukaṃ  pana bhikkhuṃ disvā āgantukapaṭisanthārau kātabbauva| avasaisānipi caitiyaṅgaṇavattabaudhiyaṅgaṇavattaupausathāgāravattabhaujanasālājantāgharaācariyupajjhāyaāgantukagamikavattādīni sabbāni khandhakavattāni pūraitabbānaiva| tassa tāni pūraintassāpi taṃ taruṇanimittaṃ nassati, puna gantvā nimittaṃ gaṇhissāmīti gantukāmassāpi amanussaihi vā vāḷamigaihi vā adhiṭṭhitattā susānampi gantuṃ na sak‍kā hauti, nimittaṃ vā antaradhāyati| uddhumātakaṃ hi aikamaiva vā dvai vā divasai ṭhatvā vinīlakādibhāvaṃ gacchati| sabbakammaṭṭhānaisu aitaina samaṃ dul‍labhaṃ kammaṭṭhānaṃ nāma natthi| tasmā aivaṃ naṭṭhai nimittai taina bhikkhunā rattiṭṭhānai vā divāṭhānai vā nisīditvā ahaṃ iminā nāma dvāraina vihārā nikkhamitvā asukadisābhimukhaṃ maggaṃ paṭipaj‍jitvā asukasmiṃ  nāma ṭhānai vāmaṃ gaṇhi, asukasmiṃ dakkhiṇaṃ| tassa asukasmiṃ ṭhānai pāsāṇau, asukasmiṃ vammikarukkhagacchalatānamañ‍ñataraṃ| sauhaṃ taina maggaina gantvā asukasmiṃ nāma ṭhānai asubhaṃ addasaṃ| tattha asukadisābhimukhau ṭhatvā aivañ‍caivañ‍ca samantā nimittāni sal‍lakkhaitvā aivaṃ asubhanimittaṃ uggahaitvā asukadisāya susānatau nikkhamitvā aivarūpaina nāma maggaina idañ‍cidañ‍ca karauntau āgantvā idha nisin‍nauti aivaṃ yāva pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā nisin‍naṭṭhānaṃ, tāva gatāgatamaggau pac‍cavaikkhitabbau| tassaivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhatau taṃ nimittaṃ pākaṭaṃ hauti, puratau nikkhittaṃ viya upaṭṭhāti| kammaṭṭhānaṃ purimākārainaiva vīthiṃ paṭipaj‍jati| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘gatāgatamaggapac‍cavaikkhaṇā vīthisampaṭipādanatthā’’ti|

116. idāni ānisaṃsadassāvī ratanasañ‍ñī hutvā cittīkāraṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā sampiyāyamānau tasmiṃ ārammaṇai cittaṃ upanibandhatīti aittha uddhumātakapaṭik‍kūlai mānasaṃ cāraitvā jhānaṃ nibbattaitvā jhānapadaṭṭhānaṃ vipassanaṃ vaḍḍhaintau ‘‘addhā imāya paṭipadāya jarāmaraṇamhā parimuc‍cissāmī’’ti aivaṃ ānisaṃsadassāvinā bhavitabbaṃ|

yathā pana duggatau purisau mahagghaṃ maṇiratanaṃ labhitvā dul‍labhaṃ vata mai laddhanti tasmiṃ ratanasañ‍ñī hutvā gāravaṃ janaitvā vipulaina paimaina sampiyāyamānau taṃ rakkhaiyya, aivamaiva ‘‘dul‍labhaṃ mai idaṃ kammaṭṭhānaṃ laddhaṃ duggatassa mahagghamaṇiratanasadisaṃ| catudhātukammaṭṭhānikau hi attanau cattārau mahābhūtai pariggaṇhāti, ānāpānakammaṭṭhānikau attanau nāsikavātaṃ pariggaṇhāti, kasiṇakammaṭṭhānikau kasiṇaṃ katvā yathāsukhaṃ bhāvaiti, aivaṃ itarāni kammaṭṭhānāni  sulabhāni| ‘idaṃ pana aikamaiva vā dvai vā divasai tiṭṭhati, tatau paraṃ vinīlakādibhāvaṃ pāpuṇātī’ti natthi itau dul‍labhatara’’nti tasmiṃ ratanasañ‍ñinā hutvā cittīkāraṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā sampiyāyamānaina taṃ nimittaṃ rakkhitabbaṃ| rattiṭṭhānai ca divāṭhānai ca ‘‘uddhumātakapaṭik‍kūlaṃ uddhumātakapaṭik‍kūla’’nti tattha punappunaṃ cittaṃ upanibandhitabbaṃ| punappunaṃ taṃ nimittaṃ āvaj‍jitabbaṃ, manasikātabbaṃ| tak‍kāhataṃ vitak‍kāhataṃ kātabbaṃ|

117. tassaivaṃ karautau paṭibhāganimittaṃ uppaj‍jati| tatridaṃ nimittadvayassa nānākaraṇaṃ, uggahanimittaṃ virūpaṃ bībhacchaṃ bhairavadassanaṃ hutvā upaṭṭhāti| paṭibhāganimittaṃ pana yāvadatthaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā nipan‍nau thūlaṅgapac‍caṅgapurisau viya| tassa paṭibhāganimittapaṭilābhasamakālamaiva bahiddhā kāmānaṃ amanasikārā vikkhambhanavasaina kāmacchandau pahīyati| anunayappahānainaiva cassa lauhitappahānaina pubbau viya byāpādaupi pahīyati| tathā āraddhavīriyatāya thinamiddhaṃ, avippaṭisārakarasantadhammānuyaugavasaina uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ, adhigatavisaisassa pac‍cakkhatāya paṭipattidaisakai satthari paṭipattiyaṃ paṭipattiphalai ca vicikicchā pahīyatīti pañ‍ca nīvaraṇāni pahīyanti| tasmiñ‍ñaiva ca nimittai caitasau abhiniraupanalakkhaṇau vitak‍kau, nimittānumaj‍janakic‍caṃ sādhayamānau vicārau, paṭiladdhavisaisādhigamapac‍cayā pīti, pītimanassa passaddhisambhavatau passaddhi, tan‍nimittaṃ sukhaṃ, sukhitassa cittasamādhisambhavatau sukhanimittā aikaggatā cāti jhānaṅgāni pātubhavanti| aivamassa paṭhamajjhānapaṭibimbabhūtaṃ upacārajjhānampi taṅkhaṇañ‍ñaiva nibbattati| itau paraṃ yāva paṭhamajjhānassa appanā caiva vasippatti ca, tāva sabbaṃ pathavīkasiṇai vuttanayainaiva vaiditabbaṃ|

vinīlakādikammaṭṭhānāni

118. itau paraisu pana vinīlakādīsupi yaṃ taṃ ‘‘uddhumātakaṃ asubhanimittaṃ uggaṇhantau aikau adutiyau gacchati upaṭṭhitāya satiyā’’tiādinā nayaina gamanaṃ ādiṃ katvā lakkhaṇaṃ vuttaṃ, taṃ sabbaṃ ‘‘vinīlakaṃ asubhanimittaṃ uggaṇhantau, vipubbakaṃ asubhanimittaṃ uggaṇhantau’’ti aivaṃ tassa tassa vasaina tattha tattha uddhumātakapadamattaṃ parivattaitvā vuttanayainaiva savinicchayādhippāyaṃ vaiditabbaṃ|

ayaṃ  pana visaisau – vinīlakai ‘‘vinīlakapaṭik‍kūlaṃ vinīlakapaṭik‍kūla’’nti manasikārau pavattaitabbau| uggahanimittañ‍caittha kabarakabaravaṇṇaṃ hutvā upaṭṭhāti| paṭibhāganimittaṃ pana ussadavasaina upaṭṭhāti|

vipubbakai ‘‘vipubbakapaṭik‍kūlaṃ vipubbakapaṭik‍kūla’’nti manasikārau pavattaitabbau| uggahanimittaṃ panaittha paggharantamiva upaṭṭhāti| paṭibhāganimittaṃ nic‍calaṃ san‍nisin‍naṃ hutvā upaṭṭhāti|

vicchiddakaṃ yuddhamaṇḍalai vā caurāṭaviyaṃ vā susānai vā yattha rājānau  caurai chindāpainti| arañ‍ñai vā pana sīhabyagghaihi chin‍napurisaṭṭhānai labbhati| tasmā tathārūpaṃ ṭhānaṃ gantvā sacai nānādisāyaṃ patitampi aikāvaj‍janaina āpāthamāgacchati ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ| nau cai āgacchati, sayaṃ hatthaina na parāmasitabbaṃ| parāmasantau hi vissāsaṃ āpaj‍jati| tasmā ārāmikaina vā samaṇuddaisaina vā añ‍ñaina vā kainaci aikaṭṭhānai kāraitabbaṃ| alabhantaina kattarayaṭṭhiyā vā daṇḍakaina vā aikaṅgulantaraṃ katvā upanāmaitabbaṃ| aivaṃ upanāmaitvā ‘‘vicchiddakapaṭik‍kūlaṃ vicchiddakapaṭik‍kūla’’nti manasikārau pavattaitabbau| tattha uggahanimittaṃ majjhai chiddaṃ viya upaṭṭhāti| paṭibhāganimittaṃ pana paripuṇṇaṃ hutvā upaṭṭhāti|

vikkhāyitakai vikkhāyitakapaṭik‍kūlaṃ vikkhāyitakapaṭik‍kūlanti manasikārau pavattaitabbau| uggahanimittaṃ panaittha tahiṃ tahiṃ khāyitasadisamaiva upaṭṭhāti| paṭibhāganimittaṃ paripuṇṇaṃva hutvā upaṭṭhāti|

vikkhittakampi vicchiddakai vuttanayainaiva aṅgulaṅgulantaraṃ kāraitvā vā katvā vā ‘‘vikkhittakapaṭik‍kūlaṃ vikkhittakapaṭik‍kūla’’nti manasikārau pavattaitabbau| aittha uggahanimittaṃ pākaṭantaraṃ hutvā upaṭṭhāti| paṭibhāganimittaṃ pana paripuṇṇaṃva hutvā upaṭṭhāti|

hatavikkhittakampi vicchiddakai vuttappakāraisuyaiva ṭhānaisu labbhati| tasmā tattha gantvā vuttanayainaiva aṅgulaṅgulantaraṃ kāraitvā vā katvā vā ‘‘hatavikkhittakapaṭik‍kūlaṃ hatavikkhittakapaṭik‍kūla’’nti manasikārau pavattaitabbau| uggahanimittaṃ panaittha pañ‍ñāyamānaṃ pahāramukhaṃ viya hauti| paṭibhāganimittaṃ paripuṇṇamaiva hutvā upaṭṭhāti|

lauhitakaṃ  yuddhamaṇḍalādīsu laddhappahārānaṃ hatthapādādīsu vā chin‍naisu bhin‍nagaṇḍapīḷakādīnaṃ vā mukhatau paggharamānakālai labbhati| tasmā taṃ disvā ‘‘lauhitakapaṭik‍kūlaṃ lauhitakapaṭik‍kūla’’nti manasikārau pavattaitabbau| aittha uggahanimittaṃ vātappahatā viya rattapaṭākā calamānākāraṃ upaṭṭhāti| paṭibhāganimittaṃ pana san‍nisin‍naṃ hutvā upaṭṭhāti|

puḷavakaṃ dvīhatīhac‍cayaina kuṇapassa navahi vaṇamukhaihi kimirāsipaggharaṇakālai hauti| apica taṃ sauṇasiṅgālamanussagaumahiṃsahatthiassaajagarādīnaṃ sarīrappamāṇamaiva hutvā sālibhattarāsi viya tiṭṭhati| taisu yattha katthaci ‘‘puḷavakapaṭik‍kūlaṃ puḷavakapaṭik‍kūla’’nti manasikārau pavattaitabbau| cūḷapiṇḍapātikatissatthairassa hi kāḷadīghavāpiyā antau hatthikuṇapai nimittaṃ upaṭṭhāsi| uggahanimittaṃ panaittha calamānaṃ viya upaṭṭhāti| paṭibhāganimittaṃ sālibhattapiṇḍau viya san‍nisin‍naṃ hutvā upaṭṭhāti|

aṭṭhikaṃ ‘‘saiyyathāpi passaiyya sarīraṃ sivathikāya chaḍḍitaṃ aṭṭhisaṅkhalikaṃ samaṃsalauhitaṃ nahārusambandha’’ntiādinā (ma॰ ni॰ 3.154) nayaina nānappakāratau vuttaṃ| tattha yattha taṃ nikkhittaṃ hauti, tattha purimanayainaiva gantvā samantā pāsāṇādīnaṃ vasaina sanimittaṃ sārammaṇaṃ katvā idaṃ aṭṭhikanti sabhāvabhāvatau upalakkhaitvā vaṇṇādivasaina aikādasahākāraihi nimittaṃ uggahaitabbaṃ|

119. taṃ pana vaṇṇatau saitanti aulaukaintassa na upaṭṭhāti, audātakasiṇasambhaidau hauti| tasmā aṭṭhikanti paṭik‍kūlavasainaiva aulaukaitabbaṃ| liṅganti idha hatthādīnaṃ nāmaṃ| tasmā hatthapādasīsaurabāhukaṭiūrujaṅghānaṃ vasaina liṅgatau vavatthapaitabbaṃ| dīgharassavaṭṭacaturassakhuddakamahantavasaina pana saṇṭhānatau vavatthapaitabbaṃ| disaukāsā vuttanayā aiva| tassa tassa aṭṭhinau pariyantavasaina paricchaidatau vavatthapaitvā yadaivaittha pākaṭaṃ hutvā upaṭṭhāti, taṃ gahaitvā appanā pāpuṇitabbā| tassa tassa aṭṭhinau nin‍naṭṭhānathalaṭṭhānavasaina pana nin‍natau ca thalatau ca vavatthapaitabbaṃ| padaisavasaināpi ahaṃ nin‍nai ṭhitau, aṭṭhi thalai, ahaṃ thalai, aṭṭhi nin‍naitipi vavatthapaitabbaṃ| dvin‍naṃ pana aṭṭhikānaṃ ghaṭitaghaṭitaṭṭhānavasaina sandhitau vavatthapaitabbaṃ| aṭṭhikānaṃyaiva antaravasaina vivaratau vavatthapaitabbaṃ| sabbatthaiva pana ñāṇaṃ cāraitvā imasmiṃ ṭhānai idamaṭṭhīti samantatau vavatthapaitabbaṃ| aivampi nimittai anupaṭṭhahantai nalāṭaṭṭhimhi cittaṃ saṇṭhapaitabbaṃ|

120. yathā  caittha, aivaṃ idaṃ aikādasavidhaina nimittaggahaṇaṃ itau purimaisu puḷavakādīsupi yuj‍jamānavasaina sal‍lakkhaitabbaṃ| idañ‍ca pana kammaṭṭhānaṃ sakalāyapi aṭṭhikasaṅkhalikāya aikasmimpi aṭṭhikai sampaj‍jati| tasmā taisu yatthakatthaci aikādasavidhaina nimittaṃ uggahaitvā ‘‘aṭṭhikapaṭik‍kūlaṃ aṭṭhikapaṭik‍kūla’’nti  manasikārau pavattaitabbau| idha uggahanimittampi paṭibhāganimittampi aikasadisamaiva hautīti vuttaṃ, taṃ aikasmiṃ aṭṭhikai yuttaṃ| aṭṭhikasaṅkhalikāya pana uggahanimittai pañ‍ñāyamānai vivaratā| paṭibhāganimittai paripuṇṇabhāvau yuj‍jati| aikaṭṭhikaipi ca uggahanimittaina bībhacchaina bhayānakaina bhavitabbaṃ| paṭibhāganimittaina pītisaumanassajanakaina, upacārāvahattā|

imasmiṃ hi aukāsai yaṃ aṭṭhakathāsu vuttaṃ, taṃ dvāraṃ datvāva vuttaṃ| tathā hi tattha ‘‘catūsu brahmavihāraisu dasasu ca asubhaisu paṭibhāganimittaṃ natthi| brahmavihāraisu hi sīmasambhaidauyaiva nimittaṃ| dasasu ca asubhaisu nibbikappaṃ katvā paṭik‍kūlabhāvaiyaiva diṭṭhai nimittaṃ nāma hautī’’ti vatvāpi puna anantaramaiva ‘‘duvidhaṃ idha nimittaṃ uggahanimittaṃ paṭibhāganimittaṃ| uggahanimittaṃ virūpaṃ bībhacchaṃ bhayānakaṃ hutvā upaṭṭhātī’’tiādi vuttaṃ| tasmā yaṃ vicāraitvā avaucumha, idamaivaittha yuttaṃ|

apica mahātissatthairassa dantaṭṭhikamattāvalaukanaina sakalitthisarīrassa aṭṭhisaṅghātabhāvaina upaṭṭhānādīni caittha nidassanānīti|

iti asubhāni subhaguṇau, dasasatalaucanaina thutakitti|

yāni avauca dasabalau, aikaikajjhānahaitunīti||

aivaṃ tāni ca taisañ‍ca, bhāvanānayamimaṃ viditvāna|

taisvaiva ayaṃ bhiyyau, pakiṇṇakakathāpi viñ‍ñaiyyā||

pakiṇṇakakathā

121. aitaisu hi yattha katthaci adhigatajjhānau suvikkhambhitarāgattā vītarāgau viya nil‍lauluppacārau  hauti| aivaṃ santaipi yvāyaṃ asubhappabhaidau vuttau, sau sarīrasabhāvappattivasaina ca rāgacaritabhaidavasaina cāti vaiditabbau| chavasarīraṃ hi paṭik‍kūlabhāvaṃ āpaj‍jamānaṃ uddhumātakasabhāvappattaṃ vā siyā, vinīlakādīnaṃ vā añ‍ñatarasabhāvappattaṃ| iti yādisaṃ yādisaṃ sak‍kā hauti laddhuṃ, tādisai tādisai uddhumātakapaṭik‍kūlaṃ vinīlakapaṭik‍kūlanti aivaṃ nimittaṃ gaṇhitabbamaivāti sarīrasabhāvappattivasaina dasadhā asubhappabhaidau vuttauti vaiditabbau|

visaisatau  caittha uddhumātakaṃ sarīrasaṇṭhānavipattippakāsanatau saṇṭhānarāginau sappāyaṃ| vinīlakaṃ chavirāgavipattippakāsanatau sarīravaṇṇarāginau sappāyaṃ| vipubbakaṃ kāyavaṇapaṭibaddhassa duggandhabhāvassa pakāsanatau mālāgandhādivasaina samuṭṭhāpitasarīragandharāginau sappāyaṃ| vicchiddakaṃ antaususirabhāvappakāsanatau sarīrai ghanabhāvarāginau sappāyaṃ| vikkhāyitakaṃ maṃsupacayasampattivināsappakāsanatau thanādīsu sarīrappadaisaisu maṃsupacayarāginau sappāyaṃ| vikkhittakaṃ aṅgapac‍caṅgānaṃ vikkhaipappakāsanatau aṅgapac‍caṅgalīlārāginau sappāyaṃ| hatavikkhittakaṃ sarīrasaṅghātabhaidavikārappakāsanatau sarīrasaṅghātasampattirāginau sappāyaṃ| lauhitakaṃ lauhitamakkhitapaṭik‍kūlabhāvappakāsanatau alaṅkārajanitasaubharāginau sappāyaṃ| puḷavakaṃ kāyassa anaikakimikulasādhāraṇabhāvappakāsanatau kāyai mamattarāginau sappāyaṃ| aṭṭhikaṃ sarīraṭṭhīnaṃ paṭik‍kūlabhāvappakāsanatau dantasampattirāginau sappāyanti aivaṃ rāgacaritabhaidavasaināpi dasadhā asubhappabhaidau vuttauti vaiditabbau|

yasmā pana dasavidhaipi aitasmiṃ asubhai saiyyathāpi nāma aparisaṇṭhitajalāya sīghasautāya nadiyā arittabalainaiva nāvā tiṭṭhati, vinā arittaina na sak‍kā ṭhapaituṃ, aivamaiva dubbalattā ārammaṇassa vitak‍kabalainaiva cittaṃ aikaggaṃ hutvā tiṭṭhati, vinā vitak‍kaina na sak‍kā ṭhapaituṃ, tasmā paṭhamajjhānamaivaittha hauti, na dutiyādīni|

paṭik‍kūlaipi ca aitasmiṃ ārammaṇai ‘‘addhā imāya paṭipadāya jarāmaraṇamhā parimuc‍cissāmī’’ti aivamānisaṃsadassāvitāya caiva nīvaraṇasantāpappahānaina ca pītisaumanassaṃ uppaj‍jati, ‘‘bahuṃ dāni vaitanaṃ labhissāmī’’ti ānisaṃsadassāvinau pupphachaḍḍakassa gūtharāsimhi viya, ussan‍nabyādhidukkhassa rauginau vamanaviraicanappavattiyaṃ viya ca|

122. dasavidhampi  caitaṃ asubhaṃ lakkhaṇatau aikamaiva hauti| dasavidhassāpi haitassa asuciduggandhajaigucchapaṭik‍kūlabhāvau aiva lakkhaṇaṃ| tadaitaṃ iminā lakkhaṇaina na kaivalaṃ matasarīrai, dantaṭṭhikadassāvinau pana caitiyapabbatavāsinau mahātissatthairassa viya, hatthikkhandhagataṃ rājānaṃ aulaukaintassa saṅgharakkhitatthairūpaṭṭhākasāmaṇairassa viya ca jīvamānakasarīraipi  upaṭṭhāti| yathaiva hi matasarīraṃ, aivaṃ jīvamānakampi asubhamaiva| asubhalakkhaṇaṃ panaittha āgantukaina alaṅkāraina paṭicchan‍nattā na pañ‍ñāyati| pakatiyā pana idaṃ sarīraṃ nāma atiraikatisataaṭṭhikasamussayaṃ asītisatasandhisaṅghaṭitaṃ navanhārusatanibandhanaṃ navamaṃsapaisisatānulittaṃ al‍lacammapariyaunaddhaṃ chaviyā paṭicchan‍naṃ chiddāvachiddaṃ maidakathālikā viya nic‍cuggharitapaggharitaṃ kimisaṅghanisaivitaṃ raugānaṃ āyatanaṃ dukkhadhammānaṃ vatthu paribhin‍napurāṇagaṇḍau viya navahi vaṇamukhaihi satatavissandanaṃ| yassa ubhauhi akkhīhi akkhigūthakau paggharati, kaṇṇabilaihi kaṇṇagūthakau, nāsāpuṭaihi siṅghāṇikā, mukhatau āhārapittasaimharudhirāni, adhaudvāraihi uc‍cārapassāvā, navanavutiyā laumakūpasahassaihi asucisaidayūsau paggharati| nīlamakkhikādayau samparivārainti| yaṃ dantakaṭṭhamukhadhauvanasīsamakkhananahānanivāsanapārupanādīhi appaṭijaggitvā yathājātauva pharusavippakiṇṇakaisau hutvā gāmaina gāmaṃ vicarantau rājāpi pupphachaḍḍakacaṇḍālādīsu añ‍ñataraupi samasarīrapaṭik‍kūlatāya nibbisaisau hauti, aivaṃ asuciduggandhajaigucchapaṭik‍kūlatāya rañ‍ñau vā caṇḍālassa vā sarīrai vaimattaṃ nāma natthi| dantakaṭṭhamukhadhauvanādīhi panaittha dantamalādīni pamaj‍jitvā nānāvatthaihi hirikaupīnaṃ paṭicchādaitvā nānāvaṇṇaina surabhivilaipanaina vilimpitvā pupphābharaṇādīhi alaṅkaritvā ‘‘ahaṃ mama’’nti gahaitabbākārappattaṃ karaunti| tatau iminā āgantukaina alaṅkāraina paṭicchan‍nattā tadassa yāthāvasarasaṃ asubhalakkhaṇaṃ asañ‍jānantā purisā itthīsu, itthiyau ca purisaisu ratiṃ karaunti| paramatthatau panaittha raj‍jitabbakayuttaṭṭhānaṃ nāma aṇumattampi natthi| tathā hi kaisalaumanakhadantakhaiḷasiṅghāṇikauc‍cārapassāvādīsu aikakauṭṭhāsampi sarīratau bahi patitaṃ sattā hatthaina chupitumpi na icchanti, aṭṭīyanti harāyanti jigucchanti| yaṃ yaṃ panaittha avasaisaṃ hauti, taṃ taṃ aivaṃ paṭik‍kūlampi samānaṃ avij‍jandhakārapariyaunaddhā attasinaiharāgarattā ‘‘iṭṭhaṃ kantaṃ nic‍caṃ sukhaṃ attā’’ti gaṇhanti| tai aivaṃ gaṇhantā aṭaviyaṃ kiṃsukarukkhaṃ disvā rukkhatau apatitapupphaṃ ‘‘ayaṃ maṃsapaisī’’ti vihañ‍ñamānaina jarasiṅgālaina samānataṃ āpaj‍janti| tasmā –

yathāpi  pupphitaṃ disvā, siṅgālau kiṃsukaṃ vanai|

maṃsarukkhau mayā laddhau, iti gantvāna vaigasā||

patitaṃ patitaṃ pupphaṃ, ḍaṃsitvā atilaulupau|

nayidaṃ maṃsaṃ aduṃ maṃsaṃ, yaṃ rukkhasminti gaṇhati||

kauṭṭhāsaṃ patitaṃyaiva, asubhanti tathā budhau|

aggahaitvāna gaṇhaiyya, sarīraṭṭhampi naṃ tathā||

imañhi subhatau kāyaṃ, gahaitvā tattha mucchitā|

bālā karauntā pāpāni, dukkhā na parimuc‍carai||

tasmā passaiyya maidhāvī, jīvatau vā matassa vā|

sabhāvaṃ pūtikāyassa, subhabhāvaina vaj‍jitaṃ||

vuttañhaitaṃ –

duggandhau asuci kāyau, kuṇapau uk‍karūpamau|

ninditau cakkhubhūtaihi, kāyau bālābhinanditau||

al‍lacammapaṭicchan‍nau, navadvārau mahāvaṇau|

samantatau paggharati, asuci pūtigandhiyau||

sacai imassa kāyassa, antau bāhirakau siyā|

daṇḍaṃ nūna gahaitvāna, kākai sauṇai nivārayaiti||

tasmā dabbajātikaina bhikkhunā jīvamānasarīraṃ vā hautu

matasarīraṃ  vā yattha yattha asubhākārau pañ‍ñāyati, tattha tatthaiva nimittaṃ gahaitvā kammaṭṭhānaṃ appanaṃ pāpaitabbanti|

iti sādhujanapāmauj‍jatthāya katai visuddhimaggai

samādhibhāvanādhikārai

asubhakammaṭṭhānaniddaisau nāma

chaṭṭhau paricchaidau|

7. chaanussatiniddaisau

1. buddhānussatikathā

123. asubhānantaraṃ  uddiṭṭhāsu pana dasasu anussatīsu punappunaṃ uppaj‍janatau satiyaiva anussati, pavattitabbaṭṭhānamhiyaiva vā pavattattā saddhāpabbajitassa kulaputtassa anurūpā satītipi anussati, buddhaṃ ārabbha uppan‍nā anussati buddhānussati, buddhaguṇārammaṇāya satiyā aitamadhivacanaṃ| dhammaṃ ārabbha uppan‍nā anussati dhammānussati, svākkhātatādidhammaguṇārammaṇāya satiyā aitamadhivacanaṃ| saṅghaṃ ārabbha uppan‍nā anussati saṅghānussati, suppaṭipan‍natādisaṅghaguṇārammaṇāya satiyā aitamadhivacanaṃ| sīlaṃ ārabbha uppan‍nā anussati sīlānussati, akhaṇḍatādisīlaguṇārammaṇāya satiyā aitamadhivacanaṃ| cāgaṃ ārabbha uppan‍nā anussati cāgānussati, muttacāgatādicāgaguṇārammaṇāya satiyā aitamadhivacanaṃ| daivatā ārabbha uppan‍nā anussati daivatānussati, daivatā sakkhiṭṭhānai ṭhapaitvā attanau saddhādiguṇārammaṇāya satiyā aitamadhivacanaṃ| maraṇaṃ ārabbha uppan‍nā anussati maraṇānussati, jīvitindriyupacchaidārammaṇāya satiyā aitamadhivacanaṃ| kaisādibhaidaṃ rūpakāyaṃ gatā, kāyai vā gatāti kāyagatā, kāyagatā ca sā sati cāti kāyagatasatīti vattabbai rassaṃ akatvā kāyagatāsatīti vuttā, kaisādikāyakauṭṭhāsanimittārammaṇāya satiyā aitamadhivacanaṃ| ānāpānai ārabbha uppan‍nā sati ānāpānassati, assāsapassāsanimittārammaṇāya satiyā aitamadhivacanaṃ| upasamaṃ ārabbha uppan‍nā anussati upasamānussati, sabbadukkhūpasamārammaṇāya satiyā aitamadhivacanaṃ|

124. iti imāsu dasasu anussatīsu buddhānussatiṃ tāva bhāvaitukāmaina avaic‍cappasādasaman‍nāgataina yauginā patirūpasaināsanai rahaugataina paṭisal‍līnaina ‘‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi  satthā  daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 6.10) aivaṃ buddhassa bhagavatau guṇā anussaritabbā|

tatrāyaṃ anussaraṇanayau – sau bhagavā itipi arahaṃ, itipi sammāsambuddhau…pai॰… itipi bhagavāti anussarati| iminā ca iminā ca kāraṇaināti vuttaṃ hauti|

125. tattha ārakattā arīnaṃ arānañ‍ca hatattā pac‍cayādīnaṃ arahattā pāpakaraṇai rahābhāvāti imaihi tāva kāraṇaihi sau bhagavā arahanti anussarati| ārakā hi sau sabbakilaisaihi suvidūravidūrai ṭhitau maggaina savāsanānaṃ kilaisānaṃ viddhaṃsitattāti ārakattā arahaṃ|

sau tatau ārakā nāma, yassa yaināsamaṅgitā|

asamaṅgī ca dausaihi, nāthau tainārahaṃ matauti||

126. tai cānaina kilaisārayau maggaina hatāti arīnaṃ hatattāpi arahaṃ|

yasmā rāgādisaṅkhātā, sabbaipi arayau hatā|

pañ‍ñāsatthaina nāthaina, tasmāpi arahaṃ matauti||

127. yañ‍caitaṃ avij‍jābhavataṇhāmayanābhi puñ‍ñādiabhisaṅkhārāraṃ jarāmaraṇanaimi āsavasamudayamayaina akkhaina vijjhitvā tibhavarathai samāyaujitaṃ anādikālappavattaṃ saṃsāracak‍kaṃ, tassānaina baudhimaṇḍai vīriyapādaihi sīlapathaviyaṃ patiṭṭhāya saddhāhatthaina kammakkhayakaraṃ ñāṇapharasuṃ gahaitvā sabbai arā hatāti arānaṃ hatattāpi arahaṃ|

128. atha vā saṃsāracak‍kanti anamataggaṃ saṃsāravaṭṭaṃ vuc‍cati| tassa ca avij‍jā nābhi, mūlattā| jarāmaraṇaṃ naimi, pariyausānattā| saisā dasa dhammā arā, avij‍jāmūlakattā jarāmaraṇapariyantattā ca, tattha dukkhādīsu añ‍ñāṇaṃ avij‍jā| kāmabhavai ca avij‍jā kāmabhavai saṅkhārānaṃ pac‍cayau hauti, rūpabhavai avij‍jā rūpabhavai saṅkhārānaṃ pac‍cayau hauti, arūpabhavai  avij‍jā  arūpabhavai saṅkhārānaṃ pac‍cayau hauti| kāmabhavai saṅkhārā kāmabhavai paṭisandhiviñ‍ñāṇassa pac‍cayā haunti, aisa nayau itaraisu| kāmabhavai paṭisandhiviñ‍ñāṇaṃ kāmabhavai nāmarūpassa pac‍cayau hauti, tathā rūpabhavai| arūpabhavai nāmassaiva pac‍cayau hauti| kāmabhavai nāmarūpaṃ kāmabhavai saḷāyatanassa pac‍cayau hauti, rūpabhavai nāmarūpaṃ rūpabhavai tiṇṇaṃ āyatanānaṃ pac‍cayau hauti, arūpabhavai nāmaṃ arūpabhavai aikassa āyatanassa pac‍cayau hauti| kāmabhavai saḷāyatanaṃ kāmabhavai chabbidhassa phassassa pac‍cayau hauti, rūpabhavai tīṇi āyatanāni rūpabhavai tiṇṇaṃ phassānaṃ pac‍cayā haunti, arūpabhavai aikaṃ āyatanaṃ arūpabhavai aikassa phassassa pac‍cayau hauti| kāmabhavai cha phassā kāmabhavai chan‍naṃ vaidanānaṃ pac‍cayā haunti, rūpabhavai tayau phassā tatthaiva tissan‍naṃ, arūpabhavai aikau tatthaiva aikissā vaidanāya pac‍cayau hauti| kāmabhavai cha vaidanā kāmabhavai chan‍naṃ taṇhākāyānaṃ pac‍cayā haunti, rūpabhavai tissau tatthaiva tiṇṇaṃ, arūpabhavai aikā vaidanā arūpabhavai aikassa taṇhākāyassa pac‍cayau hauti| tattha tattha sā sā taṇhā tassa tassa upādānassa, upādānādayau bhavādīnaṃ|

kathaṃ? idhaikac‍cau kāmai paribhuñ‍jissāmīti kāmupādānapac‍cayā kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ carati, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ carati, manasā duc‍caritaṃ carati, duc‍caritapāripūriyā apāyai upapaj‍jati| tatthassa upapattihaitubhūtaṃ kammaṃ kammabhavau, kammanibbattā khandhā upapattibhavau, khandhānaṃ nibbatti jāti, paripākau jarā, bhaidau maraṇaṃ|

aparau saggasampattiṃ anubhavissāmīti tathaiva sucaritaṃ carati, sucaritapāripūriyā saggai upapaj‍jati| tatthassa upapattihaitubhūtaṃ kammaṃ kammabhavauti sau aiva nayau|

aparau pana brahmalaukasampattiṃ anubhavissāmīti kāmupādānapac‍cayāaiva maittaṃ bhāvaiti, karuṇaṃ, muditaṃ, upaikkhaṃ bhāvaiti, bhāvanāpāripūriyā brahmalaukai nibbattati| tatthassa nibbattihaitubhūtaṃ kammaṃ kammabhavauti sau aiva nayau|

aparau  arūpabhavai sampattiṃ anubhavissāmīti tathaiva ākāsānañ‍cāyatanādisamāpattiyau bhāvaiti, bhāvanāpāripūriyā tattha tattha nibbattati| tatthassa nibbattihaitubhūtaṃ kammaṃ kammabhavau, kammanibbattā  khandhā upapattibhavau, khandhānaṃ nibbatti jāti, paripākau jarā, bhaidau maraṇanti| aisa nayau saisupādānamūlikāsupi yaujanāsu|

aivaṃ ayaṃ avij‍jā haitu, saṅkhārā haitusamuppan‍nā, ubhaupaitai haitusamuppan‍nāti pac‍cayapariggahai pañ‍ñā dhammaṭṭhitiñāṇaṃ| atītampi addhānaṃ anāgatampi addhānaṃ avij‍jā haitu, saṅkhārā haitusamuppan‍nā, ubhaupaitai haitusamuppan‍nāti pac‍cayapariggahai pañ‍ñā dhammaṭṭhitiñāṇanti aitainaiva nayaina sabbapadāni vitthāraitabbāni|

tattha avij‍jāsaṅkhārā aikau saṅkhaipau, viñ‍ñāṇanāmarūpasaḷāyatanaphassavaidanā aikau, taṇhupādānabhavā aikau, jātijarāmaraṇaṃ aikau| purimasaṅkhaipau caittha atītau addhā, dvai majjhimā pac‍cuppan‍nau, jātijarāmaraṇaṃ anāgatau| avij‍jāsaṅkhāraggahaṇaina caittha taṇhupādānabhavā gahitāva hauntīti imai pañ‍ca dhammā atītai kammavaṭṭaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇādayau pañ‍ca aitarahi vipākavaṭṭaṃ, taṇhupādānabhavaggahaṇaina avij‍jāsaṅkhārā gahitāva hauntīti imai pañ‍ca dhammā aitarahi kammavaṭṭaṃ, jātijarāmaraṇāpadaisaina viñ‍ñāṇādīnaṃ niddiṭṭhattā imai pañ‍ca dhammā āyatiṃ vipākavaṭṭaṃ| tai ākāratau vīsatividhā haunti| saṅkhāraviñ‍ñāṇānañ‍caittha antarā aikau sandhi, vaidanātaṇhānamantarā aikau, bhavajātīnamantarā aikauti, iti bhagavā aitaṃ catusaṅkhaipaṃ tiyaddhaṃ vīsatākāraṃ tisandhiṃ paṭic‍casamuppādaṃ sabbākāratau jānāti passati añ‍ñāti paṭivijjhati| taṃ ñātaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā, taina vuc‍cati pac‍cayapariggahai pañ‍ñā dhammaṭṭhitiñāṇanti| iminā dhammaṭṭhitiñāṇaina bhagavā tai dhammai yathābhūtaṃ ñatvā taisu nibbindantau viraj‍jantau vimuc‍cantau vuttappakārassa imassa saṃsāracak‍kassa arai hani vihani viddhaṃsaisi| aivampi arānaṃ hatattā arahaṃ|

arā saṃsāracak‍kassa, hatā ñāṇāsinā yatau|

laukanāthaina tainaisa, arahanti pavuc‍cati||

129. aggadakkhiṇaiyyattā  ca cīvarādipac‍cayai arahati pūjāvisaisañ‍ca| tainaiva ca uppan‍nai tathāgatai yaikaici mahaisakkhā daivamanussā, na tai añ‍ñattha pūjaṃ karaunti| tathā hi brahmā sahampati sinairumattaina ratanadāmaina tathāgataṃ pūjaisi| yathābalañ‍ca añ‍ñai daivā manussā ca  bimbisārakausalarājādayau| parinibbutampi ca bhagavantaṃ uddissa chan‍navutikauṭidhanaṃ vissaj‍jaitvā asaukamahārājā sakalajambudīpai caturāsītivihārasahassāni patiṭṭhāpaisi| kau pana vādau añ‍ñaisaṃ pūjāvisaisānanti pac‍cayādīnaṃ arahattāpi arahaṃ|

pūjāvisaisaṃ saha pac‍cayaihi,

yasmā ayaṃ arahati laukanāthau|

atthānurūpaṃ arahanti laukai,

tasmā jinau arahati nāmamaitaṃ||

130. yathā ca laukai yaikaici paṇḍitamāninau bālā asilaukabhayaina rahau pāpaṃ karaunti, aivamaisa na kadāci karautīti pāpakaraṇai rahābhāvataupi arahaṃ|

yasmā natthi rahau nāma, pāpakammaisu tādinau|

rahābhāvaina tainaisa, arahaṃ iti vissutau||

aivaṃ sabbathāpi –

ārakattā hatattā ca, kilaisārīna sau muni|

hatasaṃsāracak‍kārau, pac‍cayādīna cārahau|

na rahau karauti pāpāni, arahaṃ taina vuc‍catīti||

131. sammā sāmañ‍ca sabbadhammānaṃ buddhattā pana sammāsambuddhau| tathāhi aisa sabbadhammai sammā sāmañ‍ca buddhau, abhiñ‍ñaiyyai dhammai abhiñ‍ñaiyyatau buddhau, pariñ‍ñaiyyai dhammai pariñ‍ñaiyyatau, pahātabbai dhammai pahātabbatau, sacchikātabbai dhammai sacchikātabbatau, bhāvaitabbai dhammai bhāvaitabbatau| tainaiva cāha –

abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ abhiñ‍ñātaṃ, bhāvaitabbañ‍ca bhāvitaṃ|

pahātabbaṃ pahīnaṃ mai, tasmā buddhausmi brāhmaṇāti|| (ma॰ ni॰ 2.399; su॰ ni॰ 563)|

132. apica  cakkhuṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ, tassa mūlakāraṇabhāvaina samuṭṭhāpikā purimataṇhā samudayasac‍caṃ, ubhin‍naṃ appavatti niraudhasac‍caṃ, niraudhapajānanā paṭipadā maggasac‍canti aivaṃ aikaikapaduddhāraināpi sabbadhammai sammā sāmañ‍ca buddhau, aisa nayau sautaghānajivhākāyamanaisu| aitainaiva nayaina rūpādīni cha āyatanāni, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇādayau chaviñ‍ñāṇakāyā, cakkhusamphassādayau cha phassā, cakkhusamphassajādayau cha vaidanā, rūpasañ‍ñādayau cha sañ‍ñā, rūpasañ‍caitanādayau cha caitanā, rūpataṇhādayau cha taṇhākāyā, rūpavitak‍kādayau cha vitak‍kā, rūpavicārādayau cha vicārā, rūpakkhandhādayau pañ‍cakkhandhā, dasa kasiṇāni, dasa anussatiyau, uddhumātakasañ‍ñādivasaina dasa sañ‍ñā, kaisādayau dvattiṃsākārā, dvādasāyatanāni, aṭṭhārasa dhātuyau, kāmabhavādayau nava bhavā, paṭhamādīni cattāri jhānāni, maittābhāvanādayau catassau appamañ‍ñā, catassau arūpasamāpattiyau, paṭilaumatau jarāmaraṇādīni, anulaumatau avij‍jādīni paṭic‍casamuppādaṅgāni ca yaujaitabbāni|

tatrāyaṃ aikapadayaujanā, jarāmaraṇaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ, jāti samudayasac‍caṃ, ubhin‍nampi nissaraṇaṃ niraudhasac‍caṃ, niraudhapajānanā paṭipadā maggasac‍canti aivamaikaikapaduddhāraina sabbadhammai sammā sāmañ‍ca buddhau anubuddhau paṭibuddhau| taina vuttaṃ – ‘‘sammā sāmañ‍ca sabbadhammānaṃ buddhattā pana sammāsambuddhau’’ti|

133. vij‍jāhi pana caraṇaina ca sampan‍nattā vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau| tattha vij‍jāti tissaupi vij‍jā aṭṭhapi vij‍jā| tissau vij‍jā bhayabhairavasuttai (ma॰ ni॰ 1.52 ādayau) vuttanayainaiva vaiditabbā, aṭṭha ambaṭṭhasuttai (dī॰ ni॰ 1.278 ādayau)| tatra hi vipassanāñāṇaina manaumayiddhiyā ca saha cha abhiñ‍ñā pariggahaitvā aṭṭha vij‍jā vuttā| caraṇanti sīlasaṃvarau, indriyaisu guttadvāratā, bhaujanai mattañ‍ñutā, jāgariyānuyaugau, satta saddhammā, cattāri rūpāvacarajjhānānīti imai pan‍narasa dhammā vaiditabbā| imaiyaiva hi pan‍narasa dhammā yasmā aitaihi carati ariyasāvakau gacchati amataṃ disaṃ, tasmā caraṇanti vuttā| yathāha – ‘‘idha, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau sīlavā hautī’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 2.24) sabbaṃ majjhimapaṇṇāsakai vuttanayainaiva vaiditabbaṃ| bhagavā imāhi vij‍jāhi iminā ca caraṇaina saman‍nāgatau| taina vuc‍cati vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nauti|

tattha  vij‍jāsampadā bhagavatau sabbañ‍ñutaṃ pūraitvā ṭhitā| caraṇasampadā mahākāruṇikataṃ| sau sabbañ‍ñutāya sabbasattānaṃ atthānatthaṃ ñatvā mahākāruṇikatāya anatthaṃ parivaj‍jaitvā atthai niyaujaiti| yathā taṃ vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau| tainassa sāvakā suppaṭipan‍nā haunti, nau duppaṭipan‍nā vij‍jācaraṇavipan‍nānaṃ sāvakā attantapādayau viya|

134. saubhanagamanattā, sundaraṃ ṭhānaṃ gatattā, sammā gatattā, sammā ca gadattā sugatau| gamanampi hi gatanti vuc‍cati| tañ‍ca bhagavatau saubhanaṃ parisuddhamanavaj‍jaṃ| kiṃ pana tanti? ariyamaggau| taina haisa gamanaina khaimaṃ disaṃ asaj‍jamānau gatauti saubhanagamanattā sugatau| sundarañ‍caisa ṭhānaṃ gatau amataṃ nibbānanti sundaraṃ ṭhānaṃ gatattāpi sugatau| sammā ca gatau taina taina maggaina pahīnai kilaisai puna apac‍cāgacchantau| vuttañhaitaṃ – ‘‘sautāpattimaggaina yai kilaisā pahīnā, tai kilaisai na punaiti na pac‍caiti na pac‍cāgacchatīti sugatau…pai॰… arahattamaggaina yai kilaisā pahīnā, tai kilaisai na punaiti na pac‍caiti na pac‍cāgacchatīti sugatau’’ti, sammā vā gatau dīpaṅkarapādamūlatau pabhuti yāva baudhimaṇḍā tāva samatiṃsapāramīpūrikāya sammāpaṭipattiyā sabbalaukassa hitasukhamaiva karauntau sassataṃ, ucchaidaṃ, kāmasukhaṃ, attakilamathanti imai ca antai anupagacchantau gatauti sammā gatattāpi sugatau| sammā caisa gadati yuttaṭṭhānai yuttamaiva vācaṃ bhāsatīti sammā gadattāpi sugatau| tatridaṃ sādhakasuttaṃ ‘‘yaṃ tathāgatau vācaṃ jānāti abhūtaṃ atacchaṃ anatthasañhitaṃ, sā ca paraisaṃ appiyā amanāpā, na taṃ tathāgatau vācaṃ bhāsati| yampi tathāgatau vācaṃ jānāti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ anatthasañhitaṃ, sā ca paraisaṃ appiyā amanāpā, tampi tathāgatau vācaṃ na bhāsati| yañ‍ca khau tathāgatau vācaṃ jānāti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ atthasañhitaṃ, sā ca paraisaṃ appiyā amanāpā, tatra kālañ‍ñū tathāgatau hauti tassā vācāya vaiyyākaraṇāya| yaṃ tathāgatau vācaṃ jānāti abhūtaṃ atacchaṃ anatthasañhitaṃ, sā ca paraisaṃ piyā manāpā, na taṃ tathāgatau vācaṃ bhāsati| yampi tathāgatau vācaṃ jānāti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ anatthasañhitaṃ, sā ca paraisaṃ piyā manāpā, tampi tathāgatau vācaṃ na bhāsati| yañ‍ca khau tathāgatau vācaṃ jānāti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ atthasañhitaṃ, sā  ca paraisaṃ piyā manāpā, tatra kālañ‍ñū tathāgatau hauti tassā vācāya vaiyyākaraṇāyā’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 2.86)| aivaṃ sammā gadattāpi sugatauti vaiditabbau|

135. sabbathāpi viditalaukattā pana laukavidū| sau hi bhagavā sabhāvatau samudayatau niraudhatau  niraudhūpāyatauti sabbathā laukaṃ avaidi añ‍ñāsi paṭivijjhi| yathāha – ‘‘yattha khau, āvusau, na jāyati na jīyati na mīyati na cavati na upapaj‍jati, nāhaṃ taṃ gamanaina laukassa antaṃ ñātaiyyaṃ daṭṭhaiyyaṃ pattaiyyanti vadāmi, na cāhaṃ, āvusau, apatvāva laukassa antaṃ dukkhassa antakiriyaṃ vadāmi| api cāhaṃ, āvusau, imasmiñ‍ñaiva byāmamattai kaḷaivarai sasañ‍ñimhi samanakai laukañ‍ca pañ‍ñapaimi laukasamudayañ‍ca laukaniraudhañ‍ca laukaniraudhagāminiñ‍ca paṭipadaṃ|

gamanaina na pattabbau, laukassantau kudācanaṃ|

na ca apatvā laukantaṃ, dukkhā atthi pamaucanaṃ||

tasmā havai laukavidū sumaidhau,

laukantagū vūsitabrahmacariyau|

laukassa antaṃ samitāvi ñatvā,

nāsīsati laukamimaṃ parañ‍cāti|| (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.107; a॰ ni॰ 4.45)|

136. apica tayau laukā saṅkhāralaukau sattalaukau aukāsalaukauti| tattha aikau laukau sabbai sattā āhāraṭṭhitikāti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.112) āgataṭṭhānai saṅkhāralaukau vaiditabbau| sassatau laukauti vā asassatau laukauti vāti (dī॰ ni॰ 1.421) āgataṭṭhānai sattalaukau|

yāvatā candimasūriyā pariharanti, disā bhanti viraucamānā|

tāva sahassadhā laukau, aittha tai vattatī vasauti|| (ma॰ ni॰ 1.503) –

āgataṭṭhānai aukāsalaukau| tampi bhagavā sabbathā avaidi| tathā hissa ‘‘aikau laukau sabbai sattā āhāraṭṭhitikā| dvai laukā nāmañ‍ca rūpañ‍ca| tayau laukā tissau vaidanā| cattārau laukā  cattārau āhārā| pañ‍ca laukā pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā| cha laukā cha ajjhattikāni āyatanāni| satta laukā satta viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitiyau| aṭṭha laukā aṭṭha laukadhammā| nava laukā nava  sattāvāsā| dasa laukā dasāyatanāni| dvādasa laukā dvādasāyatanāni| aṭṭhārasa laukā aṭṭhārasa dhātuyau’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.112) ayaṃ saṅkhāralaukaupi sabbathā viditau|

yasmā panaisa sabbaisampi sattānaṃ āsayaṃ jānāti, anusayaṃ jānāti, caritaṃ jānāti, adhimuttiṃ jānāti, apparajakkhai mahārajakkhai, tikkhindriyai mudindriyai, svākārai dvākārai, suviñ‍ñāpayai duviñ‍ñāpayai, bhabbai abhabbai sattai jānāti| tasmāssa sattalaukaupi sabbathā viditau|

137. yathā ca sattalaukau, aivaṃ aukāsalaukaupi| tathā haisa aikaṃ cak‍kavāḷaṃ āyāmatau ca vitthāratau ca yaujanānaṃ dvādasasatasahassāni catutiṃsasatāni ca pañ‍ñāsañ‍ca yaujanāni| parikkhaipatau pana –

sabbaṃ satasahassāni, chattiṃsaparimaṇḍalaṃ|

dasa caiva sahassāni, aḍḍhuḍḍhāni satāni ca||

tattha –

duvai satasahassāni, cattāri nahutāni ca|

aittakaṃ bahalattaina, saṅkhātāyaṃ vasundharā||

tassāyaiva sandhārakaṃ –

cattāri satasahassāni, aṭṭhaiva nahutāni ca|

aittakaṃ bahalattaina, jalaṃ vātai patiṭṭhitaṃ||

tassāpi sandhārakau –

nava satasahassāni, mālutau nabhamuggatau|

saṭṭhiñ‍caiva sahassāni, aisā laukassa saṇṭhiti||

aivaṃ saṇṭhitai caittha yaujanānaṃ –

caturāsīti  sahassāni, ajjhaugāḷhau mahaṇṇavai|

ac‍cuggatau tāvadaiva, sinairu pabbatuttamau||

tatau upaḍḍhupaḍḍhaina, pamāṇaina yathāk‍kamaṃ|

ajjhaugāḷhuggatā dibbā, nānāratanacittitā||

yugandharau īsadharau, karavīkau sudassanau|

naimindharau vinatakau, assakaṇṇau giri brahā||

aitai satta mahāsailā, sinairussa samantatau|

mahārājānamāvāsā, daivayakkhanisaivitā||

yaujanānaṃ satānuc‍cau, himavā pañ‍ca pabbatau|

yaujanānaṃ sahassāni, tīṇi āyatavitthatau||

caturāsītisahassaihi, kūṭaihi paṭimaṇḍitau|

tipañ‍cayaujanakkhandha-parikkhaipā nagavhayā||

pañ‍ñāsayaujanakkhandha-sākhāyāmā samantatau|

satayaujanavitthiṇṇā, tāvadaiva ca uggatā|

jambū yassānubhāvaina, jambudīpau pakāsitau||

yañ‍caitaṃ jambuyā pamāṇaṃ, aitadaiva asurānaṃ citrapāṭaliyā, garuḷānaṃ simbalirukkhassa, aparagauyānai kadambassa, uttarakurūsu kapparukkhassa, pubbavidaihai sirīsassa, tāvatiṃsaisu pāricchattakassāti| taināhu paurāṇā –

‘‘pāṭalī simbalī jambū, daivānaṃ pāricchattakau|

kadambau kapparukkhau ca, sirīsaina bhavati sattamanti||

‘‘dvaiasīti  sahassāni, ajjhaugāḷhau mahaṇṇavai|

ac‍cuggatau tāvadaiva, cak‍kavāḷasiluc‍cayau|

parikkhipitvā taṃ sabbaṃ, laukadhātumayaṃ ṭhitau’’ti||

tattha candamaṇḍalaṃ aikūnapañ‍ñāsayaujanaṃ| sūriyamaṇḍalaṃ pañ‍ñāsayaujanaṃ| tāvatiṃsabhavanaṃ dasasahassayaujanaṃ| tathā asurabhavanaṃ avīcimahānirayau jambudīpau ca| aparagauyānaṃ sattasahassayaujanaṃ| tathā  pubbavidaihaṃ| uttarakuru aṭṭhasahassayaujanaṃ| aikamaikau caittha mahādīpau pañ‍casatapañ‍casataparittadīpaparivārau| taṃ sabbampi aikaṃ cak‍kavāḷaṃ aikā laukadhātu| tadantaraisu laukantarikanirayā|

aivaṃ anantāni cak‍kavāḷāni anantā laukadhātuyau bhagavā anantaina buddhañāṇaina avaidi añ‍ñāsi paṭivijjhi| aivamassa aukāsalaukaupi sabbathā viditau| aivampi sabbathā viditalaukattā laukavidū|

138. attanā pana guṇaihi visiṭṭhatarassa kassaci abhāvatau natthi aitassa uttarauti anuttarau| tathā haisa sīlaguṇaināpi sabbaṃ laukamabhibhavati, samādhipañ‍ñāvimuttivimuttiñāṇadassanaguṇaināpi| sīlaguṇaināpi asamau asamasamau appaṭimau appaṭibhāgau appaṭipuggalau…pai॰… vimuttiñāṇadassanaguṇaināpi| yathāha – ‘‘na khau panāhaṃ samanupassāmi sadaivakai laukai samārakai…pai॰… sadaivamanussāya pajāya attanā sīlasampan‍natara’’nti vitthārau| aivaṃ aggapasādasuttādīni (a॰ ni॰ 4.34; itivu॰ 90) ‘‘na mai ācariyau atthī’’tiādikā (ma॰ ni॰ 1.285; mahāva॰ 11) gāthāyau ca vitthāraitabbā|

139. purisadammai sāraitīti purisadammasārathi| damaiti vinaitīti vuttaṃ hauti| tattha purisadammāti adantā damaituṃ yuttā tiracchānapurisāpi manussapurisāpi amanussapurisāpi| tathā hi bhagavatā tiracchānapurisāpi apalālau nāgarājā, cūḷaudarau, mahaudarau, aggisikhau, dhūmasikhau, aravāḷau nāgarājā, dhanapālakau hatthīti aivamādayau damitā nibbisā katā saraṇaisu ca sīlaisu ca patiṭṭhāpitā, manussapurisāpi sac‍cakanigaṇṭhaputtaambaṭṭhamāṇavapaukkharasāti sauṇadantakūṭadantādayau, amanussapurisāpi āḷavakasūcilaumakharalaumayakkhasak‍kadaivarājādayau  damitā vinītā vicitraihi vinayanūpāyaihi| ‘‘ahaṃ khau, kaisi, purisadammai saṇhainapi vinaimi, pharusainapi vinaimi, saṇhapharusainapi vinaimī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 4.11) idañ‍caittha suttaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|

apica bhagavā visuddhasīlādīnaṃ paṭhamajjhānādīni sautāpan‍nādīnañ‍ca uttari maggapaṭipadaṃ ācikkhantau dantaipi damaitiyaiva|

atha  vā anuttarau purisadammasārathīti aikamaividaṃ atthapadaṃ| bhagavā hi tathā purisadammai sāraiti, yathā aikapal‍laṅkainaiva nisin‍nā aṭṭha disā asaj‍jamānā dhāvanti| tasmā anuttarau purisadammasārathīti vuc‍cati| ‘‘hatthidamakaina, bhikkhavai, hatthidammau sāritau aikaṃyaiva disaṃ dhāvatī’’ti idañ‍caittha suttaṃ (ma॰ ni॰ 3.312) vitthāraitabbaṃ|

140. diṭṭhadhammikasamparāyikaparamatthaihi yathārahaṃ anusāsatīti satthā| apica satthā viyāti satthā, bhagavā satthavāhau| yathā satthavāhau satthai kantāraṃ tāraiti caurakantāraṃ tāraiti vāḷakantāraṃ tāraiti dubbhikkhakantāraṃ tāraiti nirudakakantāraṃ tāraiti uttāraiti nittāraiti patāraiti khaimantabhūmiṃ sampāpaiti, aivamaiva bhagavā satthā satthavāhau sattai kantāraṃ tāraiti, jātikantāraṃ tāraitītiādinā niddaisanayainapaittha atthau vaiditabbau| daivamanussānanti daivānañ‍ca manussānañ‍ca| uk‍kaṭṭhaparicchaidavasaina, bhabbapuggalaparicchaidavasaina caitaṃ vuttaṃ| bhagavā pana tiracchānagatānampi anusāsanippadānaina satthāyaiva| taipi hi bhagavatau dhammassavanaina upanissayasampattiṃ patvā tāya aiva upanissayasampattiyā dutiyai vā tatiyai vā attabhāvai maggaphalabhāginau haunti| maṇḍūkadaivaputtādayau caittha nidassanaṃ|

bhagavati kira gaggarāya paukkharaṇiyā tīrai campānagaravāsīnaṃ dhammaṃ daisiyamānai aikau maṇḍūkau bhagavatau sarai nimittaṃ aggahaisi, taṃ aikau vacchapālakau daṇḍaṃ aulubbha tiṭṭhantau sīsai san‍nirumbhitvā aṭṭhāsi| sau tāvadaiva kālaṅkatvā tāvatiṃsabhavanai dvādasayaujanikai kanakavimānai nibbatti| suttappabuddhau viya ca tattha accharāsaṅghaparivutaṃ attānaṃ disvā ‘‘arai ahampi nāma idha nibbattau, kiṃ nu khau kammamakāsi’’nti āvaj‍jaintau na añ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍ci addasa  añ‍ñatra bhagavatau sarai nimittaggāhā| sau tāvadaiva saha vimānaina āgantvā bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandi| bhagavā jānantauva pucchi –

‘‘kau mai vandati pādāni, iddhiyā yasasā jalaṃ|

abhik‍kantaina vaṇṇaina, sabbā aubhāsayaṃ disā’’ti||

maṇḍūkauhaṃ  purai āsiṃ, udakai vārigaucarau|

tava dhammaṃ suṇantassa, avadhi vacchapālakauti||

bhagavā tassa dhammaṃ daisaisi| caturāsītiyā pāṇasahassānaṃ dhammābhisamayau ahausi| daivaputtaupi sautāpattiphalai patiṭṭhāya sitaṃ katvā pak‍kamīti|

141. yaṃ pana kiñ‍ci atthi ñaiyyaṃ nāma, sabbassaiva buddhattā vimaukkhantikañ‍ñāṇavasaina buddhau| yasmā vā cattāri sac‍cāni attanāpi bujjhi, añ‍ñaipi sattai baudhaisi, tasmā aivamādīhipi kāraṇaihi buddhau| imassa ca panatthassa viñ‍ñāpanatthaṃ ‘‘bujjhitā sac‍cānīti buddhau| baudhaitā pajāyāti buddhau’’ti aivaṃ pavattau sabbaupi niddaisanayau (mahāni॰ 192) paṭisambhidānayau (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.162) vā vitthāraitabbau|

142. bhagavāti idaṃ panassa guṇavisiṭṭhasabbasattuttamagarugāravādhivacanaṃ| taināhu paurāṇā –

‘‘bhagavāti vacanaṃ saiṭṭhaṃ, bhagavāti vacanamuttamaṃ|

garugāravayuttau sau, bhagavā taina vuc‍catī’’ti||

catubbidhaṃ vā nāmaṃ āvatthikaṃ liṅgikaṃ naimittikaṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍nanti| adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ nāma laukiyavauhāraina yadicchakanti vuttaṃ hauti| tattha vacchau dammau balībaddauti aivamādi āvatthikaṃ| daṇḍī chattī sikhī karīti aivamādi liṅgikaṃ| taivij‍jau chaḷabhiñ‍ñauti aivamādi naimittikaṃ| sirivaḍḍhakau dhanavaḍḍhakauti aivamādi vacanatthaṃ anapaikkhitvā pavattaṃ adhic‍casamuppan‍naṃ| idaṃ  pana bhagavāti nāmaṃ naimittikaṃ, na mahāmāyāya, na suddhaudanamahārājaina, na asītiyā ñātisahassaihi kataṃ, na sak‍kasantusitādīhi daivatāvisaisaihi| vuttampi caitaṃ dhammasaināpatinā ‘‘bhagavāti naitaṃ nāmaṃ mātarā kataṃ…pai॰… vimaukkhantikamaitaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ baudhiyā mūlai saha sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇassa paṭilābhā sacchikā pañ‍ñatti yadidaṃ bhagavā’’ti (mahāni॰ 84)|

143. yaṃguṇanaimittikañ‍caitaṃ nāmaṃ, taisaṃ guṇānaṃ pakāsanatthaṃ imaṃ gāthaṃ vadanti –

‘‘bhagī  bhajī bhāgi vibhattavā iti,

akāsi bhagganti garūti bhāgyavā|

bahūhi ñāyaihi subhāvitattanau,

bhavantagau sau bhagavāti vuc‍catī’’ti|| –

niddaisai (mahāni॰ 84) vuttanayainaiva caittha taisaṃ taisaṃ padānaṃ atthau daṭṭhabbau|

144. ayaṃ pana aparau nayau|

bhāgyavā bhaggavā yuttau, bhagaihi ca vibhattavā|

bhattavā vantagamanau, bhavaisu bhagavā tatauti||

tattha vaṇṇāgamau vaṇṇavipariyayautiādikaṃ niruttilakkhaṇaṃ gahaitvā saddanayaina vā pisaudarādipakkhaipalakkhaṇaṃ gahaitvā yasmā laukiyalaukuttarasukhābhinibbattakaṃ dānasīlādipārappattaṃ bhāgyamassa atthi, tasmā bhāgyavāti vattabbai bhagavāti vuc‍catīti ñātabbaṃ|

yasmā pana ahirikānauttappakaudhūpanāhamakkhapaḷāsaissāmacchariyamāyāsāṭhaiyyathambhasārambhamānātimānamadapamādataṇhāavij‍jā- tividhākusalamūladuc‍caritasaṃkilaisamalavisamasañ‍ñāvitak‍kapapañ‍cacatubbidhavipariyaisa- āsavaganthaaughayaugaagatitaṇhuppādupādānapañ‍cacaitaukhīlavinibandhanīvaraṇābhinandanā- chavivādamūlataṇhākāyasattānusayaaṭṭhamicchattanavataṇhāmūlakadasākusalakammapathadvāsaṭṭhidiṭṭhigata- aṭṭhasatataṇhāvicaritappabhaidasabbadarathapariḷāhakilaisasatasahassāni, saṅkhaipatau vā pañ‍ca kilaisakhandhaabhisaṅkhāradaivaputtamac‍cumārai abhañ‍ji| tasmā bhaggattā aitaisaṃ parissayānaṃ bhaggavāti vattabbai bhagavāti vuc‍cati| āha caittha –

‘‘bhaggarāgau bhaggadausau, bhaggamauhau anāsavau|

bhaggāssa pāpakā dhammā, bhagavā taina vuc‍catī’’ti||

bhāgyavatāya cassa satapuñ‍ñalakkhaṇadharassa rūpakāyasampatti dīpitā hauti| bhaggadausatāya dhammakāyasampatti| tathā laukiyasarikkhakānaṃ bahumatabhāvau, gahaṭṭhapabbajitaihi abhigamanīyatā, abhigatānañ‍ca naisaṃ kāyacittadukkhāpanayanai  paṭibalabhāvau, āmisadānadhammadānaihi upakāritā, laukiyalaukuttarasukhaihi ca saṃyaujanasamatthatā dīpitā hauti|

yasmā ca laukai issariyadhammayasasirikāmapayattaisu chasu dhammaisu bhagasaddau pavattati, paramañ‍cassa sakacittai issariyaṃ, aṇimālaṅghimādikaṃ vā laukiyasammataṃ sabbākāraparipūraṃ atthi| tathā laukuttarau dhammau| laukattayabyāpakau yathābhuc‍caguṇādhigatau ativiya parisuddhau yasau| rūpakāyadassanabyāvaṭajananayanappasādajananasamatthā sabbākāraparipūrā sabbaṅgapac‍caṅgasirī| yaṃ yaṃ aitaina icchitaṃ patthitaṃ attahitaṃ parahitaṃ vā, tassa tassa tathaiva abhinipphan‍nattā icchitatthanibbattisañ‍ñitau kāmau| sabbalaukagarubhāvappattihaitubhūtau sammāvāyāmasaṅkhātau payattau ca atthi| tasmā imaihi bhagaihi yuttattāpi bhagā assa santīti iminā atthaina bhagavāti vuc‍cati|

yasmā pana kusalādīhi bhaidaihi sabbadhammai, khandhāyatanadhātusac‍caindriyapaṭic‍casamuppādādīhi vā kusalādidhammai, pīḷanasaṅkhatasantāpavipariṇāmaṭṭhaina vā dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, āyūhananidānasaṃyaugapalibaudhaṭṭhaina samudayaṃ, nissaraṇavivaikāsaṅkhataamataṭṭhaina niraudhaṃ, niyyānikahaitudassanādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina maggaṃ vibhattavā, vibhajitvā vivaritvā daisitavāti vuttaṃ hauti| tasmā vibhattavāti vattabbai bhagavāti vuc‍cati|

yasmā ca aisa dibbabrahmaariyavihārai kāyacittaupadhivivaikai suñ‍ñatappaṇihitānimittavimaukkhai añ‍ñai ca laukiyalaukuttarai uttarimanussadhammai bhaji saivi bahulaṃ akāsi, tasmā bhattavāti vattabbai bhagavāti vuc‍cati|

yasmā  pana tīsu bhavaisu taṇhāsaṅkhātaṃ gamanaṃ anaina vantaṃ, tasmā bhavaisu vantagamanauti vattabbai bhavasaddatau bhakāraṃ gamanasaddatau gakāraṃ vantasaddatau vakārañ‍ca dīghaṃ katvā ādāya bhagavāti vuc‍cati yathā laukai maihanassa khassa mālāti vattabbai maikhalāti|

145. tassaivaṃ iminā ca iminā ca kāraṇaina sau bhagavā arahaṃ…pai॰… iminā ca iminā ca kāraṇaina bhagavāti buddhaguṇai anussaratau naiva tasmiṃ samayai rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na dausapariyuṭṭhitaṃ, na mauhapariyuṭṭhitaṃ  cittaṃ hauti| ujugatamaivassa tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti tathāgatamārabbha (a॰ ni॰ 6.10)| ic‍cassa aivaṃ rāgādipariyuṭṭhānābhāvaina vikkhambhitanīvaraṇassa kammaṭṭhānābhimukhatāya ujugatacittassa buddhaguṇapauṇā vitak‍kavicārā pavattanti| buddhaguṇai anuvitak‍kayatau anuvicārayatau pīti uppaj‍jati| pītimanassa pītipadaṭṭhānāya passaddhiyā kāyacittadarathā paṭippassambhanti| passaddhadarathassa kāyikampi caitasikampi sukhaṃ uppaj‍jati| sukhinau buddhaguṇārammaṇaṃ hutvā cittaṃ samādhiyatīti anuk‍kamaina aikakkhaṇai jhānaṅgāni uppaj‍janti| buddhaguṇānaṃ pana gambhīratāya nānappakāraguṇānussaraṇādhimuttatāya vā appanaṃ appatvā upacārappattamaiva jhānaṃ hauti| tadaitaṃ buddhaguṇānussaraṇavasaina uppan‍nattā buddhānussatic‍caiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

imañ‍ca pana buddhānussatiṃ anuyuttau bhikkhu satthari sagāravau hauti sappatissau, saddhāvaipul‍laṃ sativaipul‍laṃ pañ‍ñāvaipul‍laṃ puñ‍ñavaipul‍lañ‍ca adhigacchati, pītipāmauj‍jabahulau hauti, bhayabhairavasahau dukkhādhivāsanasamatthau, satthārā saṃvāsasañ‍ñaṃ paṭilabhati| buddhaguṇānussatiyā ajjhāvutthañ‍cassa sarīrampi caitiyagharamiva pūjārahaṃ hauti| buddhabhūmiyaṃ cittaṃ namati| vītik‍kamitabbavatthusamāyaugai cassa sammukhā satthāraṃ passatau viya hirauttappaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhāti| uttari appaṭivijjhantau pana sugatiparāyanau hauti|

tasmā havai appamādaṃ, kayirātha sumaidhasau|

aivaṃ mahānubhāvāya, buddhānussatiyā sadāti||

idaṃ tāva buddhānussatiyaṃ vitthārakathāmukhaṃ|

2. dhammānussatikathā

146. dhammānussatiṃ  bhāvaitukāmaināpi rahaugataina paṭisal‍līnaina ‘‘svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau sandiṭṭhikau akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 6.10) aivaṃ pariyattidhammassa caiva navavidhassa ca laukuttaradhammassa guṇā anussaritabbā|

147. svākkhātauti imasmiṃ hi padai pariyattidhammaupi saṅgahaṃ gacchati, itaraisu laukuttaradhammauva| tattha pariyattidhammau tāva svākkhātau ādimajjhapariyausānakalyāṇattā  sātthasabyañ‍janakaivalaparipuṇṇaparisuddhabrahmacariyappakāsanattā ca| yañhi bhagavā aikagāthampi daisaiti, sā samantabhaddakattā dhammassa paṭhamapādaina ādikalyāṇā, dutiyatatiyapādaihi majjhaikalyāṇā, pacchimapādaina pariyausānakalyāṇā| aikānusandhikaṃ suttaṃ nidānaina ādikalyāṇaṃ, nigamanaina pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ, saisaina majjhaikalyāṇaṃ| nānānusandhikaṃ suttaṃ paṭhamānusandhinā ādikalyāṇaṃ, pacchimaina pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ, saisaihi majjhaikalyāṇaṃ| apica sanidānasauppattikattā ādikalyāṇaṃ, vainaiyyānaṃ anurūpatau atthassa aviparītatāya ca haitudāharaṇayuttatau ca majjhaikalyāṇaṃ, sautūnaṃ saddhāpaṭilābhajananaina nigamanaina ca pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ|

sakalaupi sāsanadhammau attanau atthabhūtaina sīlaina ādikalyāṇau, samathavipassanāmaggaphalaihi majjhaikalyāṇau, nibbānaina pariyausānakalyāṇau| sīlasamādhīhi vā ādikalyāṇau, vipassanāmaggaihi majjhaikalyāṇau, phalanibbānaihi pariyausānakalyāṇau| buddhasubaudhitāya vā ādikalyāṇau, dhammasudhammatāya majjhaikalyāṇau, saṅghasuppaṭippattiyā pariyausānakalyāṇau| taṃ sutvā tathatthāya paṭipan‍naina adhigantabbāya abhisambaudhiyā vā ādikalyāṇau, pac‍caikabaudhiyā majjhaikalyāṇau, sāvakabaudhiyā pariyausānakalyāṇau|

suyyamānau caisa nīvaraṇavikkhambhanatau savanainapi kalyāṇamaiva āvahatīti ādikalyāṇau, paṭipaj‍jiyamānau samathavipassanāsukhāvahanatau paṭipattiyāpi kalyāṇaṃ āvahatīti majjhaikalyāṇau, tathāpaṭipan‍nau ca paṭipattiphalai niṭṭhitai tādibhāvāvahanatau paṭipattiphalainapi  kalyāṇaṃ āvahatīti pariyausānakalyāṇauti aivaṃ ādimajjhapariyausānakalyāṇattā svākkhātau|

yaṃ panaisa bhagavā dhammaṃ daisaintau sāsanabrahmacariyaṃ maggabrahmacariyañ‍ca pakāsaiti nānānayaihi dīpaiti, taṃ yathānurūpaṃ atthasampattiyā sātthaṃ, byañ‍janasampattiyā sabyañ‍janaṃ| saṅkāsanapakāsanavivaraṇavibhajanauttānīkaraṇapañ‍ñattiatthapadasamāyaugatau sātthaṃ, akkharapadabyañ‍janākāraniruttiniddaisasampattiyā sabyañ‍janaṃ| atthagambhīratāpaṭivaidhagambhīratāhi sātthaṃ, dhammagambhīratādaisanāgambhīratāhi  sabyañ‍janaṃ| atthapaṭibhānapaṭisambhidāvisayatau sātthaṃ, dhammaniruttipaṭisambhidāvisayatau sabyañ‍janaṃ| paṇḍitavaidanīyatau parikkhakajanappasādakanti sātthaṃ, saddhaiyyatau laukiyajanappasādakanti sabyañ‍janaṃ| gambhīrādhippāyatau sātthaṃ, uttānapadatau sabyañ‍janaṃ| upanaitabbassa abhāvatau sakalaparipuṇṇabhāvaina kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ| apanaitabbassa abhāvatau niddausabhāvaina parisuddhaṃ|

apica paṭipattiyā adhigamabyattitau sātthaṃ, pariyattiyā āgamabyattitau sabyañ‍janaṃ, sīlādipañ‍cadhammakkhandhayuttatau kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ, nirupak‍kilaisatau nittaraṇatthāya pavattitau laukāmisanirapaikkhatau ca parisuddhanti aivaṃ sātthasabyañ‍janakaivalaparipuṇṇaparisuddhabrahmacariyappakāsanatau svākkhātau|

atthavipal‍lāsābhāvatau vā suṭṭhu akkhātauti svākkhātau| yathā hi añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ dhammassa atthau vipal‍lāsamāpaj‍jati, antarāyikāti vuttadhammānaṃ antarāyikattābhāvatau, niyyānikāti vuttadhammānaṃ niyyānikattābhāvatau| taina tai durakkhātadhammāyaiva haunti, na tathā bhagavatau dhammassa atthau vipal‍lāsamāpaj‍jati| imai dhammā antarāyikā, imai dhammā niyyānikāti aivaṃ vuttadhammānaṃ tathābhāvānatik‍kamanatauti| aivaṃ tāva pariyattidhammau svākkhātau|

laukuttaradhammau pana nibbānānurūpāya paṭipattiyā paṭipadānurūpassa ca nibbānassa akkhātattā svākkhātau| yathāha – ‘‘supañ‍ñattā khau pana taina bhagavatā sāvakānaṃ nibbānagāminī paṭipadā saṃsandati nibbānañ‍ca paṭipadā ca| saiyyathāpi nāma gaṅgaudakaṃ yamunaudakaina saṃsandati samaiti, aivamaiva supañ‍ñattā (dī॰ ni॰ 2.296) taina bhagavatā sāvakānaṃ  nibbānagāminī paṭipadā saṃsandati nibbānañ‍ca paṭipadā cā’’ti| ariyamaggau caittha antadvayaṃ anupagamma majjhimā paṭipadābhūtauva ‘‘majjhimā paṭipadā’’ti akkhātattā svākkhātau| sāmañ‍ñaphalāni paṭipassaddhakilaisānaiva ‘‘paṭipassaddhakilaisānī’’ti akkhātattā svākkhātāni| nibbānaṃ sassatāmatatāṇalaiṇādisabhāvamaiva sassatādisabhāvavasaina akkhātattā svākkhātanti aivaṃ laukuttaradhammaupi svākkhātau|

148. sandiṭṭhikauti  aittha pana ariyamaggau tāva attanau santānai rāgādīnaṃ abhāvaṃ karauntaina ariyapuggalaina sāmaṃ daṭṭhabbauti sandiṭṭhikau| yathāha –‘‘rattau khau, brāhmaṇa, rāgaina abhibhūtau pariyādiṇṇacittau attabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, parabyābādhāyapi caitaiti, ubhayabyābādhāyapi caitaiti| caitasikaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| rāgai pahīnai naiva attabyābādhāya caitaiti, na parabyābādhāya caitaiti, na ubhayabyābādhāya caitaiti, na caitasikaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| aivampi khau, brāhmaṇa, sandiṭṭhikau dhammau hautī’’ti(a॰ ni॰ 3.54)| apica navavidhaupi laukuttaradhammau yaina yaina adhigatau hauti, taina taina parasaddhāya gantabbataṃ hitvā pac‍cavaikkhaṇañāṇaina sayaṃ daṭṭhabbauti sandiṭṭhikau| atha vā pasatthā diṭṭhi sandiṭṭhi, sandiṭṭhiyā jayatīti sandiṭṭhikau| tathā haittha ariyamaggau sampayuttāya, ariyaphalaṃ kāraṇabhūtāya, nibbānaṃ visayibhūtāya sandiṭṭhiyā kilaisai jayati| tasmā yathā rathaina jayatīti rathikau, aivaṃ navavidhaupi laukuttaradhammau sandiṭṭhiyā jayatīti sandiṭṭhikau|

atha vā diṭṭhanti dassanaṃ vuc‍cati| diṭṭhamaiva sandiṭṭhaṃ, dassananti atthau| sandiṭṭhaṃ arahatīti sandiṭṭhikau| laukuttaradhammau hi bhāvanābhisamayavasaina sacchikiriyābhisamayavasaina ca dissamānauyaiva vaṭṭabhayaṃ nivattaiti| tasmā yathā vatthaṃ arahatīti vatthikau, aivaṃ sandiṭṭhaṃ arahatīti sandiṭṭhikau|

149. attanau phaladānaṃ sandhāya nāssa kālauti akālau| akālauyaiva akālikau| na pañ‍cāhasattāhādibhaidaṃ kālaṃ khaipaitvā phalaṃ daiti, attanau pana pavattisamanantaramaiva phaladauti vuttaṃ hauti| atha vā attanau phaladānai pakaṭṭhau kālau pattau assāti kālikau| kau sau? laukiyau  kusaladhammau| ayaṃ pana samanantaraphalattā na kālikauti akālikau| idaṃ maggamaiva sandhāya vuttaṃ|

150. ‘‘aihi passa imaṃ dhamma’’nti aivaṃ pavattaṃ aihipassavidhiṃ arahatīti aihipassikau| kasmā panaisa taṃ vidhiṃ arahatīti? vij‍jamānattā parisuddhattā ca| rittamuṭṭhiyaṃ hi hirañ‍ñaṃ vā suvaṇṇaṃ vā atthīti vatvāpi ‘‘aihi passa ima’’nti na sak‍kā vattuṃ| kasmā? avij‍jamānattā| vij‍jamānampi ca gūthaṃ vā muttaṃ vā manuñ‍ñabhāvappakāsanaina cittasampahaṃsanatthaṃ ‘‘aihi passa ima’’nti na sak‍kā vattuṃ| apica khau pana tiṇaihi vā paṇṇaihi vā paṭicchādaitabbamaiva hauti| kasmā? aparisuddhattā| ayaṃ pana navavidhaupi laukuttaradhammau sabhāvatauva vij‍jamānau vigatavalāhakai ākāsai sampuṇṇacandamaṇḍalaṃ viya paṇḍukambalai nikkhittajātimaṇi viya ca parisuddhau| tasmā vij‍jamānattā parisuddhattā ca aihipassavidhiṃ arahatīti aihipassikau|

151. upanaitabbauti aupanaiyyikau| ayaṃ panaittha vinicchayau, upanayanaṃ upanayau, ādittaṃ cailaṃ vā sīsaṃ vā ajjhupaikkhitvāpi bhāvanāvasaina attanau cittai upanayanaṃ arahatīti aupanayikau| aupanayikauva aupanaiyyikau| idaṃ saṅkhatai laukuttaradhammai yuj‍jati| asaṅkhatai pana attanau cittaina upanayanaṃ arahatīti aupanaiyyikau| sacchikiriyāvasaina al‍līyanaṃ arahatīti atthau|

atha vā nibbānaṃ upanaitīti ariyamaggau upanaiyyau| sacchikātabbataṃ upanaitabbauti phalanibbānadhammau upanaiyyau| upanaiyyau aiva aupanaiyyikau|

152. pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhīti sabbaihipi ugghaṭitañ‍ñūādīhi viñ‍ñūhi attani attani vaiditabbau ‘‘bhāvitau mai maggau, adhigataṃ phalaṃ, sacchikatau niraudhau’’ti| na hi upajjhāyaina bhāvitaina maggaina saddhivihārikassa kilaisā pahīyanti, na sau tassa phalasamāpattiyā phāsuviharati, na taina sacchikataṃ nibbānaṃ sacchikarauti| tasmā na aisa parassa sīsai ābharaṇaṃ viya daṭṭhabbau, attanau pana cittaiyaiva daṭṭhabbau, anubhavitabbau viñ‍ñūhīti vuttaṃ hauti| bālānaṃ pana avisayau caisa|

apica  svākkhātau ayaṃ dhammau| kasmā? sandiṭṭhikattā| sandiṭṭhikau, akālikattā| akālikau, aihipassikattā| yau ca aihipassikau, sau nāma aupanaiyyikau hautīti|

153. tassaivaṃ svākkhātatādibhaidai dhammaguṇai anussaratau naiva tasmiṃ samayai rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti| na dausa…pai॰… na mauhapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti| ujugatamaivassa tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti dhammaṃ ārabbhāti (a॰ ni॰ 6.10) purimanayainaiva vikkhambhitanīvaraṇassa aikakkhaṇai jhānaṅgāni uppaj‍janti| dhammaguṇānaṃ pana gambhīratāya nānappakāraguṇānussaraṇādhimuttatāya vā appanaṃ appatvā upacārappattamaiva jhānaṃ hauti| tadaitaṃ dhammaguṇānussaraṇavasaina uppan‍nattā dhammānussatic‍caiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

imañ‍ca pana dhammānussatiṃ anuyuttau bhikkhu aivaṃ aupanaiyyikassa dhammassa daisaitāraṃ imināpaṅgaina saman‍nāgataṃ satthāraṃ naiva atītaṃsai samanupassāmi, na panaitarahi añ‍ñatra taina bhagavatāti aivaṃ dhammaguṇadassanainaiva satthari sagāravau hauti sappatissau| dhammai garucittīkārau saddhādivaipul‍laṃ adhigacchati, pītipāmauj‍jabahulau hauti, bhayabhairavasahau, dukkhādhivāsanasamatthau, dhammaina saṃvāsasañ‍ñaṃ paṭilabhati, dhammaguṇānussatiyā ajjhāvutthañ‍cassa sarīrampi caitiyagharamiva pūjārahaṃ hauti, anuttaradhammādhigamāya cittaṃ namati, vītik‍kamitabbavatthusamāyaugai cassa dhammasudhammataṃ samanussaratau hirauttappaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhāti| uttari appaṭivijjhantau pana sugatiparāyanau hauti|

tasmā havai appamādaṃ, kayirātha sumaidhasau|

aivaṃ mahānubhāvāya, dhammānussatiyā sadāti||

idaṃ dhammānussatiyaṃ vitthārakathāmukhaṃ|

3. saṅghānussatikathā

154. saṅghānussatiṃ bhāvaitukāmaināpi rahaugataina paṭisal‍līnaina ‘‘suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, ujuppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, ñāyappaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau  bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, yadidaṃ cattāri purisayugāni aṭṭha purisapuggalā, aisa bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau āhunaiyyau, pāhunaiyyau, dakkhiṇaiyyau, añ‍jalikaraṇīyau, anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 6.10) aivaṃ ariyasaṅghaguṇā anussaritabbā|

155. tattha suppaṭipan‍nauti suṭṭhu paṭipan‍nau, sammāpaṭipadaṃ anivattipaṭipadaṃ anulaumapaṭipadaṃ apac‍canīkapaṭipadaṃ dhammānudhammapaṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍nauti vuttaṃ hauti| bhagavatau auvādānusāsaniṃ sak‍kac‍caṃ suṇantīti sāvakā| sāvakānaṃ saṅghau sāvakasaṅghau, sīladiṭṭhisāmañ‍ñatāya saṅghātabhāvamāpan‍nau sāvakasamūhauti atthau| yasmā pana sā sammāpaṭipadā uju avaṅkā akuṭilā ajimhā, ariyau ca ñāyautipi vuc‍cati, anucchavikattā ca sāmīcītipi saṅkhaṃ gatā| tasmā tampaṭipan‍nau ariyasaṅghau ujuppaṭipan‍nau ñāyappaṭipan‍nau sāmīcippaṭipan‍nautipi vuttau|

aittha ca yai maggaṭṭhā, tai sammāpaṭipattisamaṅgitāya suppaṭipan‍nā| yai phalaṭṭhā, tai sammāpaṭipadāya adhigantabbassa adhigatattā atītaṃ paṭipadaṃ sandhāya suppaṭipan‍nāti vaiditabbā|

apica svākkhātai dhammavinayai yathānusiṭṭhaṃ paṭipan‍nattāpi apaṇṇakapaṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍nattāpi suppaṭipan‍nau|

majjhimāya paṭipadāya antadvayamanupagamma paṭipan‍nattā kāyavacīmanauvaṅkakuṭilajimhadausappahānāya paṭipan‍nattā ca ujuppaṭipan‍nattā ca ujuppaṭipan‍nau|

ñāyau vuc‍cati nibbānaṃ| tadatthāya paṭipan‍nattā ñāyappaṭipan‍nau|

yathā paṭipan‍nā sāmīcippaṭipan‍nārahā haunti, tathā paṭipan‍nattā sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau|

156. yadidanti yāni imāni| cattāri purisayugānīti yugaḷavasaina paṭhamamaggaṭṭhau phalaṭṭhauti idamaikaṃ yugaḷanti aivaṃ cattāri purisayugaḷāni haunti| aṭṭha purisapuggalāti purisapuggalavasaina aikau paṭhamamaggaṭṭhau aikau phalaṭṭhauti iminā nayaina aṭṭhaiva purisapuggalā haunti| aittha ca purisauti vā puggalauti vā aikatthāni aitāni padāni| vainaiyyavasaina panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| aisa bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghauti yānimāni yugavasaina  cattāri purisayugāni, pāṭiaik‍katau aṭṭha purisapuggalā, aisa bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, āhunaiyyautiādīsu ānaitvā hunitabbanti āhunaṃ, dūrataupi ānaitvā sīlavantaisu dātabbanti atthau| catun‍naṃ pac‍cayānamaitamadhivacanaṃ| taṃ āhunaṃ paṭiggahaituṃ yuttau tassa mahapphalakaraṇatauti āhunaiyyau| atha vā dūrataupi āgantvā sabbasāpataiyyampi aittha hunitabbanti āhavanīyau| sak‍kādīnampi vā āhavanaṃ arahatīti āhavanīyau| yau cāyaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ āhavanīyau nāma aggi, yattha hutaṃ mahapphalanti taisaṃ laddhi| sacai hutassa mahapphalatāya āhavanīyau, saṅghauva āhavanīyau| saṅghai hutañhi mahapphalaṃ hauti| yathāha –

‘‘yau ca vassasataṃ jantu, aggiṃ paricarai vanai|

aikañ‍ca bhāvitattānaṃ, muhuttamapi pūjayai|

sāyaiva pūjanā saiyyau, yañ‍cai vassasataṃ huta’’nti|| (dha॰ pa॰ 107)|

tadaitaṃ nikāyantarai āhavanīyauti padaṃ idha āhunaiyyauti iminā padaina atthatau aikaṃ| byañ‍janatau panaittha kiñ‍cimattamaiva nānaṃ| iti āhunaiyyau|

pāhunaiyyauti aittha pana pāhunaṃ vuc‍cati disāvidisatau āgatānaṃ piyamanāpānaṃ ñātimittānamatthāya sak‍kāraina paṭiyattaṃ āgantukadānaṃ| tampi ṭhapaitvā tai tathārūpai pāhunakai saṅghassaiva dātuṃ yuttaṃ, saṅghauva taṃ paṭiggahaituṃ yuttau| saṅghasadisau hi pāhunakau natthi| tathā haisa aikabuddhantarai ca dissati, abbaukiṇṇañ‍ca piyamanāpattakaraihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatauti| aivaṃ pāhunamassa dātuṃ yuttaṃ pāhunañ‍ca paṭiggahaituṃ yuttauti pāhunaiyyau| yaisaṃ pana pāhavanīyauti pāḷi, taisaṃ yasmā saṅghau pubbakāramarahati, tasmā sabbapaṭhamaṃ ānaitvā aittha hunitabbanti pāhavanīyau| sabbappakāraina vā āhavanamarahatīti pāhavanīyau| svāyamidha tainaiva atthaina pāhunaiyyauti vuc‍cati|

dakkhiṇāti pana paralaukaṃ saddahitvā dātabbadānaṃ vuc‍cati| taṃ dakkhiṇaṃ arahati, dakkhiṇāya vā hitau yasmā naṃ mahapphalakaraṇatāya visaudhaitīti dakkhiṇaiyyau|

ubhau  hatthai sirasmiṃ patiṭṭhapaitvā sabbalaukaina kayiramānaṃ añ‍jalikammaṃ arahatīti añ‍jalikaraṇīyau|

anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassāti sabbalaukassa asadisaṃ puñ‍ñavirūhanaṭṭhānaṃ| yathā hi rañ‍ñau vā amac‍cassa vā sālīnaṃ vā yavānaṃ vā virūhanaṭṭhānaṃ rañ‍ñau sālikkhaittaṃ rañ‍ñau yavakkhaittanti vuc‍cati, aivaṃ saṅghau sabbalaukassa puñ‍ñānaṃ virūhanaṭṭhānaṃ| saṅghaṃ nissāya hi laukassa nānappakārahitasukhasaṃvattanikāni puñ‍ñāni virūhanti| tasmā saṅghau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassāti|

157. aivaṃ suppaṭipan‍natādibhaidai saṅghaguṇai anussaratau naiva tasmiṃ samayai rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti| na dausa…pai॰… na mauhapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti| ujugatamaivassa tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti saṅghaṃ ārabbhāti (a॰ ni॰ 6.10) purimanayainaiva vikkhambhitanīvaraṇassa aikakkhaṇai jhānaṅgāni uppaj‍janti| saṅghaguṇānaṃ pana gambhīratāya nānappakāraguṇānussaraṇādhimuttatāya vā appanaṃ appatvā upacārappattamaiva jhānaṃ hauti| tadaitaṃ saṅghaguṇānussaraṇavasaina uppan‍nattā saṅghānussatic‍caiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

imañ‍ca pana saṅghānussatiṃ anuyuttau bhikkhu saṅghai sagāravau hauti sappatissau| saddhādivaipul‍laṃ adhigacchati, pītipāmauj‍jabahulau hauti, bhayabhairavasahau, dukkhādhivāsanasamatthau, saṅghaina saṃvāsasañ‍ñaṃ paṭilabhati| saṅghaguṇānussatiyā ajjhāvutthañ‍cassa sarīraṃ san‍nipatitasaṅghamiva upausathāgāraṃ pūjārahaṃ hauti, saṅghaguṇādhigamāya cittaṃ namati, vītik‍kamitabbavatthusamāyaugai cassa sammukhā saṅghaṃ passatau viya hirauttappaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhāti, uttari appaṭivijjhantau pana sugatiparāyanau hauti|

tasmā havai appamādaṃ, kayirātha sumaidhasau|

aivaṃ mahānubhāvāya, saṅghānussatiyā sadāti||

idaṃ saṅghānussatiyaṃ vitthārakathāmukhaṃ|

4. sīlānussatikathā

158. sīlānussatiṃ  bhāvaitukāmaina pana rahaugataina paṭisal‍līnaina ‘‘ahau vata mai sīlāni akhaṇḍāni acchiddāni asabalāni akammāsāni bhujissāni viñ‍ñuppasatthāni aparāmaṭṭhāni samādhisaṃvattanikānī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 6.10) aivaṃ akhaṇḍatādiguṇavasaina attanau sīlāni anussaritabbāni| tāni ca gahaṭṭhaina gahaṭṭhasīlāni, pabbajitaina pabbajitasīlāni|

gahaṭṭhasīlāni vā hauntu pabbajitasīlāni vā, yaisaṃ ādimhi vā antai vā aikampi na bhin‍naṃ, tāni pariyantai chin‍nasāṭakau viya na khaṇḍānīti akhaṇḍāni| yaisaṃ vaimajjhai aikampi na bhin‍naṃ, tāni majjhai vinividdhasāṭakau viya na chiddānīti acchiddāni| yaisaṃ paṭipāṭiyā dvai vā tīṇi vā na bhin‍nāni, tāni piṭṭhiyā vā kucchiyā vā uṭṭhitaina dīghavaṭṭādisaṇṭhānaina visabhāgavaṇṇaina kāḷarattādīnaṃ añ‍ñatarasarīravaṇṇā gāvī viya na sabalānīti asabalāni| yāni antarantarā na bhin‍nāni, tāni visabhāgabinduvicitrā gāvī viya na kammāsānīti akammāsāni| avisaisaina vā sabbānipi sattavidhaina maithunasaṃyaugaina kaudhupanāhādīhi ca pāpadhammaihi anupahatattā akhaṇḍāni acchiddāni asabalāni akammāsāni| tāniyaiva taṇhādāsabyatau maucaitvā bhujissabhāvakaraṇaina bhujissāni| buddhādīhi viñ‍ñūhi pasatthattā viñ‍ñuppasatthāni| taṇhādiṭṭhīhi aparāmaṭṭhatāya kainaci vā ayaṃ tai sīlaisu dausauti aivaṃ parāmaṭṭhuṃ asak‍kuṇaiyyatāya aparāmaṭṭhāni| upacārasamādhiṃ appanāsamādhiṃ vā, atha vā pana maggasamādhiṃ phalasamādhiñ‍cāpi saṃvattaintīti samādhisaṃvattanikāni|

159. aivaṃ akhaṇḍatādiguṇavasaina attanau sīlāni anussaratau naivassa tasmiṃ samayai rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti| na dausa…pai॰… na mauhapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti| ujugatamaivassa tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti, sīlaṃ ārabbhāti purimanayainaiva vikkhambhitanīvaraṇassa aikakkhaṇai jhānaṅgāni uppaj‍janti| sīlaguṇānaṃ pana gambhīratāya nānappakāraguṇānussaraṇādhimuttatāya vā appanaṃ appatvā upacārappattamaiva jhānaṃ hauti| tadaitaṃ  sīlaguṇānussaraṇavasaina uppan‍nattā sīlānussatic‍caiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

imañ‍ca  pana sīlānussatiṃ anuyuttau bhikkhu sikkhāya sagāravau hauti sabhāgavutti, paṭisanthārai appamattau, attānuvādādibhayavirahitau, aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, saddhādivaipul‍laṃ adhigacchati, pītipāmauj‍jabahulau hauti| uttari appaṭivijjhantau pana sugatiparāyanau hauti|

tasmā havai appamādaṃ, kayirātha sumaidhasau|

aivaṃ mahānubhāvāya, sīlānussatiyā sadāti||

idaṃ sīlānussatiyaṃ vitthārakathāmukhaṃ|

5. cāgānussatikathā

160. cāgānussatiṃ bhāvaitukāmaina pana pakatiyā cāgādhimuttaina nic‍cappavattadānasaṃvibhāgaina bhavitabbaṃ| atha vā pana bhāvanaṃ ārabhantaina itau dāni pabhuti sati paṭiggāhakai antamasau aikālaupamattampi dānaṃ adatvā na bhuñ‍jissāmīti samādānaṃ katvā taṃdivasaṃ guṇavisiṭṭhaisu paṭiggāhakaisu yathāsatti yathābalaṃ dānaṃ datvā tattha nimittaṃ gaṇhitvā rahaugataina paṭisal‍līnaina ‘‘lābhā vata mai suladdhaṃ vata mai, yauhaṃ macchairamalapariyuṭṭhitāya pajāya vigatamalamacchairaina caitasā viharāmi muttacāgau payatapāṇi vaussaggaratau yācayaugau dānasaṃvibhāgaratau’’ti aivaṃ vigatamalamacchairatādiguṇavasaina attanau cāgau anussaritabbau|

tattha lābhā vata maiti mayhaṃ vata lābhā, yai imai ‘‘āyuṃ khau pana datvā āyussa bhāgī hauti dibbassa vā mānusassa vā’’ iti (a॰ ni॰ 5.37) ca, ‘‘dadaṃ piyau hauti bhajanti naṃ bahū’’ iti (a॰ ni॰ 5.34) ca, ‘‘dadamānau piyau hauti, sataṃ dhammaṃ anuk‍kamaṃ’’ iti (a॰ ni॰ 5.35) ca aivamādīhi nayaihi bhagavatā dāyakassa lābhā saṃvaṇṇitā, tai mayhaṃ avassaṃ bhāginauti adhippāyau| suladdhaṃ vata maiti yaṃ mayā idaṃ sāsanaṃ manussattaṃ vā laddhaṃ, taṃ suladdhaṃ vata mai| kasmā? yauhaṃ macchairamalapariyuṭṭhitāya pajāya…pai॰… dānasaṃvibhāgaratauti|

tattha  macchairamalapariyuṭṭhitāyāti macchairamalaina abhibhūtāya| pajāyāti pajāyanavasaina sattā vuc‍canti| tasmā attanau sampattīnaṃ parasādhāraṇabhāvamasahanalakkhaṇaina cittassa pabhassarabhāvadūsakānaṃ kaṇhadhammānaṃ añ‍ñataraina macchairamalaina abhibhūtaisu sattaisūti ayamaittha atthau| vigatamalamacchairaināti añ‍ñaisampi rāgadausādimalānañ‍caiva macchairassa ca vigatattā vigatamalamacchairaina| caitasā viharāmīti yathāvuttappakāracittau hutvā vasāmīti atthau| suttaisu pana mahānāmasak‍kassa sautāpan‍nassa satau nissayavihāraṃ pucchatau nissayavihāravasaina daisitattā agāraṃ ajjhāvasāmīti vuttaṃ| tattha abhibhavitvā vasāmīti atthau|

muttacāgauti vissaṭṭhacāgau| payatapāṇīti parisuddhahatthau| sak‍kac‍caṃ sahatthā daiyyadhammaṃ dātuṃ sadā dhautahatthauyaivāti vuttaṃ hauti| vaussaggaratauti vaussaj‍janaṃ vaussaggau, paric‍cāgauti atthau| tasmiṃ vaussaggai satatābhiyaugavasaina ratauti vaussaggaratau| yācayaugauti yaṃ yaṃ parai yācanti, tassa tassa dānatau yācanayaugauti atthau| yājayaugautipi pāṭhau| yajanasaṅkhātaina yājaina yuttauti atthau| dānasaṃvibhāgaratauti dānai ca saṃvibhāgai ca ratau| ahañhi dānañ‍ca daimi, attanā paribhuñ‍jitabbataupi ca saṃvibhāgaṃ karaumi, aitthaiva casmi ubhayai ratauti aivaṃ anussaratīti atthau|

161. tassaivaṃ vigatamalamacchairatādiguṇavasaina attanau cāgaṃ anussaratau naiva tasmiṃ samayai rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti| na dausa…pai॰… na mauhapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti| ujugatamaivassa tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti cāgaṃ ārabbhāti (a॰ ni॰ 5.10) purimanayainaiva vikkhambhitanīvaraṇassa aikakkhaṇai jhānaṅgāni uppaj‍janti| cāgaguṇānaṃ pana gambhīratāya nānappakāracāgaguṇānussaraṇādhimuttatāya vā appanaṃ appatvā upacārappattamaiva jhānaṃ hauti| tadaitaṃ cāgaguṇānussaraṇavasaina uppan‍nattā cāgānussatic‍caiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

imañ‍ca  pana cāgānussatiṃ anuyuttau bhikkhu bhiyyausau mattāya cāgādhimuttau hauti, alaubhajjhāsayau, maittāya anulaumakārī, visāradau, pītipāmauj‍jabahulau, uttari appaṭivijjhantau pana sugatiparāyanau hauti|

tasmā  havai appamādaṃ, kayirātha sumaidhasau|

aivaṃ mahānubhāvāya, cāgānussatiyā sadāti||

idaṃ cāgānussatiyaṃ vitthārakathāmukhaṃ|

6. daivatānussatikathā

162. daivatānussatiṃ bhāvaitukāmaina pana ariyamaggavasaina samudāgataihi saddhādīhi guṇaihi saman‍nāgataina bhavitabbaṃ| tatau rahaugataina paṭisal‍līnaina ‘‘santi daivā cātumahārājikā, santi daivā tāvatiṃsā, yāmā, tusitā, nimmānaratinau, paranimmitavasavattinau, santi daivā brahmakāyikā, santi daivā tatuttari, yathārūpāya saddhāya saman‍nāgatā tā daivatā itau cutā tattha upapan‍nā, mayhampi tathārūpā saddhā saṃvij‍jati| yathārūpaina sīlaina| yathārūpaina sutaina| yathārūpaina cāgaina| yathārūpāya pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatā tā daivatā itau cutā tattha upapan‍nā, mayhampi tathārūpā pañ‍ñā saṃvij‍jatī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 6.10) aivaṃ daivatā sakkhiṭṭhānai ṭhapaitvā attanau saddhādiguṇā anussaritabbā|

suttai pana yasmiṃ mahānāma samayai ariyasāvakau attanau ca tāsañ‍ca daivatānaṃ saddhañ‍ca sīlañ‍ca sutañ‍ca cāgañ‍ca pañ‍ñañ‍ca anussarati, naivassa tasmiṃ samayai rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hautīti vuttaṃ| kiñ‍cāpi vuttaṃ, atha khau taṃ sakkhiṭṭhānai ṭhapaitabbadaivatānaṃ attanau saddhādīhi samānaguṇadīpanatthaṃ vuttanti vaiditabbaṃ| aṭṭhakathāyañhi daivatā sakkhiṭṭhānai ṭhapaitvā attanau guṇai anussaratīti daḷhaṃ katvā vuttaṃ|

163. tasmā pubbabhāgai daivatānaṃ guṇai anussaritvā aparabhāgai attanau saṃvij‍jamānai saddhādiguṇai anussaratau cassa naiva tasmiṃ samayai  rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti| na dausa…pai॰… na mauhapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, ujugatamaivassa tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti daivatā ārabbhāti (a॰ ni॰ 6.10) purimanayainaiva vikkhambhitanīvaraṇassa aikakkhaṇai jhānaṅgāni uppaj‍janti| saddhādiguṇānaṃ pana gambhīratāya nānappakāraguṇānussaraṇādhimuttatāya vā appanaṃ  appatvā upacārappattamaiva jhānaṃ hauti| tadaitaṃ daivatānaṃ guṇasadisasaddhādiguṇānussaraṇavasaina daivatānussatic‍caiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

imañ‍ca pana daivatānussatiṃ anuyuttau bhikkhu daivatānaṃ piyau hauti manāpau, bhiyyausau mattāya saddhādivaipul‍laṃ adhigacchati, pītipāmauj‍jabahulau viharati| uttari appaṭivijjhantau pana sugatiparāyanau hauti|

tasmā havai appamādaṃ, kayirātha sumaidhasau|

aivaṃ mahānubhāvāya, daivatānussatiyā sadāti||

idaṃ daivatānussatiyaṃ vitthārakathāmukhaṃ|

pakiṇṇakakathā

164. yaṃ pana aitāsaṃ vitthāradaisanāyaṃ ‘‘ujugatamaivassa tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti tathāgataṃ ārabbhā’’tiādīni vatvā ‘‘ujugatacittau khau pana, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau labhati atthavaidaṃ, labhati dhammavaidaṃ, labhati dhammūpasaṃhitaṃ pāmauj‍jaṃ, pamuditassa pīti jāyatī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 6.10) vuttaṃ, tattha itipi sau bhagavātiādīnaṃ atthaṃ nissāya uppan‍naṃ tuṭṭhiṃ sandhāya labhati atthavaidanti vuttaṃ| pāḷiṃ nissāya uppan‍naṃ tuṭṭhiṃ sandhāya labhati dhammavaidaṃ| ubhayavasaina labhati dhammūpasaṃhitaṃ pāmauj‍janti vuttanti vaiditabbaṃ|

yañ‍ca daivatānussatiyaṃ daivatā ārabbhāti vuttaṃ, taṃ pubbabhāgai daivatā ārabbha pavattacittavasaina daivatāguṇasadisai vā daivatābhāvanipphādakai guṇai ārabbha pavattacittavasaina vuttanti vaiditabbaṃ|

165. imā  pana cha anussatiyau ariyasāvakānañ‍ñaiva ijjhanti| taisaṃ hi buddhadhammasaṅghaguṇā pākaṭā haunti| tai ca akhaṇḍatādiguṇaihi sīlaihi, vigatamalamacchairaina cāgaina, mahānubhāvānaṃ daivatānaṃ guṇasadisaihi saddhādiguṇaihi saman‍nāgatā| mahānāmasuttai (a॰ ni॰ 6.10) ca  sautāpan‍nassa nissayavihāraṃ puṭṭhaina bhagavatā sautāpan‍nassa nissayavihāradassanatthamaiva aitā vitthāratau kathitā|

gaidhasuttaipi ‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau tathāgataṃ anussarati, itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… ujugatamaivassa tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti nikkhantaṃ muttaṃ vuṭṭhitaṃ gaidhamhā| gaidhauti khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naitaṃ kāmaguṇānamadhivacanaṃ| idampi khau, bhikkhavai, ārammaṇaṃ karitvā aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā visujjhantī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 6.25) aivaṃ ariyasāvakassa anussativasaina cittaṃ visaudhaitvā uttari paramatthavisuddhiadhigamatthāya kathitā|

āyasmatā mahākac‍cānaina daisitai sambādhaukāsasuttaipi ‘‘acchariyaṃ, āvusau, abbhutaṃ, āvusau, yāvañ‍cidaṃ taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina sambādhai aukāsādhigamau anubuddhau sattānaṃ visuddhiyā…pai॰… nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya yadidaṃ cha anussatiṭṭhānāni| katamāni cha? idhāvusau, ariyasāvakau tathāgataṃ anussarati…pai॰… aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā visuddhidhammā bhavantī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 6.26) aivaṃ ariyasāvakassaiva paramatthavisuddhidhammatāya aukāsādhigamavasaina kathitā|

upausathasuttaipi ‘‘kathañ‍ca, visākhai, ariyūpausathau hauti? upak‍kiliṭṭhassa, visākhai, cittassa upak‍kamaina pariyaudapanā hauti| kathañ‍ca, visākhai, upak‍kiliṭṭhassa cittassa upak‍kamaina pariyaudapanā hauti? idha, visākhai, ariyasāvakau tathāgataṃ anussaratī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 3.71) aivaṃ ariyasāvakassaiva upausathaṃ upavasatau cittavisaudhanakammaṭṭhānavasaina upausathassa mahapphalabhāvadassanatthaṃ kathitā|

aikādasanipātaipi ‘‘saddhau khau, mahānāma, ārādhakau hauti, nau assaddhau| āraddhavīriyau, upaṭṭhitasati, samāhitau, pañ‍ñavā, mahānāma, ārādhakau hauti, nau duppañ‍ñau| imaisu khau tvaṃ, mahānāma, pañ‍casu dhammaisu patiṭṭhāya cha dhammai uttari bhāvaiyyāsi| idha tvaṃ, mahānāma, tathāgataṃ anussaraiyyāsi itipi sau bhagavā’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 11.11) aivaṃ ariyasāvakassaiva ‘‘taisaṃ nau, bhantai, nānāvihāraina viharataṃ kainassa vihāraina viharitabba’’nti pucchatau vihāradassanatthaṃ kathitā|

166. aivaṃ  santaipi parisuddhasīlādiguṇasaman‍nāgataina puthuj‍janaināpi manasi kātabbā| anussavavasaināpi hi buddhādīnaṃ guṇai anussaratau cittaṃ pasīdatiyaiva| yassānubhāvaina nīvaraṇāni vikkhambhaitvā uḷārapāmauj‍jau vipassanaṃ ārabhitvā arahattaṃyaiva sacchikaraiyya kaṭaandhakāravāsī phussadaivatthairau viya|

sau kirāyasmā māraina nimmitaṃ buddharūpaṃ disvā ‘‘ayaṃ tāva sarāgadausamauhau aivaṃ saubhati, kathaṃ nu khau bhagavā na saubhati, sau hi sabbasau vītarāgadausamauhau’’ti buddhārammaṇaṃ pītiṃ paṭilabhitvā vipassanaṃ vaḍḍhaitvā arahattaṃ pāpuṇīti|

iti sādhujanapāmauj‍jatthāya katai visuddhimaggai

samādhibhāvanādhikārai

chaanussatiniddaisau nāma

sattamau paricchaidau|

8. anussatikammaṭṭhānaniddaisau

maraṇassatikathā

167. idāni  itau anantarāya maraṇassatiyā bhāvanāniddaisau anuppattau| tattha maraṇanti aikabhavapariyāpan‍nassa jīvitindriyassa upacchaidau| yaṃ panaitaṃ arahantānaṃ vaṭṭadukkhasamucchaidasaṅkhātaṃ samucchaidamaraṇaṃ, saṅkhārānaṃ khaṇabhaṅgasaṅkhātaṃ khaṇikamaraṇaṃ, rukkhau matau lauhaṃ matantiādīsu sammutimaraṇañ‍ca, na taṃ idha adhippaitaṃ|

yampi caitaṃ adhippaitaṃ, taṃ kālamaraṇaṃ akālamaraṇanti duvidhaṃ hauti| tattha kālamaraṇaṃ puñ‍ñakkhayaina vā āyukkhayaina vā ubhayakkhayaina vā hauti| akālamaraṇaṃ kammupacchaidakakammavasaina|

tattha yaṃ vij‍jamānāyapi āyusantānajanakapac‍cayasampattiyā kaivalaṃ paṭisandhijanakassa kammassa vipak‍kavipākattā maraṇaṃ hauti, idaṃ puñ‍ñakkhayaina maraṇaṃ nāma| yaṃ gatikālāhārādisampattiyā abhāvaina aj‍jatanakālapurisānaṃ viya vassasatamattaparimāṇassa āyunau khayavasaina maraṇaṃ hauti, idaṃ āyukkhayaina maraṇaṃ nāma| yaṃ pana dūsīmārakalāburājādīnaṃ viya taṅkhaṇañ‍ñaiva ṭhānācāvanasamatthaina kammunā upacchin‍nasantānānaṃ, purimakammavasaina vā satthaharaṇādīhi upak‍kamaihi upacchij‍jamānasantānānaṃ maraṇaṃ hauti, idaṃ akālamaraṇaṃ nāma| taṃ sabbampi vuttappakāraina jīvitindriyupacchaidaina saṅgahitaṃ| iti jīvitindriyupacchaidasaṅkhātassa maraṇassa saraṇaṃ maraṇassati|

168. taṃ bhāvaitukāmaina rahaugataina paṭisal‍līnaina ‘‘maraṇaṃ bhavissati, jīvitindriyaṃ upacchij‍jissatī’’ti vā, ‘‘maraṇaṃ maraṇa’’nti vā yaunisau manasikārau pavattaitabbau| ayaunisau  pavattayatau hi iṭṭhajanamaraṇānussaraṇai saukau uppaj‍jati vijātamātuyā piyaputtamaraṇānussaraṇai viya| aniṭṭhajanamaraṇānussaraṇai pāmauj‍jaṃ uppaj‍jati vairīnaṃ vairimaraṇānussaraṇai viya| majjhattajanamaraṇānussaraṇai saṃvaigau na uppaj‍jati matakaḷaivaradassanai chavaḍāhakassa viya| attanau maraṇānussaraṇai santāsau uppaj‍jati ukkhittāsikaṃ vadhakaṃ disvā bhīrukajātikassa  viya| tadaitaṃ sabbampi satisaṃvaigañāṇavirahatau hauti| tasmā tattha tattha hatamatasattai aulaukaitvā diṭṭhapubbasampattīnaṃ sattānaṃ matānaṃ maraṇaṃ āvaj‍jaitvā satiñ‍ca saṃvaigañ‍ca ñāṇañ‍ca yaujaitvā ‘‘maraṇaṃ bhavissatī’’tiādinā nayaina manasikārau pavattaitabbau| aivaṃ pavattaintau hi yaunisau pavattaiti, upāyaina pavattaitīti atthau| aivaṃ pavattayatauyaiva hi aikac‍cassa nīvaraṇāni vikkhambhanti, maraṇārammaṇā sati saṇṭhāti, upacārappattamaiva kammaṭṭhānaṃ hauti|

169. yassa pana aittāvatā na hauti, taina vadhakapac‍cupaṭṭhānatau, sampattivipattitau, upasaṃharaṇatau, kāyabahusādhāraṇatau, āyudubbalatau, animittatau, addhānaparicchaidatau, khaṇaparittatauti imaihi aṭṭhahākāraihi maraṇaṃ anussaritabbaṃ|

tattha vadhakapac‍cupaṭṭhānatauti vadhakassa viya pac‍cupaṭṭhānatau| yathā hi imassa sīsaṃ chindissāmīti asiṃ gahaitvā gīvāya cārayamānau vadhakau pac‍cupaṭṭhitauva hauti, aivaṃ maraṇampi pac‍cupaṭṭhitamaivāti anussaritabbaṃ| kasmā? saha jātiyā āgatatau, jīvitaharaṇatau ca| yathā hi ahicchattakamakuḷaṃ matthakaina paṃsuṃ gahaitvāva uggacchati, aivaṃ sattā jarāmaraṇaṃ gahaitvāva nibbattanti| tathā hi naisaṃ paṭisandhicittaṃ uppādānantaramaiva jaraṃ patvā pabbatasikharatau patitasilā viya bhij‍jati saddhiṃ sampayuttakhandhaihi| aivaṃ khaṇikamaraṇaṃ tāva saha jātiyā āgataṃ| jātassa pana avassaṃ maraṇatau idhādhippaitamaraṇampi saha jātiyā āgataṃ| tasmā aisa sattau jātakālatau paṭṭhāya yathā nāma uṭṭhitau sūriyau atthābhimukhau gacchataiva, gatagataṭṭhānatau īsakampi na nivattati| yathā vā nadī pabbataiyyā sīghasautā hārahārinī sandataiva vattataiva īsakampi na nivattati, aivaṃ īsakampi anivattamānau maraṇābhimukhauva yāti| taina vuttaṃ –

‘‘yamaikarattiṃ  paṭhamaṃ, gabbhai vasati māṇavau|

abbhuṭṭhitauva sau yāti, sa gacchaṃ na nivattatī’’ti|| (jā॰ 1.15.363)|

aivaṃ gacchatau cassa gimhābhitattānaṃ kun‍nadīnaṃ khayau viya, pātau āpaurasānugatabandhanānaṃ dumapphalānaṃ patanaṃ viya, muggarābhitāḷitānaṃ mattikabhājanānaṃ bhaidau  viya, sūriyarasmisamphuṭṭhānaṃ ussāvabindūnaṃ viddhaṃsanaṃ viya ca maraṇamaiva āsan‍naṃ hauti| taināha –

‘‘ac‍cayanti ahaurattā, jīvitamuparujjhati|

āyu khīyati mac‍cānaṃ, kun‍nadīnaṃva audakaṃ|| (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.146)|

‘‘phalānamiva pak‍kānaṃ, pātau papatatau bhayaṃ|

aivaṃ jātāna mac‍cānaṃ, nic‍caṃ maraṇatau bhayaṃ||

‘‘yathāpi kumbhakārassa, kataṃ mattikabhājanaṃ|

khuddakañ‍ca mahantañ‍ca, yaṃ pak‍kaṃ yañ‍ca āmakaṃ|

sabbaṃ bhaidanapariyantaṃ, aivaṃ mac‍cāna jīvitaṃ’|| (su॰ ni॰ 581-582)|

‘‘ussāvauva tiṇaggamhi, sūriyuggamanaṃ pati|

aivamāyu manussānaṃ, mā maṃ amma nivārayā’’ti|| (jā॰ 1.11.79)|

aivaṃ ukkhittāsikau vadhakau viya saha jātiyā āgataṃ panaitaṃ maraṇaṃ gīvāya asiṃ cārayamānau sau vadhakau viya jīvitaṃ haratiyaiva, na aharitvā nivattati| tasmā saha jātiyā āgatatau, jīvitaharaṇatau ca ukkhittāsikau vadhakau viya maraṇampi pac‍cupaṭṭhitamaivāti aivaṃ vadhakapac‍cupaṭṭhānatau maraṇaṃ anussaritabbaṃ|

170. sampattivipattitauti idha sampatti nāma tāvadaiva saubhati, yāva naṃ vipatti nābhibhavati, na ca sā sampatti nāma atthi, yā vipattiṃ atik‍kamma tiṭṭhaiyya| tathā hi –

‘‘sakalaṃ  maidiniṃ bhutvā, datvā kauṭisataṃ sukhī|

aḍḍhāmalakamattassa, antai issarataṃ gatau||

‘‘tainaiva daihabandhaina, puñ‍ñamhi khayamāgatai|

maraṇābhimukhau saupi, asaukau saukamāgatau’’ti||

apica  sabbaṃ āraugyaṃ byādhipariyausānaṃ, sabbaṃ yaubbanaṃ jarāpariyausānaṃ, sabbaṃ jīvitaṃ maraṇapariyausānaṃ, sabbauyaiva laukasan‍nivāsau jātiyā anugatau, jarāya anusaṭau, byādhinā abhibhūtau, maraṇaina abbhāhatau| taināha –

‘‘yathāpi sailā vipulā, nabhaṃ āhac‍ca pabbatā|

samantā anupariyaiyyuṃ, nippauthaintā catuddisā||

‘‘aivaṃ jarā ca mac‍cu ca, adhivattanti pāṇinai|

khattiyai brāhmaṇai vaissai, suddai caṇḍālapuk‍kusai|

na kiñ‍ci parivaj‍jaiti, sabbamaivābhimaddati||

‘‘na tattha hatthīnaṃ bhūmi, na rathānaṃ na pattiyā|

na cāpi mantayuddhaina, sak‍kā jaituṃ dhanaina vā’’ti|| (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.136)|

aivaṃ jīvitasampattiyā maraṇavipattipariyausānataṃ vavatthapaintaina sampattivipattitau maraṇaṃ anussaritabbaṃ|
