171. upasaṃharaṇatauti paraihi saddhiṃ attanau upasaṃharaṇatau| tattha sattahākāraihi upasaṃharaṇatau maraṇaṃ anussaritabbaṃ, yasamahattatau, puñ‍ñamahattatau, thāmamahattatau, iddhimahattatau, pañ‍ñāmahattatau, pac‍caikabuddhatau, sammāsambuddhatauti| kathaṃ? idaṃ maraṇaṃ nāma mahāyasānaṃ mahāparivārānaṃ  sampan‍nadhanavāhanānaṃ mahāsammatamandhātumahāsudassana daḷhanaimi nimippabhutīnampi upari nirāsaṅkamaiva patitaṃ, kimaṅgaṃ pana mayhaṃ upari na patissati?

mahāyasā rājavarā, mahāsammataādayau|

taipi mac‍cuvasaṃ pattā, mādisaisu kathāva kāti||

aivaṃ tāva yasamahattatau anussaritabbaṃ|

kathaṃ puñ‍ñamahattatau?

jautikau jaṭilau uggau, maiṇḍakau atha puṇṇakau|

aitai cañ‍ñai ca yai laukai, mahāpuñ‍ñāti vissutā|

sabbai maraṇamāpan‍nā, mādisaisu kathāva kāti||

aivaṃ puñ‍ñamahattatau anussaritabbaṃ|

kathaṃ  thāmamahattatau?

vāsudaivau baladaivau, bhīmasainau yudhiṭṭhilau|

cānurau yau mahāmal‍lau, antakassa vasaṃ gatā||

aivaṃ thāmabalūpaitā, iti laukamhi vissutā|

aitaipi maraṇaṃ yātā, mādisaisu kathāva kāti||

aivaṃ thāmamahattatau anussaritabbaṃ|

kathaṃ iddhimahattatau?

pādaṅguṭṭhakamattaina, vaijayantamakampayi|

yau nāmiddhimataṃ saiṭṭhau, dutiyau aggasāvakau||

saupi mac‍cumukhaṃ ghauraṃ, migau sīhamukhaṃ viya|

paviṭṭhau saha iddhīhi, mādisaisu kathāva kāti||

aivaṃ iddhimahattatau anussaritabbaṃ|

kathaṃ pañ‍ñāmahattatau?

laukanāthaṃ ṭhapaitvāna, yai cañ‍ñai atthi pāṇinau|

pañ‍ñāya sāriputtassa, kalaṃ nāgghanti sauḷasiṃ||

aivaṃ nāma mahāpañ‍ñau, paṭhamau aggasāvakau|

maraṇassa vasaṃ pattau, mādisaisu kathāva kāti||

aivaṃ pañ‍ñāmahattatau anussaritabbaṃ|

kathaṃ pac‍caikabuddhatau? yaipi tai attanau ñāṇavīriyabalaina sabbakilaisasattunimmathanaṃ katvā pac‍caikabaudhiṃ pattā khaggavisāṇakappā sayambhunau, taipi maraṇatau na muttā, kutau panāhaṃ muc‍cissāmīti|

taṃ taṃ nimittamāgamma, vīmaṃsantā mahaisayau|

sayambhuñ‍ñāṇataijaina, yai pattā āsavakkhayaṃ||

aikacariyanivāsaina, khaggasiṅgasamūpamā|

taipi nātigatā mac‍cuṃ, mādisaisu kathāva kāti||

aivaṃ  pac‍caikabuddhatau anussaritabbaṃ|

kathaṃ sammāsambuddhatau? yaupi sau bhagavā asītianubyañ‍janapaṭimaṇḍitadvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇavicitrarūpakāyau sabbākāraparisuddhasīlakkhandhādiguṇaratanasamiddhadhammakāyau yasamahattapuñ‍ñamahattathāmamahattaiddhimahattapañ‍ñāmahattānaṃ pāraṃ gatau asamau asamasamau appaṭipuggalau arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau, saupi salilavuṭṭhinipātaina mahāaggikkhandhau viya maraṇavuṭṭhinipātaina ṭhānasau vūpasantau|

aivaṃ mahānubhāvassa, yaṃ nāmaitaṃ mahaisinau|

na bhayaina na laj‍jāya, maraṇaṃ vasamāgataṃ||

nil‍laj‍jaṃ vītasāraj‍jaṃ, sabbasattābhimaddanaṃ|

tayidaṃ mādisaṃ sattaṃ, kathaṃ nābhibhavissatīti||

aivaṃ sammāsambuddhatau anussaritabbaṃ|

tassaivaṃ yasamahattatādisampan‍naihi paraihi saddhiṃ maraṇasāmañ‍ñatāya attānaṃ upasaṃharitvā taisaṃ viya sattavisaisānaṃ mayhampi maraṇaṃ bhavissatīti anussaratau upacārappattaṃ kammaṭṭhānaṃ hautīti| aivaṃ upasaṃharaṇatau maraṇaṃ anussaritabbaṃ|

172. kāyabahusādhāraṇatauti ayaṃ kāyau bahusādhāraṇau| asītiyā tāva kimikulānaṃ sādhāraṇau, tattha chavinissitā pāṇā chaviṃ khādanti, cammanissitā cammaṃ khādanti, maṃsanissitā maṃsaṃ khādanti, nhārunissitā nhāruṃ khādanti, aṭṭhinissitā aṭṭhiṃ khādanti, miñ‍janissitā miñ‍jaṃ khādanti| tatthaiva jāyanti jīyanti mīyanti, uc‍cārapassāvaṃ karaunti| kāyauva naisaṃ sūtigharañ‍caiva gilānasālā ca susānañ‍ca vac‍cakuṭi ca passāvadauṇikā ca| svāyaṃ taisampi kimikulānaṃ pakaupaina maraṇaṃ nigacchatiyaiva| yathā ca asītiyā kimikulānaṃ, aivaṃ ajjhattikānaṃyaiva anaikasatānaṃ raugānaṃ bāhirānañ‍ca ahivicchikādīnaṃ maraṇassa pac‍cayānaṃ sādhāraṇau|

yathā  hi catumahāpathai ṭhapitai lakkhamhi sabbadisāhi āgatā sarasattitaumarapāsāṇādayau nipatanti, aivaṃ kāyaipi sabbupaddavā nipatanti| svāyaṃ taisampi upaddavānaṃ nipātaina maraṇaṃ nigacchatiyaiva| taināha bhagavā – ‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu divasai nikkhantai rattiyā paṭihitāya iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati, bahukā khau mai pac‍cayā maraṇassa, ahi vā maṃ ḍaṃsaiyya, vicchikau vā maṃ ḍaṃsaiyya, satapadī vā maṃ ḍaṃsaiyya, taina mai assa kālaṅkiriyā, sau mamassa antarāyau, upakkhalitvā vā papataiyyaṃ, bhattaṃ vā mai bhuttaṃ byāpaj‍jaiyya, pittaṃ vā mai kuppaiyya, saimhaṃ vā mai kuppaiyya, satthakā vā mai vātā kuppaiyyuṃ, taina mai assa kālaṅkiriyā, sau mamassa antarāyau’’ti| aivaṃ (a॰ ni॰ 6.20) kāyabahusādhāraṇatau maraṇaṃ anussaritabbaṃ|

173. āyudubbalatauti āyu nāmaitaṃ abalaṃ dubbalaṃ| tathā hi sattānaṃ jīvitaṃ assāsapassāsūpanibaddhañ‍caiva iriyāpathūpanibaddhañ‍ca sītuṇhūpanibaddhañ‍ca mahābhūtūpanibaddhañ‍ca āhārūpanibaddhañ‍ca| tadaitaṃ assāsapassāsānaṃ samavuttitaṃ labhamānamaiva pavattati| bahi nikkhantanāsikavātai pana antau apavisantai, paviṭṭhai vā anikkhamantai matau nāma hauti| catun‍naṃ iriyāpathānampi samavuttitaṃ labhamānamaiva pavattati| añ‍ñatarañ‍ñatarassa pana adhimattatāya āyusaṅkhārā upacchij‍janti| sītuṇhānampi samavuttitaṃ labhamānamaiva pavattati| atisītaina pana atiuṇhaina vā abhibhūtassa vipaj‍jati| mahābhūtānampi samavuttitaṃ labhamānamaiva pavattati| pathavīdhātuyā pana āpaudhātuādīnaṃ vā añ‍ñatarañ‍ñatarassa pakaupaina balasampan‍naupi puggalau patthaddhakāyau vā atisārādivasaina kilin‍napūtikāyau vā mahāḍāhaparaitau vā sambhij‍jamānasandhibandhanau vā hutvā jīvitakkhayaṃ pāpuṇāti| kabaḷīkārāhārampi yuttakālai labhantassaiva jīvitaṃ pavattati, āhāraṃ alabhamānassa pana parikkhayaṃ gacchatīti| aivaṃ āyudubbalatau maraṇaṃ anussaritabbaṃ|

174. animittatauti avavatthānatau, paricchaidābhāvatauti atthau| sattānaṃ hi –

jīvitaṃ byādhi kālau ca, daihanikkhaipanaṃ gati|

pañ‍caitai jīvalaukasmiṃ, animittā na nāyarai||

tattha  jīvitaṃ tāva ‘‘aittakamaiva jīvitabbaṃ, na itau para’’nti vavatthānābhāvatau animittaṃ| kalalakālaipi hi sattā maranti, abbudapaisighanamāsikadvaimāsataimāsacatumāsapañ‍camāsadasamāsakālaipi| kucchitau nikkhantasamayaipi| tatau paraṃ vassasatassa antaupi bahipi marantiyaiva| byādhipi ‘‘imināva byādhinā sattā maranti, nāñ‍ñainā’’ti vavatthānābhāvatau animittau| cakkhuraugaināpi hi sattā maranti, sautaraugādīnaṃ añ‍ñataraināpi| kālaupi ‘‘imasmiṃyaiva kālai maritabbaṃ, nāñ‍ñasmi’’nti aivaṃ vavatthānābhāvatau animittau| pubbaṇhaipi hi sattā maranti, majjhanhikādīnaṃ añ‍ñatarasmimpi| daihanikkhaipanampi ‘‘idhaiva mīyamānānaṃ daihaina patitabbaṃ, nāñ‍ñatrā’’ti aivaṃ vavatthānābhāvatau animittaṃ| antaugāmai jātānaṃ hi bahigāmaipi attabhāvau patati| bahigāmai jātānampi antaugāmai| tathā thalajānaṃ vā jalai, jalajānaṃ vā thalaiti anaikappakāratau vitthāraitabbaṃ| gatipi ‘‘itau cutaina idha nibbattitabba’’nti aivaṃ vavatthānābhāvatau animittā| daivalaukatau hi cutā manussaisupi nibbattanti, manussalaukatau cutā daivalaukādīnampi yattha katthaci nibbattantīti aivaṃ yantayuttagauṇau viya gatipañ‍cakai laukau samparivattatīti aivaṃ animittatau maraṇaṃ anussaritabbaṃ|

175. addhānaparicchaidatauti manussānaṃ jīvitassa nāma aitarahi parittau addhā| yau ciraṃ jīvati, sau vassasataṃ, appaṃ vā bhiyyau| taināha bhagavā – ‘‘appamidaṃ, bhikkhavai, manussānaṃ āyu, gamanīyau samparāyau, kattabbaṃ kusalaṃ, caritabbaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, natthi jātassa amaraṇaṃ| yau, bhikkhavai, ciraṃ jīvati, sau vassasataṃ, appaṃ vā bhiyyauti|

appamāyumanussānaṃ, hīḷaiyya naṃ supaurisau|

caraiyyādittasīsauva, natthi mac‍cussa nāgamauti|| (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.145)|

aparampi āha – ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, arakau nāma satthā ahausī’’ti sabbampi sattahi upamāhi alaṅkataṃ suttaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ|

aparampi āha – ‘‘yaucāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ maraṇassatiṃ bhāvaiti, ahau vatāhaṃ rattindivaṃ jīvaiyyaṃ, bhagavatau sāsanaṃ manasikaraiyyaṃ, bahuṃ vata mai kataṃ assāti| yaucāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ maraṇassatiṃ bhāvaiti, ahau vatāhaṃ divasaṃ jīvaiyyaṃ, bhagavatau sāsanaṃ manasikaraiyyaṃ, bahuṃ vata mai kataṃ assāti| yau cāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ  maraṇassatiṃ bhāvaiti, ahau vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīvaiyyaṃ, yadantaraṃ aikaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jāmi, bhagavatau sāsanaṃ manasikaraiyyaṃ, bahuṃ vata mai kataṃ assāti| yau cāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ maraṇassatiṃ bhāvaiti, ahau vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīvaiyyaṃ, yadantaraṃ cattārau pañ‍ca ālaupai saṅkhāditvā ajjhauharāmi, bhagavatau sāsanaṃ manasikaraiyyaṃ, bahuṃ vata mai kataṃ assāti| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū pamattā viharanti, dandhaṃ maraṇassatiṃ bhāvainti āsavānaṃ khayāya| yau ca khvāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ maraṇassatiṃ bhāvaiti, ahau vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīvaiyyaṃ, yadantaraṃ aikaṃ ālaupaṃ saṅkhāditvā ajjhauharāmi, bhagavatau sāsanaṃ manasikaraiyyaṃ, bahuṃ vata mai kataṃ assāti| yau cāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ maraṇassatiṃ bhāvaiti, ahau vatāhaṃ tadantaraṃ jīvaiyyaṃ, yadantaraṃ assasitvā vā passasāmi, passasitvā vā assasāmi, bhagavatau sāsanaṃ manasikaraiyyaṃ, bahuṃ vata mai kataṃ assāti| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū appamattā viharanti, tikkhaṃ maraṇassatiṃ bhāvainti āsavānaṃ khayāyā’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 6.19)| aivaṃ catupañ‍cālaupasaṅkhādanamattaṃ avissāsiyau parittau jīvitassa addhāti aivaṃ addhānaparicchaidatau maraṇaṃ anussaritabbaṃ|

176. khaṇaparittatauti paramatthatau hi atiparittau sattānaṃ jīvitakkhaṇau aikacittappavattimattauyaiva| yathā nāma rathacak‍kaṃ pavattamānampi aikainaiva naimippadaisaina pavattati, tiṭṭhamānampi aikainaiva tiṭṭhati, aivamaiva aikacittakkhaṇikaṃ sattānaṃ jīvitaṃ| tasmiṃ cittai niruddhamattai sattau niruddhauti vuc‍cati| yathāha – ‘‘atītai cittakkhaṇai jīvittha, na jīvati, na jīvissati| anāgatai cittakkhaṇai na jīvittha, na jīvati, jīvissati| pac‍cuppan‍nai cittakkhaṇai na jīvittha, jīvati, na jīvissati|

‘‘jīvitaṃ attabhāvau ca, sukhadukkhā ca kaivalā|

aikacittasamāyuttā, lahu sau vattatai khaṇau||

‘‘yai niruddhā marantassa, tiṭṭhamānassa vā idha|

sabbaipi sadisā khandhā, gatā appaṭisandhikā||

‘‘anibbattaina  na jātau, pac‍cuppan‍naina jīvati|

cittabhaṅgā matau laukau, pañ‍ñatti paramatthiyā’’ti|| (mahāni॰ 39)|

aivaṃ khaṇaparittatau maraṇaṃ anussaritabbaṃ|

177. iti imaisaṃ aṭṭhan‍naṃ ākārānaṃ añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina anussarataupi punappunaṃ manasikāravasaina cittaṃ āsaivanaṃ labhati, maraṇārammaṇā sati santiṭṭhati, nīvaraṇāni vikkhambhanti, jhānaṅgāni pātubhavanti| sabhāvadhammattā pana saṃvaijanīyattā ca ārammaṇassa appanaṃ appatvā upacārappattamaiva jhānaṃ hauti| laukuttarajjhānaṃ pana dutiyacatutthāni ca āruppajjhānāni sabhāvadhammaipi bhāvanāvisaisaina appanaṃ pāpuṇanti| visuddhibhāvanānuk‍kamavasaina hi laukuttaraṃ appanaṃ pāpuṇāti| ārammaṇātik‍kamabhāvanāvasaina āruppaṃ| appanāpattassaiva hi jhānassa ārammaṇasamatik‍kamanamattaṃ tattha hauti| idha pana tadubhayampi natthi| tasmā upacārappattamaiva jhānaṃ hauti| tadaitaṃ maraṇassatibalaina uppan‍nattā maraṇassatic‍caiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

imañ‍ca pana maraṇassatiṃ anuyuttau bhikkhu satataṃ appamattau hauti, sabbabhavaisu anabhiratisañ‍ñaṃ paṭilabhati, jīvitanikantiṃ jahāti, pāpagarahī hauti, asan‍nidhibahulau parikkhāraisu vigatamalamacchairau, anic‍casañ‍ñā cassa paricayaṃ gacchati, tadanusārainaiva dukkhasañ‍ñā anattasañ‍ñā ca upaṭṭhāti| yathā abhāvitamaraṇā sattā sahasā vāḷamigayakkhasappacauravadhakābhibhūtā viya maraṇasamayai bhayaṃ santāsaṃ sammauhaṃ āpaj‍janti, aivaṃ anāpaj‍jitvā abhayau asammūḷhau kālaṃ karauti| sacai diṭṭhaiva dhammai amataṃ nārādhaiti, kāyassa bhaidā sugatiparāyanau hauti|

tasmā havai appamādaṃ, kayirātha sumaidhasau|

aivaṃ mahānubhāvāya, maraṇassatiyā sadāti||

idaṃ maraṇassatiyaṃ vitthārakathāmukhaṃ|

kāyagatāsatikathā

178. idāni  yaṃ taṃ añ‍ñatra buddhuppādā appavattapubbaṃ sabbatitthiyānaṃ avisayabhūtaṃ taisu taisu suttantaisu ‘‘aikadhammau, bhikkhavai, bhāvitau bahulīkatau mahatau saṃvaigāya saṃvattati| mahatau atthāya saṃvattati| mahatau yaugakkhaimāya saṃvattati| mahatau satisampajañ‍ñāya saṃvattati| ñāṇadassanapaṭilābhāya saṃvattati| diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārāya saṃvattati| vij‍jāvimuttiphalasacchikiriyāya saṃvattati| katamau aikadhammau? kāyagatā sati… (a॰ ni॰ 1.563 ādayau)| amataṃ tai, bhikkhavai, paribhuñ‍janti, yai kāyagatāsatiṃ paribhuñ‍janti| amataṃ tai, bhikkhavai, na paribhuñ‍janti, yai kāyagatāsatiṃ na paribhuñ‍janti| amataṃ taisaṃ, bhikkhavai, paribhuttaṃ… aparibhuttaṃ… parihīnaṃ… aparihīnaṃ… viraddhaṃ… aviraddhaṃ, yaisaṃ kāyagatāsati āraddhāti (a॰ ni॰ 1.603) aivaṃ bhagavatā anaikaihi ākāraihi pasaṃsitvā ‘‘kathaṃ bhāvitā, bhikkhavai, kāyagatāsati kathaṃ bahulīkatā mahapphalā hauti mahānisaṃsā? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā’’tiādinā (ma॰ ni॰ 3.154) nayaina ānāpānapabbaṃ, iriyāpathapabbaṃ, catusampajañ‍ñapabbaṃ, paṭik‍kūlamanasikārapabbaṃ, dhātumanasikārapabbaṃ, navasivathikapabbānīti imaisaṃ cuddasan‍naṃ pabbānaṃ vasaina kāyagatāsatikammaṭṭhānaṃ niddiṭṭhaṃ, tassa bhāvanāniddaisau anuppattau|

tattha yasmā iriyāpathapabbaṃ catusampajañ‍ñapabbaṃ dhātumanasikārapabbanti imāni tīṇi vipassanāvasaina vuttāni| nava sivathikapabbāni vipassanāñāṇaisuyaiva ādīnavānupassanāvasaina vuttāni| yāpi caittha uddhumātakādīsu samādhibhāvanā ijjhaiyya, sā asubhaniddaisai pakāsitāyaiva| ānāpānapabbaṃ pana paṭik‍kūlamanasikārapabbañ‍ca imānaivaittha dvai samādhivasaina vuttāni| taisu ānāpānapabbaṃ ānāpānassativasaina visuṃ kammaṭṭhānaṃyaiva| yaṃ panaitaṃ ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ uddhaṃ pādatalā adhau kaisamatthakā tacapariyantaṃ pūraṃ nānappakārassa asucinau pac‍cavaikkhati| atthi imasmiṃ kāyai kaisā laumā…pai॰… mutta’’nti (ma॰ ni॰ 3.154) aivaṃ matthaluṅgaṃ aṭṭhimiñ‍jaina saṅgahaitvā paṭik‍kūlamanasikāravasaina daisitaṃ dvattiṃsākārakammaṭṭhānaṃ, idamidha kāyagatāsatīti adhippaitaṃ|

179. tatthāyaṃ  pāḷivaṇṇanāpubbaṅgamau bhāvanāniddaisau| imamaiva kāyanti imaṃ catumahābhūtikaṃ  pūtikāyaṃ| uddhaṃ pādatalāti pādatalatau upari| adhau kaisamatthakāti kaisaggatau haiṭṭhā| tacapariyantanti tiriyaṃ tacaparicchin‍naṃ| pūraṃ nānappakārassa asucinau pac‍cavaikkhatīti nānappakārakaisādiasucibharitau ayaṃ kāyauti passati| kathaṃ? atthi imasmiṃ kāyai kaisā…pai॰… muttanti|

tattha atthīti saṃvij‍janti| imasminti yvāyaṃ uddhaṃ pādatalā adhau kaisamatthakā tacapariyantau pūrau nānappakārassa asucinauti vuc‍cati, tasmiṃ| kāyaiti sarīrai| sarīraṃ hi asucisañ‍cayatau kucchitānaṃ kaisādīnañ‍caiva cakkhuraugādīnañ‍ca raugasatānaṃ āyabhūtatau kāyauti vuc‍cati| kaisā laumāti aitai kaisādayau dvattiṃsākārā| tattha atthi imasmiṃ kāyai kaisā, atthi imasmiṃ kāyai laumāti aivaṃ sambandhau vaiditabbau|

imasmiṃ hi pādatalā paṭṭhāya upari, kaisamatthakā paṭṭhāya haiṭṭhā, tacatau paṭṭhāya paritauti aittakai byāmamattai kaḷaivarai sabbākārainapi vicinantau na kauci kiñ‍ci muttaṃ vā maṇiṃ vā vaiḷuriyaṃ vā agaruṃ vā kuṅkumaṃ vā kappūraṃ vā vāsacuṇṇādiṃ vā aṇumattampi sucibhāvaṃ passati, atha khau paramaduggandhajaigucchaṃ asirikadassanaṃ nānappakāraṃ kaisalaumādibhaidaṃ asuciṃyaiva passati| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘atthi imasmiṃ kāyai kaisā laumā…pai॰… mutta’’nti| ayamaittha padasambandhatau vaṇṇanā|

180. imaṃ pana kammaṭṭhānaṃ bhāvaitukāmaina ādikammikaina kulaputtaina vuttappakāraṃ kalyāṇamittaṃ upasaṅkamitvā idaṃ kammaṭṭhānaṃ gahaitabbaṃ| taināpissa kammaṭṭhānaṃ kathaintaina sattadhā uggahakausal‍laṃ dasadhā ca manasikārakausal‍laṃ ācikkhitabbaṃ| tattha vacasā manasā vaṇṇatau saṇṭhānatau disatau aukāsatau paricchaidatauti aivaṃ sattadhā uggahakausal‍laṃ ācikkhitabbaṃ|

imasmiṃ hi paṭik‍kūlamanasikārakammaṭṭhānai yaupi tipiṭakau hauti, taināpi manasikārakālai paṭhamaṃ vācāya sajjhāyau kātabbau| aikac‍cassa hi sajjhāyaṃ karauntassaiva kammaṭṭhānaṃ pākaṭaṃ hauti malayavāsī mahādaivatthairassa santikai uggahitakammaṭṭhānānaṃ dvin‍naṃ thairānaṃ viya| thairau  kira taihi kammaṭṭhānaṃ yācitau cattārau māsai imaṃyaiva sajjhāyaṃ karauthāti dvattiṃsākārapāḷiṃ adāsi| tai kiñ‍cāpi naisaṃ dvai tayau nikāyā paguṇā, padakkhiṇaggāhitāya  pana cattārau māsai dvattiṃsākāraṃ sajjhāyantāva sautāpan‍nā ahaisuṃ| tasmā kammaṭṭhānaṃ kathaintaina ācariyaina antaivāsikau vattabbau ‘‘paṭhamaṃ tāva vācāya sajjhāyaṃ karauhī’’ti|

karauntaina ca tacapañ‍cakādīni paricchinditvā anulaumapaṭilaumavasaina sajjhāyau kātabbau| kaisā laumā nakhā dantā tacauti hi vatvā puna paṭilaumatau tacau dantā nakhā laumā kaisāti vattabbaṃ|

tadanantaraṃ vak‍kapañ‍cakai maṃsaṃ nhāru aṭṭhi aṭṭhimiñ‍jaṃ vak‍kanti vatvā puna paṭilaumatau vak‍kaṃ aṭṭhimiñ‍jaṃ aṭṭhi nhāru maṃsaṃ, tacau dantā nakhā laumā kaisāti vattabbaṃ|

tatau papphāsapañ‍cakai hadayaṃ yakanaṃ kilaumakaṃ pihakaṃ papphāsanti vatvā puna paṭilaumatau papphāsaṃ pihakaṃ kilaumakaṃ yakanaṃ hadayaṃ, vak‍kaṃ aṭṭhimiñ‍jaṃ aṭṭhi nhāru maṃsaṃ, tacau dantā nakhā laumā kaisāti vattabbaṃ|

tatau matthaluṅgapañ‍cakai antaṃ antaguṇaṃ udariyaṃ karīsaṃ matthaluṅganti vatvā puna paṭilaumatau matthaluṅgaṃ karīsaṃ udariyaṃ antaguṇaṃ antaṃ, papphāsaṃ pihakaṃ kilaumakaṃ yakanaṃ hadayaṃ, vak‍kaṃ aṭṭhimiñ‍jaṃ aṭṭhi nhāru maṃsaṃ, tacau dantā nakhā laumā kaisāti vattabbaṃ|

tatau maidachak‍kai pittaṃ saimhaṃ pubbau lauhitaṃ saidau maidauti vatvā puna paṭilaumatau maidau saidau lauhitaṃ pubbau saimhaṃ pittaṃ, matthaluṅgaṃ karīsaṃ udariyaṃ antaguṇaṃ antaṃ, papphāsaṃ pihakaṃ kilaumakaṃ yakanaṃ hadayaṃ, vak‍kaṃ aṭṭhimiñ‍jaṃ aṭṭhi nhāru maṃsaṃ, tacau dantā nakhā laumā kaisāti vattabbaṃ|

tatau muttachak‍kai assu vasā khaiḷau siṅghāṇikā lasikā muttanti vatvā puna paṭilaumatau muttaṃ lasikā siṅghāṇikā khaiḷau vasā assu, maidau saidau lauhitaṃ pubbau saimhaṃ pittaṃ, matthaluṅgaṃ karīsaṃ udariyaṃ antaguṇaṃ antaṃ, papphāsaṃ pihakaṃ kilaumakaṃ yakanaṃ hadayaṃ, vak‍kaṃ aṭṭhimiñ‍jaṃ aṭṭhi nhāru maṃsaṃ, tacau dantā nakhā laumā kaisāti vattabbaṃ|

aivaṃ  kālasataṃ kālasahassaṃ kālasatasahassampi vācāya sajjhāyau kātabbau| vacasā sajjhāyaina hi kammaṭṭhānatanti paguṇā hauti, na itau citau ca cittaṃ vidhāvati| kauṭṭhāsā pākaṭā haunti, hatthasaṅkhalikā viya vatipādapanti viya ca khāyanti|

yathā pana vacasā, tathaiva manasāpi sajjhāyau kātabbau| vacasā sajjhāyau hi manasā sajjhāyassa pac‍cayau hauti| manasā sajjhāyau lakkhaṇapaṭivaidhassa pac‍cayau hauti|

vaṇṇatauti kaisādīnaṃ vaṇṇau vavatthapaitabbau|

saṇṭhānatauti taisañ‍ñaiva saṇṭhānaṃ vavatthapaitabbaṃ|

disatauti imasmiṃ hi sarīrai nābhitau uddhaṃ uparimadisā, adhau haiṭṭhimadisā, tasmā ayaṃ kauṭṭhāsau imissā nāma disāyāti disā vavatthapaitabbā|

aukāsatauti ayaṃ kauṭṭhāsau imasmiṃ nāma aukāsai patiṭṭhitauti aivaṃ tassa tassa aukāsau vavatthapaitabbau|

paricchaidatauti sabhāgaparicchaidau visabhāgaparicchaidauti dvai paricchaidā| tattha ayaṃ kauṭṭhāsau haiṭṭhā ca upari ca tiriyañ‍ca iminā nāma paricchin‍nauti aivaṃ sabhāgaparicchaidau vaiditabbau| kaisā na laumā, laumāpi na kaisāti aivaṃ amissakatāvasaina visabhāgaparicchaidau vaiditabbau|

aivaṃ sattadhā uggahakausal‍laṃ ācikkhantaina pana idaṃ kammaṭṭhānaṃ asukasmiṃ suttai paṭik‍kūlavasaina kathitaṃ, asukasmiṃ dhātuvasaināti ñatvā ācikkhitabbaṃ| idañhi mahāsatipaṭṭhānai (dī॰ ni॰ 2.377) paṭik‍kūlavasainaiva kathitaṃ| mahāhatthipadaupama(ma॰ ni॰ 1.300 ādayau) mahārāhulauvāda(ma॰ ni॰ 2.113 ādayau) dhātuvibhaṅgaisu(ma॰ ni॰ 3.342 ādayau) dhātuvasaina kathitaṃ| kāyagatāsatisuttai (ma॰ ni॰ 3.153) pana yassa vaṇṇatau upaṭṭhāti, taṃ sandhāya cattāri jhānāni vibhattāni| tattha dhātuvasaina kathitaṃ vipassanākammaṭṭhānaṃ  hauti| paṭik‍kūlavasaina kathitaṃ samathakammaṭṭhānaṃ| tadaitaṃ idha samathakammaṭṭhānamaivāti|

181. aivaṃ  sattadhā uggahakausal‍laṃ ācikkhitvā anupubbatau, nātisīghatau, nātisaṇikatau, vikkhaipapaṭibāhanatau, paṇṇattisamatik‍kamanatau, anupubbamuñ‍canatau, appanātau, tayau ca suttantāti aivaṃ dasadhā manasikārakausal‍laṃ ācikkhitabbaṃ| tattha anupubbatauti idañhi sajjhāyakaraṇatau paṭṭhāya anupaṭipāṭiyā manasikātabbaṃ, na aikantarikāya| aikantarikāya hi manasikarauntau yathā nāma akusalau purisau dvattiṃsapadaṃ nissaiṇiṃ aikantarikāya ārauhantau kilantakāyau patati, na ārauhanaṃ sampādaiti, aivamaiva bhāvanāsampattivasaina adhigantabbassa assādassa anadhigamā kilantacittau patati, na bhāvanaṃ sampādaiti|

anupubbatau manasikarauntaināpi ca nātisīghatau manasikātabbaṃ| atisīghatau manasikarautau hi yathā nāma tiyaujanamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jitvā auk‍kamanavissaj‍janaṃ asal‍lakkhaitvā sīghaina javaina satakkhattumpi gamanāgamanaṃ karautau purisassa kiñ‍cāpi addhānaṃ parikkhayaṃ gacchati, atha khau pucchitvāva gantabbaṃ hauti, aivamaiva kaivalaṃ kammaṭṭhānaṃ pariyausānaṃ pāpuṇāti, avibhūtaṃ pana hauti, na visaisaṃ āvahati, tasmā nātisīghatau manasikātabbaṃ|

yathā ca nātisīghatau, aivaṃ nātisaṇikataupi| atisaṇikatau manasikarautau hi yathā nāma tadahaiva tiyaujanamaggaṃ gantukāmassa purisassa antarāmaggai rukkhapabbatataḷākādīsu vilambamānassa maggau parikkhayaṃ na gacchati, dvīhatīhaina pariyausāpaitabbau hauti, aivamaiva kammaṭṭhānaṃ pariyausānaṃ na gacchati, visaisādhigamassa pac‍cayau na hauti|

vikkhaipapaṭibāhanatauti kammaṭṭhānaṃ vissaj‍jaitvā bahiddhā puthuttārammaṇai caitasau vikkhaipau paṭibāhitabbau| appaṭibāhatau hi yathā nāma aikapadikaṃ papātamaggaṃ paṭipan‍nassa purisassa ak‍kamanapadaṃ asal‍lakkhaitvā itau citau ca vilaukayatau padavārau virajjhati, tatau satapaurisai papātai patitabbaṃ hauti, aivamaiva bahiddhā vikkhaipai sati kammaṭṭhānaṃ parihāyati paridhaṃsati| tasmā vikkhaipapaṭibāhanatau manasikātabbaṃ|

paṇṇattisamatik‍kamanatauti  yāyaṃ kaisā laumātiādikā paṇṇatti, taṃ atik‍kamitvā paṭik‍kūlanti cittaṃ ṭhapaitabbaṃ| yathā hi udakadul‍labhakālai manussā arañ‍ñai udapānaṃ disvā tattha tālapaṇṇādikaṃ kiñ‍cidaiva sañ‍ñāṇaṃ bandhitvā taina sañ‍ñāṇaina āgantvā nhāyanti caiva pivanti ca| yadā pana naisaṃ abhiṇhasañ‍cāraina āgatāgatapadaṃ pākaṭaṃ hauti, tadā sañ‍ñāṇaina kic‍caṃ na hauti, icchiticchitakkhaṇai gantvā nhāyanti caiva pivanti ca, aivamaiva pubbabhāgai kaisā laumātipaṇṇattivasaina manasikarautau paṭik‍kūlabhāvau pākaṭau hauti| atha kaisā laumātipaṇṇattiṃ samatik‍kamitvā paṭik‍kūlabhāvaiyaiva cittaṃ ṭhapaitabbaṃ|

anupubbamuñ‍canatauti yau yau kauṭṭhāsau na upaṭṭhāti, taṃ taṃ muñ‍cantaina anupubbamuñ‍canatau manasikātabbaṃ| ādikammikassa hi kaisāti manasikarautau manasikārau gantvā muttanti imaṃ pariyausānakauṭṭhāsamaiva āhac‍ca tiṭṭhati| muttanti ca manasikarautau manasikārau gantvā kaisāti imaṃ ādikauṭṭhāsamaiva āhac‍ca tiṭṭhati| athassa manasikarautau manasikarautau kaici kauṭṭhāsā upaṭṭhahanti, kaici na upaṭṭhahanti| taina yai yai upaṭṭhahanti, taisu taisu tāva kammaṃ kātabbaṃ| yāva dvīsu upaṭṭhitaisu taisampi aikau suṭṭhutaraṃ upaṭṭhahati, aivaṃ upaṭṭhitaṃ pana tamaiva punappunaṃ manasikarauntaina appanā uppādaitabbā|

tatrāyaṃ upamā – yathā hi dvattiṃsatālakai tālavanai vasantaṃ mak‍kaṭaṃ gahaitukāmau luddau ādimhi ṭhitatālassa paṇṇaṃ saraina vijjhitvā uk‍kuṭṭhiṃ karaiyya, atha khau sau mak‍kaṭau paṭipāṭiyā tasmiṃ tasmiṃ tālai patitvā pariyantatālamaiva gacchaiyya, tatthapi gantvā luddaina tathaiva katai puna tainaiva nayaina āditālaṃ āgacchaiyya, sau aivaṃ punappunaṃ paripātiyamānau uk‍kuṭṭhuk‍kuṭṭhiṭṭhānaiyaiva uṭṭhahitvā anuk‍kamaina aikasmiṃ tālai nipatitvā tassa vaimajjhai makuḷatālapaṇṇasūciṃ daḷhaṃ gahaitvā vijjhiyamānaupi na uṭṭhahaiyya, aivaṃsampadamidaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ|

tatridaṃ aupammasaṃsandanaṃ – yathā hi tālavanai dvattiṃsatālā, aivaṃ imasmiṃ kāyai dvattiṃsakauṭṭhāsā| mak‍kaṭau viya cittaṃ| luddau viya yaugāvacarau| mak‍kaṭassa dvattiṃsatālakai tālavanai nivāsau viya yauginau  cittassa dvattiṃsakauṭṭhāsakai kāyai ārammaṇavasaina anusañ‍caraṇaṃ| luddaina ādimhi ṭhitatālassa paṇṇaṃ saraina vijjhitvā uk‍kuṭṭhiyā katāya mak‍kaṭassa tasmiṃ tasmiṃ tālai patitvā pariyantatālagamanaṃ viya yauginau kaisāti manasikārai āraddhai  paṭipāṭiyā gantvā pariyausānakauṭṭhāsaiyaiva cittassa saṇṭhānaṃ| puna pac‍cāgamanaipi aisaiva nayau| punappunaṃ paripātiyamānassa mak‍kaṭassa uk‍kuṭṭhuk‍kuṭṭhiṭṭhānai uṭṭhānaṃ viya punappunaṃ manasikarautau kaisuci kaisuci upaṭṭhitaisu anupaṭṭhahantai vissaj‍jaitvā upaṭṭhitaisu parikammakaraṇaṃ| anuk‍kamaina aikasmiṃ tālai nipatitvā tassa majjhai makuḷatālapaṇṇasūciṃ daḷhaṃ gahaitvā vijjhiyamānassapi anuṭṭhānaṃ viya avasānai dvīsu upaṭṭhitaisu yau suṭṭhutaraṃ upaṭṭhāti, tamaiva punappunaṃ manasikaritvā appanāya uppādanaṃ|

aparāpi upamā – yathā nāma piṇḍapātikau bhikkhu dvattiṃsakulaṃ gāmaṃ upanissāya vasantau paṭhamagaihaiyaiva dvai bhikkhā labhitvā paratau aikaṃ vissaj‍jaiyya| punadivasai tissau labhitvā paratau dvai vissaj‍jaiyya| tatiyadivasai ādimhiyaiva pattapūraṃ labhitvā āsanasālaṃ gantvā paribhuñ‍jaiyya| aivaṃsampadamidaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| dvattiṃsakulagāmau viya hi dvattiṃsākārau| piṇḍapātikau viya yaugāvacarau| tassa taṃ gāmaṃ upanissāya vāsau viya yauginau dvattiṃsākārai parikammakaraṇaṃ| paṭhamagaihai dvai bhikkhā labhitvā paratau aikissā vissaj‍janaṃ viya dutiyadivasai tissau labhitvā paratau dvin‍naṃ vissaj‍janaṃ viya ca manasikarautau manasikarautau anupaṭṭhahantai vissaj‍jaitvā upaṭṭhitaisu yāva kauṭṭhāsadvayai parikammakaraṇaṃ| tatiyadivasai ādimhiyaiva pattapūraṃ labhitvā āsanasālāyaṃ nisīditvā paribhaugau viya dvīsu yau suṭṭhutaraṃ upaṭṭhāti, tamaiva punappunaṃ manasikaritvā appanāya uppādanaṃ|

appanātauti appanākauṭṭhāsatau kaisādīsu aikaikasmiṃ kauṭṭhāsai appanā hautīti vaiditabbāti ayamaivaittha adhippāyau|

tayau ca suttantāti adhicittaṃ, sītibhāvau, baujjhaṅgakausal‍lanti imai tayau suttantā vīriyasamādhiyaujanatthaṃ vaiditabbāti ayamaittha adhippāyau| tattha –

‘‘adhicittamanuyuttaina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā tīṇi nimittāni kālainakālaṃ manasikātabbāni| kālainakālaṃ samādhinimittaṃ manasikātabbaṃ| kālainakālaṃ paggahanimittaṃ manasikātabbaṃ| kālainakālaṃ upaikkhānimittaṃ manasikātabbaṃ| sacai, bhikkhavai, adhicittamanuyuttau bhikkhu aikantaṃ samādhinimittañ‍ñaiva manasikaraiyya, ṭhānaṃ taṃ  cittaṃ kausaj‍jāya saṃvattaiyya| sacai, bhikkhavai, adhicittamanuyuttau bhikkhu aikantaṃ paggahanimittañ‍ñaiva manasikaraiyya, ṭhānaṃ taṃ cittaṃ uddhac‍cāya saṃvattaiyya| sacai, bhikkhavai, adhicittamanuyuttau bhikkhu aikantaṃ upaikkhānimittañ‍ñaiva manasikaraiyya, ṭhānaṃ taṃ cittaṃ na sammā samādhiyaiyya āsavānaṃ khayāya| yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, adhicittamanuyuttau bhikkhu kālainakālaṃ samādhinimittaṃ paggahanimittaṃ upaikkhānimittaṃ manasikarauti, taṃ hauti cittaṃ muduñ‍ca kammañ‍ñañ‍ca pabhassarañ‍ca, na ca pabhaṅgu, sammā samādhiyati āsavānaṃ khayāya|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, suvaṇṇakārau vā suvaṇṇakārantaivāsī vā uk‍kaṃ bandhati, uk‍kaṃ bandhitvā uk‍kāmukhaṃ ālimpaiti, uk‍kāmukhaṃ ālimpaitvā saṇḍāsaina jātarūpaṃ gahaitvā uk‍kāmukhai pakkhipitvā kālainakālaṃ abhidhamati, kālainakālaṃ udakaina paripphausaiti, kālainakālaṃ ajjhupaikkhati| sacai, bhikkhavai, suvaṇṇakārau vā suvaṇṇakārantaivāsī vā taṃ jātarūpaṃ aikantaṃ abhidhamaiyya, ṭhānaṃ taṃ jātarūpaṃ ḍahaiyya| sacai, bhikkhavai, suvaṇṇakārau vā suvaṇṇakārantaivāsī vā taṃ jātarūpaṃ aikantaṃ udakaina paripphausaiyya, ṭhānaṃ taṃ jātarūpaṃ nibbāyaiyya| sacai, bhikkhavai, suvaṇṇakārau vā suvaṇṇakārantaivāsī vā taṃ jātarūpaṃ aikantaṃ ajjhupaikkhaiyya, ṭhānaṃ taṃ jātarūpaṃ na sammā paripākaṃ gacchaiyya| yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, suvaṇṇakārau vā suvaṇṇakārantaivāsī vā taṃ jātarūpaṃ kālainakālaṃ abhidhamati, kālainakālaṃ udakaina paripphausaiti, kālainakālaṃ ajjhupaikkhati, taṃ hauti jātarūpaṃ muduñ‍ca kammañ‍ñañ‍ca pabhassarañ‍ca, na ca pabhaṅgu, sammā upaiti kammāya| yassā yassā ca piḷandhanavikatiyā ākaṅkhati yadi paṭikāya yadi kuṇḍalāya yadi  gīvaiyyāya yadi suvaṇṇamālāya, tañ‍cassa atthaṃ anubhauti|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, adhicittamanuyuttaina…pai॰… samādhiyati āsavānaṃ khayāya| yassa yassa ca abhiñ‍ñā sacchi karaṇīyassa dhammassa cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti abhiñ‍ñā sacchi kiriyāya, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati sati āyatanai’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 3.103)|

idaṃ  suttaṃ adhicittanti vaiditabbaṃ|

‘‘chahi, bhikkhavai, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bhabbau anuttaraṃ sītibhāvaṃ sacchikātuṃ| katamaihi chahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ niggahaitabbaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ niggaṇhāti| yasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ paggahaitabbaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ paggaṇhāti| yasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ sampahaṃsitabbaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ sampahaṃsaiti| yasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ ajjhupaikkhitabbaṃ, tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ ajjhupaikkhati| paṇītādhimuttikau ca hauti nibbānābhiratau| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, chahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bhabbau anuttaraṃ sītibhāvaṃ sacchikātu’’nti (a॰ ni॰ 6.85)|

idaṃ suttaṃ anuttaraṃ sītibhāvauti vaiditabbaṃ|

baujjhaṅgakausal‍laṃ ‘‘pana aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yasmiṃ samayai līnaṃ cittaṃ hauti, akālau tasmiṃ samayai passaddhisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāyā’’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.234) appanākausal‍lakathāyaṃ dassitamaiva|

iti idaṃ sattavidhaṃ uggahakausal‍laṃ suggahitaṃ katvā idañ‍ca dasavidhaṃ manasikārakausal‍laṃ suṭṭhu vavatthapaitvā taina yauginā ubhayakausal‍lavasaina kammaṭṭhānaṃ sādhukaṃ uggahaitabbaṃ| sacai panassa ācariyaina saddhiṃ aikavihāraiyaiva phāsu hauti, aivaṃ vitthāraina akathāpaitvā kammaṭṭhānaṃ suṭṭhu vavatthapaitvā kammaṭṭhānaṃ anuyuj‍jantaina visaisaṃ labhitvā uparūpari kathāpaitabbaṃ| añ‍ñattha vasitukāmaina yathāvuttaina vidhinā vitthāratau kathāpaitvā punappunaṃ parivattaitvā sabbaṃ gaṇṭhiṭṭhānaṃ chinditvā pathavīkasiṇaniddaisai vuttanayainaiva ananurūpaṃ saināsanaṃ pahāya anurūpai  vihārai vasantaina khuddakapalibaudhupacchaidaṃ katvā paṭik‍kūlamanasikārai parikammaṃ kātabbaṃ|

karauntaina pana kaisaisu tāva nimittaṃ gahaitabbaṃ| kathaṃ? aikaṃ vā dvai vā kaisai luñ‍citvā hatthatalai ṭhapaitvā vaṇṇau tāva vavatthapaitabbau| chin‍naṭṭhānaipi kaisai aulaukaituṃ vaṭṭati| udakapattai vā yāgupattai vā aulaukaitumpi vaṭṭatiyaiva| kāḷakakālai disvā kāḷakāti manasikātabbā| saitakālai saitāti| missakakālai pana ussadavasaina manasikātabbā haunti| yathā ca kaisaisu, aivaṃ sakalaipi tacapañ‍cakai disvāva nimittaṃ gahaitabbaṃ|

kauṭṭhāsavavatthāpanakathā

182. aivaṃ nimittaṃ gahaitvā sabbakauṭṭhāsai vaṇṇasaṇṭhānadisaukāsaparicchaidavasaina vavatthapaitvā vaṇṇasaṇṭhānagandhaāsayaukāsavasaina pañ‍cadhā paṭik‍kūlatau vavatthapaitabbā|

tatrāyaṃ sabbakauṭṭhāsaisu anupubbakathā| kaisā tāva pakativaṇṇaina kāḷakā addāriṭṭhakavaṇṇā| saṇṭhānatau dīghavaṭṭalikā tulādaṇḍasaṇṭhānā| disatau uparimadisāya jātā| aukāsatau ubhausu passaisu kaṇṇacūḷikāhi, puratau nalāṭantaina, pacchatau galavāṭakaina paricchin‍nā| sīsakaṭāhavaiṭhanaṃ al‍lacammaṃ kaisānaṃ aukāsau| paricchaidatau kaisā sīsavaiṭhanacammai vīhaggamattaṃ pavisitvā patiṭṭhitaina haiṭṭhā attanau mūlatalaina, upari ākāsaina, tiriyaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaina paricchin‍nā, dvai kaisā aikatau natthīti ayaṃ sabhāgaparicchaidau| kaisā na laumā, laumā na kaisāti aivaṃ avasaisaaikatiṃsakauṭṭhāsaihi amissīkatā kaisā nāma pāṭiyaik‍kau aikakauṭṭhāsauti ayaṃ visabhāgaparicchaidau| idaṃ kaisānaṃ vaṇṇāditau vavatthāpanaṃ|

183. idaṃ pana naisaṃ vaṇṇādivasaina pañ‍cadhā paṭik‍kūlatau vavatthāpanaṃ| kaisā nāmaitai vaṇṇataupi paṭik‍kūlā| saṇṭhānataupi gandhataupi āsayataupi aukāsataupi paṭik‍kūlā|

manuñ‍ñaipi  hi yāgupattai vā bhattapattai vā kaisavaṇṇaṃ kiñ‍ci disvā kaisamissakamidaṃ haratha nanti jigucchanti| aivaṃ kaisā vaṇṇatau paṭik‍kūlā| rattiṃ bhuñ‍jantāpi kaisasaṇṭhānaṃ ak‍kavākaṃ vā makacivākaṃ vā chupitvāpi tathaiva jigucchanti| aivaṃ saṇṭhānatau paṭik‍kūlā|

tailamakkhanapupphadhūpādi saṅkhāravirahitānañ‍ca kaisānaṃ gandhau paramajaigucchau hauti| tatau jaigucchatarau aggimhi pakkhittānaṃ| kaisā hi vaṇṇasaṇṭhānatau appaṭik‍kūlāpi siyuṃ, gandhaina pana paṭik‍kūlāyaiva| yathā hi daharassa kumārassa vac‍caṃ vaṇṇatau haliddivaṇṇaṃ, saṇṭhānataupi haliddipiṇḍasaṇṭhānaṃ| saṅkāraṭṭhānai chaḍḍitañ‍ca uddhumātakakāḷasunakhasarīraṃ vaṇṇatau tālapak‍kavaṇṇaṃ| saṇṭhānatau vaṭṭaitvā vissaṭṭhamudiṅgasaṇṭhānaṃ| dāṭhāpissa sumanamakuḷasadisāti ubhayampi vaṇṇasaṇṭhānatau siyā appaṭik‍kūlaṃ gandhaina pana paṭik‍kūlamaiva| aivaṃ kaisāpi siyuṃ vaṇṇasaṇṭhānatau appaṭik‍kūlā gandhaina pana paṭik‍kūlāyaivāti|

yathā pana asuciṭṭhānai gāmanissandaina jātāni sūpaiyyapaṇṇāni nāgarikamanussānaṃ jaigucchāni haunti aparibhaugāni, aivaṃ kaisāpi pubbalauhitamuttakarīsapittasaimhādinissandaina jātattā jaigucchāti idaṃ naisaṃ āsayatau pāṭik‍kulyaṃ|

imai ca kaisā nāma gūtharāsimhi uṭṭhitakaṇṇikaṃ viya aikatiṃsakauṭṭhāsarāsimhi jātā| tai susānasaṅkāraṭṭhānādīsu jātasākaṃ viya parikkhādīsu jātakamalakuvalayādipupphaṃ viya ca asuciṭṭhānai jātattā paramajaigucchāti idaṃ naisaṃ aukāsatau pāṭik‍kulyaṃ|

yathā ca kaisānaṃ, aivaṃ sabbakauṭṭhāsānaṃ vaṇṇasaṇṭhānagandhāsayaukāsavasaina pañ‍cadhā paṭik‍kūlatā vaiditabbā| vaṇṇasaṇṭhānadisaukāsaparicchaidavasaina pana sabbaipi visuṃ visuṃ vavatthapaitabbā|

184. tattha laumā tāva pakativaṇṇatau na kaisā viya asambhin‍nakāḷakā, kāḷapiṅgalā pana haunti| saṇṭhānatau aunataggā tālamūlasaṇṭhānā| disatau dvīsu disāsu jātā| aukāsatau ṭhapaitvā kaisānaṃ patiṭṭhitaukāsañ‍ca hatthapādatalāni ca yaibhuyyaina avasaisasarīravaiṭhanacammai jātā| paricchaidatau sarīravaiṭhanacammai likhāmattaṃ pavisitvā  patiṭṭhitaina haiṭṭhā attanau mūlatalaina, upari ākāsaina, tiriyaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaina paricchin‍nā, dvai laumā aikatau natthi, ayaṃ naisaṃ sabhāgaparicchaidau| visabhāgaparicchaidau pana kaisasadisauyaiva|

185. nakhāti vīsatiyā nakhapattānaṃ nāmaṃ| tai sabbaipi vaṇṇatau saitā| saṇṭhānatau macchasakalikasaṇṭhānā| disatau pādanakhā haiṭṭhimadisāya, hatthanakhā uparimadisāyāti dvīsu disāsu jātā| aukāsatau aṅgulīnaṃ aggapiṭṭhaisu patiṭṭhitā| paricchaidatau dvīsu disāsu aṅgulikauṭimaṃsaihi, antau aṅgulipiṭṭhimaṃsaina, bahi caiva aggai ca ākāsaina, tiriyaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaina  paricchin‍nā, dvai nakhā aikatau natthi, ayaṃ naisaṃ sabhāgaparicchaidau| visabhāgaparicchaidau pana kaisasadisauyaiva|

186. dantāti paripuṇṇadantassa dvattiṃsa dantaṭṭhikāni| taipi vaṇṇatau saitā| saṇṭhānatau anaikasaṇṭhānā| taisaṃ hi haiṭṭhimāya tāva dantapāḷiyā majjhai cattārau dantā mattikāpiṇḍai paṭipāṭiyā ṭhapitaalābubījasaṇṭhānā| taisaṃ ubhausu passaisu aikaikau aikamūlakau aikakauṭikau mal‍likamakuḷasaṇṭhānau| tatau aikaikau dvimūlakau dvikauṭikau yānakaupatthambhinisaṇṭhānau| tatau dvai dvai timūlā tikauṭikā| tatau dvai dvai catumūlā catukauṭikāti| uparimapāḷiyāpi aisaiva nayau| disatau uparimadisāya jātā| aukāsatau dvīsu hanukaṭṭhikaisu patiṭṭhitā| paricchaidatau haiṭṭhā hanukaṭṭhikai patiṭṭhitaina attanau mūlatalaina, upari ākāsaina, tiriyaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaina paricchin‍nā, dvai dantā aikatau natthi, ayaṃ naisaṃ sabhāgaparicchaidau| visabhāgaparicchaidau pana kaisasadisauyaiva|

187. tacauti sakalasarīraṃ vaiṭhaitvā ṭhitacammaṃ| tassa upari kāḷasāmapītādivaṇṇā chavi nāma yā sakalasarīrataupi saṅkaḍḍhiyamānā badaraṭṭhimattā hauti| tacau pana vaṇṇatau saitauyaiva| sau cassa saitabhāvau aggijālābhighātapaharaṇappahārādīhi viddhaṃsitāya chaviyā pākaṭau hauti| saṇṭhānatau sarīrasaṇṭhānauva hauti| ayamaittha saṅkhaipau|

vitthāratau  pana pādaṅgulittacau kausakārakakausasaṇṭhānau| piṭṭhipādattacau puṭabandhaupāhanasaṇṭhānau| jaṅghattacau bhattapuṭakatālapaṇṇasaṇṭhānau| ūruttacau taṇḍulabharitadīghatthavikasaṇṭhānau| ānisadattacau udakapūritapaṭaparissāvanasaṇṭhānau| piṭṭhittacau phalakaunaddhacammasaṇṭhānau| kucchittacau vīṇādauṇikaunaddhacammasaṇṭhānau| urattacau yaibhuyyaina caturassasaṇṭhānau| ubhayabāhuttacau tūṇiraunaddhacammasaṇṭhānau| piṭṭhihatthattacau khurakausasaṇṭhānau, phaṇakatthavikasaṇṭhānau vā| hatthaṅgulittacau kuñ‍cikākausakasaṇṭhānau| gīvattacau galakañ‍cukasaṇṭhānau| mukhattacau chiddāvachiddau kīṭakulāvakasaṇṭhānau| sīsattacau pattatthavikasaṇṭhānauti|

tacapariggaṇhakaina ca yaugāvacaraina uttarauṭṭhatau paṭṭhāya uparimukhaṃ ñāṇaṃ paisaitvā paṭhamaṃ tāva  mukhaṃ pariyaunandhitvā ṭhitacammaṃ vavatthapaitabbaṃ| tatau nalāṭaṭṭhicammaṃ| tatau thavikāya pakkhittapattassa ca thavikāya ca antaraina hatthamiva sīsaṭṭhikassa ca sīsacammassa ca antaraina ñāṇaṃ paisaitvā aṭṭhikaina saddhiṃ cammassa aikābaddhabhāvaṃ viyaujaintaina sīsacammaṃ vavatthapaitabbaṃ| tatau khandhacammaṃ| tatau anulaumaina paṭilaumaina ca dakkhiṇahatthacammaṃ| atha tainaiva nayaina vāmahatthacammaṃ| tatau piṭṭhicammaṃ taṃ vavatthapaitvā anulaumaina paṭilaumaina ca dakkhiṇapādacammaṃ| atha tainaiva nayaina vāmapādacammaṃ| tatau anuk‍kamainaiva vatthiudarahadayagīvacammāni vavatthapaitabbāni| atha gīvacammānantaraṃ haiṭṭhimahanucammaṃ vavatthapaitvā adharauṭṭhapariyausānaṃ pāpaitvā niṭṭhapaitabbaṃ| aivaṃ auḷārikauḷārikaṃ pariggaṇhantassa sukhumampi pākaṭaṃ hauti| disatau dvīsu disāsu jātau| aukāsatau sakalasarīraṃ pariyaunandhitvā ṭhitau| paricchaidatau haiṭṭhā patiṭṭhitatalaina, upari ākāsaina paricchin‍nau, ayamassa sabhāgaparicchaidau| visabhāgaparicchaidau pana kaisasadisauyaiva|

188. maṃsanti nava maṃsapaisisatāni| taṃ sabbampi vaṇṇatau rattaṃ kiṃsukapupphasadisaṃ| saṇṭhānatau jaṅghapiṇḍikamaṃsaṃ tālapaṇṇapuṭabhattasaṇṭhānaṃ| ūrumaṃsaṃ nisadapautasaṇṭhānaṃ| ānisadamaṃsaṃ uddhanakauṭisaṇṭhānaṃ| piṭṭhimaṃsaṃ tālaguḷapaṭalasaṇṭhānaṃ| phāsukadvayamaṃsaṃ kauṭṭhalikāya kucchiyaṃ tanumattikālaipasaṇṭhānaṃ| thanamaṃsaṃ vaṭṭaitvā avakkhittamattikāpiṇḍasaṇṭhānaṃ| bāhudvayamaṃsaṃ  dviguṇaṃ katvā ṭhapitanic‍cammamahāmūsikasaṇṭhānaṃ| aivaṃ auḷārikauḷārikaṃ pariggaṇhantassa sukhumampi pākaṭaṃ hauti| disatau dvīsu disāsu jātaṃ| aukāsatau vīsādhikāni tīṇi aṭṭhisatāni anulimpitvā ṭhitaṃ| paricchaidatau haiṭṭhā aṭṭhisaṅghātai patiṭṭhitatalaina, upari tacaina, tiriyaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaina paricchin‍naṃ, ayamassa sabhāgaparicchaidau| visabhāgaparicchaidau pana kaisasadisauyaiva|

189. nhārūti nava nhārusatāni| vaṇṇatau sabbaipi nhārū saitā| saṇṭhānatau nānāsaṇṭhānā| aitaisu hi gīvāya uparimabhāgatau paṭṭhāya pañ‍ca mahānhārū sarīraṃ vinandhamānā purimapassaina autiṇṇā| pañ‍ca pacchimapassaina| pañ‍ca dakkhiṇapassaina| pañ‍ca vāmapassaina| dakkhiṇahatthaṃ vinandhamānāpi hatthassa purimapassaina pañ‍ca| pacchimapassaina pañ‍ca| tathā vāmahatthaṃ vinandhamānā| dakkhiṇapādaṃ vinandhamānāpi pādassa purimapassaina pañ‍ca| pacchimapassaina pañ‍ca| tathā vāmapādaṃ vinandhamānāpīti aivaṃ sarīradhārakā nāma saṭṭhimahānhārū kāyaṃ vinandhamānā autiṇṇā| yai kaṇḍarātipi vuc‍canti| tai sabbaipi kandalamakuḷasaṇṭhānā| añ‍ñai pana taṃ taṃ padaisaṃ ajjhauttharitvā ṭhitā| tatau sukhumatarā suttaraj‍jukasaṇṭhānā| añ‍ñai tatau sukhumatarā pūtilatāsaṇṭhānā, añ‍ñai tatau sukhumatarā mahāvīṇātantisaṇṭhānā| añ‍ñai thūlasuttakasaṇṭhānā| hatthapādapiṭṭhīsu nhārū sakuṇapādasaṇṭhānā| sīsai nhārū dārakānaṃ sīsajālakasaṇṭhānā| piṭṭhiyaṃ nhārū ātapai pasāritaal‍lajālasaṇṭhānā| avasaisā taṃtaṃaṅgapac‍caṅgānugatā nhārū sarīrai paṭimuk‍kajālakañ‍cukasaṇṭhānā| disatau dvīsu disāsu jātā| aukāsatau sakalasarīrai aṭṭhīni ābandhitvā ṭhitā| paricchaidatau haiṭṭhā tiṇṇaṃ aṭṭhisatānaṃ upari patiṭṭhitatalaihi, upari maṃsacammāni āhac‍ca ṭhitappadaisaihi, tiriyaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaina paricchin‍nā, ayaṃ naisaṃ sabhāgaparicchaidau| visabhāgaparicchaidau pana kaisasadisauyaiva|

190. aṭṭhīti ṭhapaitvā dvattiṃsa dantaṭṭhīni avasaisāni catusaṭṭhi hatthaṭṭhīni, catusaṭṭhi pādaṭṭhīni, catusaṭṭhi maṃsanissitāni muduaṭṭhīni, dvai paṇhikaṭṭhīni, aikaikasmiṃ pādai dvai dvai gaupphakaṭṭhīni, dvai jaṅghaṭṭhīni, aikaṃ jaṇṇukaṭṭhi, aikaṃ ūruṭṭhi, dvai kaṭiṭṭhīni, aṭṭhārasa piṭṭhikaṇṭakaṭṭhīni, catuvīsati phāsukaṭṭhīni, cuddasa uraṭṭhīni, aikaṃ hadayaṭṭhi, dvai akkhakaṭṭhīni, dvai kauṭṭaṭṭhīni, dvai bāhuṭṭhīni, dvai dvai aggabāhuṭṭhīni, satta gīvaṭṭhīni, dvai hanukaṭṭhīni, aikaṃ nāsikaṭṭhi, dvai akkhiṭṭhīni, dvai kaṇṇaṭṭhīni, aikaṃ nalāṭaṭṭhi| aikaṃ muddhaṭṭhi, nava sīsakapālaṭṭhīnīti aivaṃ timattāni aṭṭhisatāni, tāni sabbānipi vaṇṇatau saitāni| saṇṭhānatau nānāsaṇṭhānāni|

tattha hi aggapādaṅguliaṭṭhīni katakabījasaṇṭhānāni| tadanantarāni majjhapabbaṭṭhīni panasaṭṭhisaṇṭhānāni| mūlapabbaṭṭhīni paṇavasaṇṭhānāni| piṭṭhipādaṭṭhīni kauṭṭitakandalakandarāsisaṇṭhānāni| paṇhikaṭṭhi aikaṭṭhitālaphalabījasaṇṭhānaṃ| gaupphakaṭṭhīni baddhakīḷāgauḷakasaṇṭhānāni| jaṅghaṭṭhīnaṃ gaupphakaṭṭhīsu patiṭṭhitaṭṭhānaṃ apanītatacasindikaḷīrasaṇṭhānaṃ| khuddakajaṅghaṭṭhikaṃ dhanukadaṇḍasaṇṭhānaṃ| mahantaṃ milātasappapiṭṭhisaṇṭhānaṃ| jaṇṇukaṭṭhi aikatau parikkhīṇaphaiṇakasaṇṭhānaṃ| tattha jaṅghaṭṭhikassa patiṭṭhitaṭṭhānaṃ atikhiṇaggagausiṅgasaṇṭhānaṃ| ūruṭṭhi duttacchitavāsiparasudaṇḍasaṇṭhānaṃ| tassa kaṭiṭṭhimhi patiṭṭhitaṭṭhānaṃ kīḷāgauḷakasaṇṭhānaṃ| taina kaṭiṭṭhinau patiṭṭhitaṭṭhānaṃ aggacchin‍namahāpun‍nāgaphalasaṇṭhānaṃ|

kaṭiṭṭhīni  dvaipi aikābaddhāni hutvā kumbhakārikauddhanasaṇṭhānāni| pāṭiyaik‍kaṃ kammārakūṭayauttakasaṇṭhānāni| kauṭiyaṃ ṭhitaṃ ānisadaṭṭhi adhaumukhaṃ katvā gahitasappaphaṇasaṇṭhānaṃ, sattaṭṭhaṭṭhānaisu chiddāvachiddaṃ| piṭṭhikaṇṭakaṭṭhīni abbhantaratau uparūpari ṭhapitasīsapaṭṭavaiṭhakasaṇṭhānāni| bāhiratau vaṭṭanāvaḷisaṇṭhānāni| taisaṃ antarantarā kakacadantasadisā dvai tayau kaṇṭakā haunti| catuvīsatiyā phāsukaṭṭhīsu aparipuṇṇāni aparipuṇṇaasisaṇṭhānāni| paripuṇṇāni paripuṇṇaasisaṇṭhānāni| sabbānipi audātakuk‍kuṭassa pasāritapakkhasaṇṭhānāni| cuddasa uraṭṭhīni jiṇṇasandamānikapañ‍jarasaṇṭhānāni| hadayaṭṭhi dabbiphaṇasaṇṭhānaṃ|

akkhakaṭṭhīni khuddakalauhavāsidaṇḍasaṇṭhānāni| kauṭṭaṭṭhīni aikatau parikkhīṇasīhaḷakuddālasaṇṭhānāni| bāhuṭṭhīni ādāsadaṇḍakasaṇṭhānāni| aggabāhuṭṭhīni yamakatālakandasaṇṭhānāni| maṇibandhaṭṭhīni aikatau al‍liyāpaitvā ṭhapitasīsakapaṭṭavaiṭhakasaṇṭhānāni| piṭṭhihatthaṭṭhīni kauṭṭitakandalakandarāsisaṇṭhānāni| hatthaṅgulīsu mūlapabbaṭṭhīni paṇavasaṇṭhānāni| majjhapabbaṭṭhīni aparipuṇṇapanasaṭṭhisaṇṭhānāni| aggapabbaṭṭhīni katakabījasaṇṭhānāni|

satta gīvaṭṭhīni daṇḍaina vijjhitvā paṭipāṭiyā ṭhapitavaṃsakaḷīracak‍kalakasaṇṭhānāni| haiṭṭhimahanukaṭṭhi kammārānaṃ ayaukūṭayauttakasaṇṭhānaṃ| uparimaṃ avalaikhanasatthakasaṇṭhānaṃ| akkhikūpanāsakūpaṭṭhīni apanītamiñ‍jataruṇatālaṭṭhisaṇṭhānāni| nalāṭaṭṭhi adhaumukhaṭṭhapitasaṅkhathālakakapālasaṇṭhānaṃ| kaṇṇacūḷikaṭṭhīni nhāpitakhurakausasaṇṭhānāni| nalāṭakaṇṇacūḷikānaṃ upari paṭṭabandhanaukāsai aṭṭhisaṅkuṭitaghaṭapuṇṇapaṭalakhaṇḍasaṇṭhānaṃ| muddhaṭṭhi mukhacchin‍navaṅkanāḷikairasaṇṭhānaṃ| sīsaṭṭhīni sibbaitvā ṭhapitajaj‍jaralābukaṭāhasaṇṭhānāni|

disatau dvīsu disāsu jātāni| aukāsatau avisaisaina sakalasarīrai ṭhitāni| visaisaina panaittha sīsaṭṭhīni givaṭṭhīsu patiṭṭhitāni| gīvaṭṭhīni piṭṭhikaṇṭakaṭṭhīsu| piṭṭhikaṇṭakaṭṭhīni kaṭiṭṭhīsu| kaṭiṭṭhīni ūruṭṭhīsu| ūruṭṭhīni jaṇṇukaṭṭhīsu| jaṇṇukaṭṭhīni jaṅghaṭṭhīsu| jaṅghaṭṭhīni gaupphakaṭṭhīsu| gaupphakaṭṭhīni piṭṭhipādaṭṭhīsu patiṭṭhitāni| paricchaidatau antau aṭṭhimiñ‍jaina, uparitau maṃsaina, aggai mūlai ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaina paricchin‍nāni, ayaṃ naisaṃ sabhāgaparicchaidau| visabhāgaparicchaidau pana kaisasadisauyaiva|

191. aṭṭhimiñ‍janti  taisaṃ taisaṃ aṭṭhīnaṃ abbhantaragataṃ miñ‍jaṃ| taṃ vaṇṇatau saitaṃ| saṇṭhānatau mahantamahantānaṃ aṭṭhīnaṃ abbhantaragataṃ vaiḷunāḷiyaṃ pakkhittasaiditamahāvaittaggasaṇṭhānaṃ| khuddānukhuddakānaṃ abbhantaragataṃ vaiḷuyaṭṭhipabbaisu pakkhittasaiditatanuvaittaggasaṇṭhānaṃ| disatau dvīsu disāsu jātaṃ| aukāsatau aṭṭhīnaṃ abbhantarai patiṭṭhitaṃ| paricchaidatau aṭṭhīnaṃ abbhantaratalaihi paricchin‍naṃ, ayamassa sabhāgaparicchaidau| visabhāgaparicchaidau pana kaisasadisauyaiva|

192. vak‍kanti aikabandhanā dvai maṃsapiṇḍikā| taṃ vaṇṇatau mandarattaṃ pāḷibhaddakaṭṭhivaṇṇaṃ| saṇṭhānatau dārakānaṃ yamakakīḷāgauḷakasaṇṭhānaṃ, aikavaṇṭapaṭibaddhaambaphaladvayasaṇṭhānaṃ vā| disatau uparimāya disāya jātaṃ| aukāsatau galavāṭakā nikkhantaina aikamūlaina thaukaṃ gantvā dvidhā  bhin‍naina thūlanhārunā vinibaddhaṃ hutvā hadayamaṃsaṃ parikkhipitvā ṭhitaṃ| paricchaidatau vak‍kaṃ vak‍kabhāgaina paricchin‍naṃ, ayamassa sabhāgaparicchaidau| visabhāgaparicchaidau pana kaisasadisauyaiva|

193. hadayanti hadayamaṃsaṃ| taṃ vaṇṇatau rattapadumapattapiṭṭhivaṇṇaṃ| saṇṭhānatau bāhirapattāni apanaitvā adhaumukhaṃ ṭhapitapadumamakuḷasaṇṭhānaṃ| bahi maṭṭhaṃ, antau kausātakīphalassa abbhantarasadisaṃ| pañ‍ñavantānaṃ thaukaṃ vikasitaṃ, mandapañ‍ñānaṃ makuḷitamaiva| antau cassa pun‍nāgaṭṭhipatiṭṭhānamattau āvāṭakau hauti, yattha addhapasatamattaṃ lauhitaṃ saṇṭhāti, yaṃ nissāya manaudhātu ca manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu ca vattanti| taṃ panaitaṃ rāgacaritassa rattaṃ hauti, dausacaritassa kāḷakaṃ, mauhacaritassa maṃsadhauvanaudakasadisaṃ, vitak‍kacaritassa kulatthayūsavaṇṇaṃ, saddhācaritassa kaṇikārapupphavaṇṇaṃ, pañ‍ñācaritassa acchaṃ vippasan‍naṃ anāvilaṃ paṇḍaraṃ parisuddhaṃ niddhautajātimaṇi viya jutimantaṃ khāyati| disatau uparimāya disāya jātaṃ| aukāsatau sarīrabbhantarai dvin‍naṃ thanānaṃ majjhai patiṭṭhitaṃ| paricchaidatau hadayaṃ hadayabhāgaina paricchin‍naṃ, ayamassa sabhāgaparicchaidau| visabhāgaparicchaidau pana kaisasadisauyaiva|

194. yakananti yamakamaṃsapaṭalaṃ| taṃ vaṇṇatau rattaṃ paṇḍukadhātukaṃ nātirattakumudassa pattapiṭṭhivaṇṇaṃ| saṇṭhānatau mūlai aikaṃ aggai yamakaṃ kauviḷārapattasaṇṭhānaṃ| tañ‍ca dandhānaṃ aikamaiva hauti mahantaṃ, pañ‍ñavantānaṃ dvai vā tīṇi vā khuddakāni| disatau uparimāya disāya jātaṃ, aukāsatau dvin‍naṃ thanānaṃ abbhantarai dakkhiṇapassaṃ nissāya ṭhitaṃ| paricchaidatau yakanaṃ  yakanabhāgaina paricchin‍naṃ, ayamassa sabhāgaparicchaidau| visabhāgaparicchaidau pana kaisasadisauyaiva|

195. kilaumakanti paṭicchan‍nāpaṭicchan‍nabhaidatau duvidhaṃ pariyaunahanamaṃsaṃ| taṃ duvidhampi vaṇṇatau saitaṃ dukūlapilautikavaṇṇaṃ| saṇṭhānatau attanau aukāsasaṇṭhānaṃ| disatau paṭicchan‍nakilaumakaṃ uparimāya disāya| itaraṃ dvīsu disāsu jātaṃ| aukāsatau paṭicchan‍nakilaumakaṃ  hadayañ‍ca vak‍kañ‍ca paṭicchādaitvā, appaṭicchan‍nakilaumakaṃ sakalasarīrai cammassa haiṭṭhatau maṃsaṃ pariyaunandhitvā ṭhitaṃ| paricchaidatau haiṭṭhā maṃsaina, upari cammaina, tiriyaṃ kilaumakabhāgaina paricchin‍naṃ, ayamassa sabhāgaparicchaidau| visabhāgaparicchaidau pana kaisasadisauyaiva|

196. pihakanti udarajivhāmaṃsaṃ| taṃ vaṇṇatau nīlaṃ nigguṇḍipupphavaṇṇaṃ| saṇṭhānatau sattaṅgulappamāṇaṃ abandhanaṃ kāḷavacchakajivhāsaṇṭhānaṃ| disatau uparimāya disāya jātaṃ| aukāsatau hadayassa vāmapassai udarapaṭalassa matthakapassaṃ nissāya ṭhitaṃ, yasmiṃ paharaṇappahāraina bahinikkhantai sattānaṃ jīvitakkhayau hauti| paricchaidatau pihakabhāgaina paricchin‍naṃ, ayamassa sabhāgaparicchaidau| visabhāgaparicchaidau pana kaisasadisauyaiva|

197. papphāsanti dvattiṃsamaṃsakhaṇḍappabhaidaṃ papphāsamaṃsaṃ| taṃ vaṇṇatau rattaṃ nātipak‍kaudumbaraphalavaṇṇaṃ| saṇṭhānatau visamacchin‍nabahalapūvakhaṇḍasaṇṭhānaṃ| abbhantarai asitapītānaṃ abhāvai uggataina kammajataijusmānā abbhāhatattā saṃkhāditapalālapiṇḍamiva nirasaṃ niraujaṃ| disatau uparimāya disāya jātaṃ| aukāsatau sarīrabbhantarai dvin‍naṃ thanānaṃ antarai hadayañ‍ca yakanañ‍ca upari chādaitvā aulambantaṃ ṭhitaṃ| paricchaidatau papphāsabhāgaina paricchin‍naṃ, ayamassa sabhāgaparicchaidau| visabhāgaparicchaidau pana kaisasadisauyaiva|

198. antanti purisassa dvattiṃsahatthā itthiyā aṭṭhavīsatihatthā aikavīsatiyā ṭhānaisu aubhaggā antavaṭṭi| tadaitaṃ vaṇṇatau saitaṃ sakkharasudhāvaṇṇaṃ| saṇṭhānatau lauhitadauṇiyaṃ ābhujitvā ṭhapitasīsacchin‍nasappasaṇṭhānaṃ| disatau dvīsu disāsu jātaṃ| aukāsatau upari galavāṭakai haiṭṭhā ca karīsamaggai vinibandhattā galavāṭakakarīsamaggapariyantai sarīrabbhantarai ṭhitaṃ| paricchaidatau  antabhāgaina paricchin‍naṃ, ayamassa sabhāgaparicchaidau| visabhāgaparicchaidau pana kaisasadisauyaiva|

199. antaguṇanti  antabhaugaṭṭhānaisu bandhanaṃ| taṃ vaṇṇatau saitaṃ dakasītalikamūlavaṇṇaṃ| saṇṭhānatau dakasītalikamūlasaṇṭhānamaiva| disatau dvīsu disāsu jātaṃ| aukāsatau kuddālapharasukammādīni karauntānaṃ yantākaḍḍhanakālai yantasuttakamiva yantaphalakāni antabhaugai aikatau agaḷantai ābandhitvā pādapuñchanaraj‍jumaṇḍalakassa antarā saṃsibbitvā ṭhitaraj‍jukā viya aikavīsatiyā antabhaugānaṃ antarā ṭhitaṃ| paricchaidatau antaguṇabhāgaina paricchin‍naṃ, ayamassa sabhāgaparicchaidau| visabhāgaparicchaidau pana kaisasadisauyaiva|

200. udariyanti udarai bhavaṃ asitapītakhāyitasāyitaṃ| taṃ vaṇṇatau ajjhauhaṭāhāravaṇṇaṃ| saṇṭhānatau parissāvanai sithilabaddhataṇḍulasaṇṭhānaṃ| disatau uparimāya disāya jātaṃ| aukāsatau udarai ṭhitaṃ|

udaraṃ nāma ubhatau nippīḷiyamānassa al‍lasāṭakassa majjhai sañ‍jātaphauṭakasadisaṃ antapaṭalaṃ, bahi maṭṭhaṃ, antau maṃsakasambupalivaiṭhanakiliṭṭhapāvārakapupphakasadisaṃ, kuthitapanasatacassa abbhantarasadisantipi vattuṃ vaṭṭati, yattha tak‍kauṭakā gaṇḍuppādakā tālahīrakā sūcimukhakā paṭatantasuttakā ic‍caivamādidvattiṃsakulappabhaidā kimayau ākulabyākulā saṇḍasaṇḍacārinau hutvā nivasanti, yai pānabhaujanādimhi avij‍jamānai ul‍laṅghitvā viravantā hadayamaṃsaṃ abhihananti, pānabhaujanādiajjhauharaṇavailāyañ‍ca uddhaṃmukhā hutvā paṭhamajjhauhaṭai dvai tayau ālaupai turitaturitā viluppanti, yaṃ taisaṃ kimīnaṃ sūtigharaṃ vac‍cakuṭi gilānasālā susānañ‍ca hauti| yattha saiyyathāpi nāma caṇḍālagāmadvārai candanikāya nidāghasamayai thūlaphusitakai daivai vassantai udakaina vuyhamānaṃ muttakarīsacammaaṭṭhinhārukhaṇḍakhaiḷasiṅghāṇikālauhitappabhutinānākuṇapajātaṃ nipatitvā kaddamaudakāluḷitaṃ dvīhatīhac‍cayaina sañ‍jātakimikulaṃ sūriyātapasantāpavaigakuthitaṃ upari phaiṇapupphuḷakai muñ‍cantaṃ abhinīlavaṇṇaṃ paramaduggandhajaigucchaṃ naiva upagantuṃ, na daṭṭhuṃ araharūpataṃ āpaj‍jitvā tiṭṭhati, pagaiva  ghāyituṃ vā sāyituṃ vā, aivamaiva nānappakāraṃ pānabhaujanādidantamusalasañ‍cuṇṇitaṃ jivhāhatthaparivattitakhaiḷalālāpalibuddhaṃ  taṅkhaṇavigatavaṇṇagandharasādisampadaṃ tantavāyakhalisuvānavamathusadisaṃ nipatitvā pittasaimhavātapalivaiṭhitaṃ hutvā udaraggisantāpavaigakuthitaṃ kimikulākulaṃ uparūpari phaiṇapupphuḷakāni muñ‍cantaṃ paramakasambuduggandhajaigucchabhāvaṃ āpaj‍jitvā tiṭṭhati| yaṃ sutvāpi pānabhaujanādīsu amanuñ‍ñatā saṇṭhāti, pagaiva pañ‍ñācakkhunā avalaukaitvā| yattha ca patitaṃ pānabhaujanādi pañ‍cadhā vivaikaṃ gacchati, aikaṃ bhāgaṃ pāṇakā khādanti, aikaṃ bhāgaṃ udaraggi jhāpaiti, aikau bhāgau muttaṃ hauti, aikau bhāgau karīsaṃ, aikau bhāgau rasabhāvaṃ āpaj‍jitvā sauṇitamaṃsādīni upabrūhayati|

paricchaidatau udarapaṭalaina caiva udariyabhāgaina ca paricchin‍naṃ| ayamassa sabhāgaparicchaidau| visabhāgaparicchaidau pana kaisasadisauyaiva|

201. karīsanti vac‍caṃ| taṃ vaṇṇatau yaibhuyyaina ajjhauhaṭāhāravaṇṇamaiva hauti| saṇṭhānatau aukāsasaṇṭhānaṃ| disatau haiṭṭhimāya disāya jātaṃ| aukāsatau pak‍kāsayai ṭhitaṃ| pak‍kāsayau nāma haiṭṭhānābhi-piṭṭhikaṇṭakamūlānaṃ antarai antāvasānai ubbaidhaina aṭṭhaṅgulamattau vaiḷunāḷikasadisau, yattha saiyyathāpi nāma upari bhūmibhāgai patitaṃ vassaudakaṃ augaḷitvā haiṭṭhā bhūmibhāgaṃ pūraitvā tiṭṭhati, aivamaiva yaṃkiñ‍ci āmāsayai patitaṃ pānabhaujanādikaṃ udaragginā phaiṇuddaihakaṃ pak‍kaṃ pak‍kaṃ nisadāya pisitamiva saṇhabhāvaṃ āpaj‍jitvā antabilaina augaḷitvā augaḷitvā aumadditvā vaiḷupabbai pakkhipamānapaṇḍumattikā viya san‍nicitaṃ hutvā tiṭṭhati| paricchaidatau pak‍kāsayapaṭalaina caiva karīsabhāgaina ca paricchin‍naṃ, ayamassa sabhāgaparicchaidau| visabhāgaparicchaidau pana kaisasadisauyaiva|

202. matthaluṅganti sīsakaṭāhabbhantarai ṭhitamiñ‍jarāsi| taṃ vaṇṇatau saitaṃ ahicchattakapiṇḍavaṇṇaṃ| dadhibhāvaṃ asampattaṃ duṭṭhakhīravaṇṇantipi vattuṃ vaṭṭati| saṇṭhānatau aukāsasaṇṭhānaṃ| disatau uparimāya disāya jātaṃ| aukāsatau sīsakaṭāhabbhantarai cattārau sibbinimaggai nissāya samaudhānaitvā ṭhapitā cattārau piṭṭhapiṇḍā viya samauhitaṃ tiṭṭhati| paricchaidatau sīsakaṭāhassa abbhantaratalaihi caiva matthaluṅgabhāgaina ca paricchin‍naṃ, ayamassa sabhāgaparicchaidau| visabhāgaparicchaidau pana kaisasadisauyaiva|

203. pittanti  dvai pittāni baddhapittañ‍ca abaddhapittañ‍ca| tattha baddhapittaṃ vaṇṇatau bahalamadhukatailavaṇṇaṃ| abaddhapittaṃ milātaākulipupphavaṇṇaṃ| saṇṭhānatau ubhayampi aukāsasaṇṭhānaṃ| disatau baddhapittaṃ uparimāya disāya jātaṃ, itaraṃ dvīsu disāsu jātaṃ| aukāsatau abaddhapittaṃ ṭhapaitvā kaisalaumadantanakhānaṃ maṃsavinimuttaṭṭhānañ‍caiva thaddhasukkhacammañ‍ca udakamiva tailabindu avasaisasarīraṃ byāpaitvā ṭhitaṃ, yamhi kupitai akkhīni pītakāni haunti, bhamanti, gattaṃ kampati, kaṇḍūyati| baddhapittaṃ hadayapapphāsānaṃ antarai yakanamaṃsaṃ nissāya patiṭṭhitai mahākausātakīkausakasadisai pittakausakai ṭhitaṃ, yamhi kupitai sattā ummattakā haunti, vipal‍latthacittā hirauttappaṃ chaḍḍaitvā akātabbaṃ karaunti, abhāsitabbaṃ bhāsanti, acintitabbaṃ cintainti| paricchaidatau pittabhāgaina paricchin‍naṃ, ayamassa sabhāgaparicchaidau| visabhāgaparicchaidau pana kaisasadisauyaiva|

204. saimhanti sarīrabbhantarai aikapatthapūrappamāṇaṃ saimhaṃ| taṃ vaṇṇatau saitaṃ nāgabalāpaṇṇarasavaṇṇaṃ| saṇṭhānatau aukāsasaṇṭhānaṃ| disatau uparimāya disāya jātaṃ| aukāsatau udarapaṭalai ṭhitaṃ| yaṃ pānabhaujanādiajjhauharaṇakālai saiyyathāpi nāma udakai saivālapaṇakaṃ kaṭṭhai vā kathalai vā patantai chij‍jitvā dvidhā hutvā puna ajjhauttharitvā tiṭṭhati, aivamaiva pānabhaujanādimhi nipatantai chij‍jitvā dvidhā hutvā puna ajjhauttharitvā tiṭṭhati, yamhi ca mandībhūtai pak‍kagaṇḍau viya pūtikuk‍kuṭaṇḍamiva ca udaraṃ paramajaigucchaṃ kuṇapagandhaṃ hauti, tatau uggataina ca gandhaina uddaikaupi mukhampi duggandhaṃ pūtikuṇapasadisaṃ hauti| sau ca purisau apaihi duggandhaṃ vāyasīti vattabbataṃ āpaj‍jati, yañ‍ca vaḍḍhitvā bahalattamāpan‍naṃ pidhānaphalakamiva vac‍cakuṭiyaṃ udarapaṭalassa abbhantaraiyaiva kuṇapagandhaṃ san‍nirumbhitvā tiṭṭhati| paricchaidatau saimhabhāgaina paricchin‍naṃ, ayamassa sabhāgaparicchaidau| visabhāgaparicchaidau pana kaisasadisauyaiva|

205. pubbauti  pūtilauhitavasaina pavattapubbaṃ| taṃ vaṇṇatau paṇḍupalāsavaṇṇau| matasarīrai pana pūtibahalācāmavaṇṇau hauti| saṇṭhānatau aukāsasaṇṭhānau| disatau dvīsu disāsu hauti| aukāsatau pana pubbassa aukāsau nāma nibaddhau natthi, yattha sau san‍nicitau tiṭṭhaiyya, yatra yatra khāṇukaṇṭakapaharaṇaggijālādīhi abhihatai sarīrappadaisai lauhitaṃ saṇṭhahitvā pac‍cati, gaṇḍapīḷakādayau vā uppaj‍janti, tatra tatra tiṭṭhati| paricchaidatau pubbabhāgaina paricchin‍nau, ayamassa sabhāgaparicchaidau| visabhāgaparicchaidau pana kaisasadisauyaiva|

206. lauhitanti  dvai lauhitāni san‍nicitalauhitañ‍ca saṃsaraṇalauhitañ‍ca| tattha san‍nicitalauhitaṃ vaṇṇatau nipak‍kabahalalākhārasavaṇṇaṃ| saṃsaraṇalauhitaṃ acchalākhārasavaṇṇaṃ| saṇṭhānatau ubhayampi aukāsasaṇṭhānaṃ| disatau san‍nicitalauhitaṃ uparimāya disāya jātaṃ| itaraṃ dvisu disāsu jātaṃ| aukāsatau saṃsaraṇalauhitaṃ ṭhapaitvā kaisalaumadantanakhānaṃ maṃsavinimuttaṭṭhānañ‍caiva thaddhasukkhacammañ‍ca dhamanijālānusāraina sabbaṃ upādiṇṇasarīraṃ pharitvā ṭhitaṃ| san‍nicitalauhitaṃ yakanaṭṭhānassa haiṭṭhābhāgaṃ pūraitvā aikapatthapūramattaṃ hadayavak‍kapapphāsānaṃ upari thaukaṃ thaukaṃ paggharantaṃ vak‍kahadayayakanapapphāsai taimayamānaṃ ṭhitaṃ| tasmiṃ hi vak‍kahadayādīni ataimaintai sattā pipāsitā haunti| paricchaidatau lauhitabhāgaina paricchin‍naṃ, ayamassa sabhāgaparicchaidau| visabhāgaparicchaidau pana kaisasadisauyaiva|

207. saidauti laumakūpādīhi paggharaṇakaāpaudhātu| sau vaṇṇatau vippasan‍natilatailavaṇṇau| saṇṭhānatau aukāsasaṇṭhānau| disatau dvīsu disāsu jātau| aukāsatau saidassaukāsau nāma nibaddhau natthi, yattha sau lauhitaṃ viya sadā tiṭṭhaiyya| yadā pana aggisantāpasūriyasantāpautuvikārādīhi sarīraṃ santapati, tadā udakatau abbūḷhamattavisamacchin‍nabhisamuḷālakumudanāḷakalāpau viya sabbakaisalaumakūpavivaraihi paggharati, tasmā tassa saṇṭhānampi kaisalaumakūpavivarānañ‍ñaiva vasaina vaiditabbaṃ| saidapariggaṇhakaina ca yauginā kaisalaumakūpavivarai pūraitvā ṭhitavasainaiva saidau manasi kātabbau| paricchaidatau  saidabhāgaina paricchin‍nau, ayamassa sabhāgaparicchaidau| visabhāgaparicchaidau pana kaisasadisauyaiva|

maidauti thinasinaihau| sau vaṇṇatau phālitahaliddivaṇṇau| saṇṭhānatau thūlasarīrassa tāva cammamaṃsantarai ṭhapitahaliddivaṇṇadukūlapilautikasaṇṭhānau hauti| kisasarīrassa jaṅghamaṃsaṃ ūrumaṃsaṃ piṭṭhikaṇṭakanissitaṃ piṭṭhimaṃsaṃ udaravaṭṭimaṃsanti aitāni nissāya diguṇatiguṇaṃ katvā ṭhapitahaliddivaṇṇadukūlapilautikasaṇṭhānau| disatau dvīsu disāsu jātau| aukāsatau thūlassa sakalasarīraṃ pharitvā kisassa jaṅghamaṃsādīni nissāya ṭhitau, yaṃ sinaihasaṅkhaṃ gatampi paramajaigucchattā naiva muddhani tailatthāya, na nāsatailādīnamatthāya gaṇhanti| paricchaidatau haiṭṭhā maṃsaina, upari cammaina, tiriyaṃ maidabhāgaina paricchin‍nau, ayamassa sabhāgaparicchaidau| visabhāgaparicchaidau pana kaisasadisauyaiva|

208. assūti  akkhīhi paggharaṇakaāpaudhātu| taṃ vaṇṇatau vippasan‍natilatailavaṇṇaṃ| saṇṭhānatau aukāsasaṇṭhānaṃ| disatau uparimāya disāya jātaṃ| aukāsatau akkhikūpakaisu ṭhitaṃ| na caitaṃ pittakausakai pittamiva akkhikūpakaisu sadā san‍nicitaṃ tiṭṭhati| yadā pana sattā saumanassajātā mahāhasitaṃ hasanti, daumanassajātā raudanti paridaivanti, tathārūpaṃ vā visamāhāraṃ āhārainti, yadā ca naisaṃ akkhīni dhūmarajapaṃsukādīhi abhihañ‍ñanti| tadā aitaihi saumanassadaumanassavisabhāgāhārautūhi samuṭṭhahitvā akkhikūpakai pūraitvā tiṭṭhati vā paggharati vā| assupariggaṇhakaina ca yauginā akkhikūpakai pūraitvā ṭhitavasainaiva pariggaṇhitabbaṃ| paricchaidatau assubhāgaina paricchin‍naṃ, ayamassa sabhāgaparicchaidau| visabhāgaparicchaidau pana kaisasadisauyaiva|

209. vasāti vilīnasinaihau| sā vaṇṇatau nāḷikairatailavaṇṇā| ācāmai āsittatailavaṇṇātipi vattuṃ vaṭṭati| saṇṭhānatau nhānakālai pasan‍naudakassa upari paribbhamantasinaihabinduvisaṭasaṇṭhānā| disatau dvīsu disāsu jātā| aukāsatau yaibhuyyaina hatthatalahatthapiṭṭhipādatalapādapiṭṭhināsapuṭanalāṭaaṃsakūṭaisu ṭhitā| na caisā aitaisu aukāsaisu sadā vilīnāva hutvā tiṭṭhati| yadā pana aggisantāpasūriyasantāpautuvisabhāgadhātuvisabhāgaihi tai padaisā usmājātā  haunti, tadā tattha nhānakālai pasan‍naudakūpari sinaihabinduvisaṭau viya itau citau ca sañ‍carati| paricchaidatau vasābhāgaina paricchin‍nā, ayamassa sabhāgaparicchaidau| visabhāgaparicchaidau pana kaisasadisauyaiva|

210. khaiḷauti antaumukhai phaiṇamissā āpaudhātu| sau vaṇṇatau saitau phaiṇavaṇṇau| saṇṭhānatau aukāsasaṇṭhānau| phaiṇasaṇṭhānautipi vattuṃ vaṭṭati| disatau uparimāya disāya jātau| aukāsatau ubhauhi kapaulapassaihi auruyha jivhāya ṭhitau| na caisa aittha sadā san‍nicitau hutvā tiṭṭhati| yadā pana sattā tathārūpamāhāraṃ passanti vā saranti vā, uṇhatittakaṭukalauṇambilānaṃ vā kiñ‍ci mukhai ṭhapainti, yadā vā naisaṃ hadayaṃ āgilāyati, kismiñ‍ci daiva vā jigucchā uppaj‍jati, tadā khaiḷau uppaj‍jitvā ubhauhi kapaulapassaihi auruyha jivhāya saṇṭhāti| aggajivhāya caisa tanukau hauti, mūlajivhāya bahalau, mukhai pakkhittañ‍ca puthukaṃ vā taṇḍulaṃ vā añ‍ñaṃ vā kiñ‍ci khādanīyaṃ nadīpulinai khatakūpakasalilaṃ viya  parikkhayaṃ agacchantauva taimaituṃ samatthau hauti| paricchaidatau khaiḷabhāgaina paricchin‍nau, ayamassa sabhāgaparicchaidau| visabhāgaparicchaidau pana kaisasadisauva|

211. siṅghāṇikāti matthaluṅgatau paggharaṇakaasuci| sā vaṇṇatau taruṇatālaṭṭhimiñ‍javaṇṇā| saṇṭhānatau aukāsasaṇṭhānā| disatau uparimāya disāya jātā| aukāsatau nāsāpuṭai pūraitvā ṭhitā| na caisā aittha sadā san‍nicitā hutvā tiṭṭhati, atha khau yathā nāma purisau paduminipattai dadhiṃ bandhitvā haiṭṭhā kaṇṭakaina vijjhaiyya, athānaina chiddaina dadhimuttaṃ gaḷitvā bahi pataiyya, aivamaiva yadā sattā raudanti, visabhāgāhārautuvasaina vā sañ‍jātadhātukhaubhā haunti, tadā antau sīsatau pūtisaimhabhāvamāpan‍naṃ matthaluṅgaṃ gaḷitvā tālumatthakavivaraina autaritvā nāsāpuṭai pūraitvā tiṭṭhati vā paggharati vā| siṅghāṇikā pariggaṇhakaina ca yauginā nāsāpuṭai pūraitvā ṭhitavasainaiva pariggaṇhitabbā| paricchaidatau siṅghāṇikābhāgaina paricchin‍nā, ayamassā sabhāgaparicchaidau| visabhāgaparicchaidau pana kaisasadisauva|

212. lasikāti  sarīrasandhīnaṃ abbhantarai picchilakuṇapaṃ| sā vaṇṇatau kaṇikāraniyyāsavaṇṇā| saṇṭhānatau aukāsasaṇṭhānā| disatau dvīsu disāsu jātā| aukāsatau aṭṭhisandhīnaṃ abbhañ‍janakic‍caṃ sādhayamānā asītisatasandhīnaṃ abbhantarai ṭhitā| yassa caisā mandā hauti, tassa uṭṭhahantassa nisīdantassa abhik‍kamantassa paṭik‍kamantassa samiñ‍jantassa pasāraintassa aṭṭhikāni kaṭakaṭāyanti, accharāsaddaṃ karauntau viya sañ‍carati| aikayaujanadviyaujanamattaṃ addhānaṃ gatassa vāyaudhātu kuppati, gattāni dukkhanti| yassa pana bahukā haunti, tassa uṭṭhānanisaj‍jādīsu na aṭṭhīni kaṭakaṭāyanti, dīghampi addhānaṃ gatassa na vāyaudhātu kuppati, na gattāni dukkhanti| paricchaidatau lasikābhāgaina paricchin‍nā, ayamassā sabhāgaparicchaidau| visabhāgaparicchaidau pana kaisasadisauva|

213. muttanti muttarasaṃ| taṃ vaṇṇatau māsakhāraudakavaṇṇaṃ| saṇṭhānatau adhaumukhaṭṭhapitaudakakumbhaabbhantaragataudakasaṇṭhānaṃ| disatau haiṭṭhimāya disāya jātaṃ| aukāsatau vatthissa abbhantarai ṭhitaṃ| vatthi nāma vatthi puṭau vuc‍cati| yattha saiyyathāpi candanikāya pakkhittai amukhai ravaṇaghaṭai candanikārasau pavisati, na cassa pavisanamaggau pañ‍ñāyati, aivamaiva sarīratau muttaṃ pavisati, na cassa pavisanamaggau pañ‍ñāyati, nikkhamanamaggau pana pākaṭau hauti| yamhi ca muttassa bharitai passāvaṃ karaumāti sattānaṃ āyūhanaṃ hauti| paricchaidatau vatthiabbhantaraina caiva muttabhāgaina ca paricchin‍naṃ, ayamassa sabhāgaparicchaidau| visabhāgaparicchaidau pana kaisasadisauva|

214. aivañhi kaisādikai kauṭṭhāsai vaṇṇasaṇṭhānadisaukāsaparicchaidavasaina vavatthapaitvā anupubbatau nātisīghatautiādinā nayaina vaṇṇasaṇṭhānagandhāsayaukāsavasaina pañ‍cadhā paṭik‍kūlā paṭik‍kūlāti manasikarautau paṇṇattisamatik‍kamāvasānai saiyyathāpi cakkhumatau purisassa dvattiṃsavaṇṇānaṃ kusumānaṃ aikasuttakaganthitaṃ mālaṃ aulaukaintassa sabbapupphāni apubbāpariyamiva pākaṭāni haunti, aivamaiva atthi imasmiṃ kāyai kaisāti imaṃ kāyaṃ aulaukaintassa sabbai tai dhammā  apubbāpariyāva pākaṭā haunti| taina vuttaṃ manasikārakausal‍lakathāyaṃ ‘‘ādikammikassa hi kaisāti manasikarautau manasikārau gantvā muttanti imaṃ pariyausānakauṭṭhāsamaiva āhac‍ca tiṭṭhatī’’ti|

sacai pana bahiddhāpi manasikāraṃ upasaṃharati, athassa aivaṃ sabbakauṭṭhāsaisu pākaṭībhūtaisu āhiṇḍantā manussatiracchānādayau sattākāraṃ vijahitvā kauṭṭhāsarāsivasainaiva upaṭṭhahanti, taihi ca ajjhauhariyamānaṃ pānabhaujanādi kauṭṭhāsarāsimhi pakkhipamānamiva upaṭṭhāti|

athassa anupubbamuñ‍canādivasaina paṭik‍kūlā paṭik‍kūlāti punappunaṃ manasikarautau anuk‍kamaina appanā uppaj‍jati| tattha kaisādīnaṃ vaṇṇasaṇṭhānadisaukāsaparicchaidavasaina upaṭṭhānaṃ uggahanimittaṃ| sabbākāratau paṭik‍kūlavasaina upaṭṭhānaṃ paṭibhāganimittaṃ| taṃ āsaivatau bhāvayatau vuttanayaina asubhakammaṭṭhānaisu viya paṭhamajjhānavasainaiva appanā uppaj‍jati|

sā yassa aikauva kauṭṭhāsau pākaṭau hauti, aikasmiṃ vā kauṭṭhāsai appanaṃ patvā puna añ‍ñasmiṃ yaugaṃ na karauti, tassa aikāva uppaj‍jati| yassa pana anaikai kauṭṭhāsā pākaṭā haunti, aikasmiṃ vā jhānaṃ patvā puna añ‍ñasmiṃpi yaugaṃ karauti, tassa mal‍lakatthairassa viya kauṭṭhāsagaṇanāya paṭhamajjhānāni nibbattanti|

sau  kirāyasmā dīghabhāṇakaabhayatthairaṃ hatthai gahaitvā ‘‘āvusau abhaya, imaṃ tāva pañhaṃ uggaṇhāhī’’ti vatvā āha – ‘‘mal‍lakatthairau dvattiṃsakauṭṭhāsaisu dvattiṃsāya paṭhamajjhānānaṃ lābhī| sacai rattiṃ aikaṃ, divā aikaṃ samāpaj‍jati, atiraikaddhamāsaina puna sampaj‍jati, sacai pana daivasikaṃ aikaṃ samāpaj‍jati, atiraikamāsaina puna sampaj‍jatī’’ti|

aivaṃ paṭhamajjhānavasaina ijjhamānampi caitaṃ kammaṭṭhānaṃ vaṇṇasaṇṭhānādīsu satibalaina ijjhanatau kāyagatāsatīti vuc‍cati|

imañ‍ca kāyagatāsatimanuyuttau bhikkhu aratiratisahau hauti, na ca naṃ arati sahati, uppan‍naṃ aratiṃ abhibhuyya abhibhuyya viharati| bhayabhairavasahau hauti, na ca naṃ bhayabhairavaṃ sahati, uppan‍naṃ bhayabhairavaṃ abhibhuyya abhibhuyya viharati| khamau hauti sītassa uṇhassa  …pai॰… pāṇaharānaṃ adhivāsakajātikau hauti (ma॰ ni॰ 3.159)| kaisādīnaṃ vaṇṇabhaidaṃ nissāya catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ lābhī hauti| cha abhiñ‍ñā paṭivijjhati (ma॰ ni॰ 3.159)|

tasmā havai appamattau, anuyuñ‍jaitha paṇḍitau|

aivaṃ anaikānisaṃsaṃ, imaṃ kāyagatāsatinti||

idaṃ kāyagatāsatiyaṃ vitthārakathāmukhaṃ|

ānāpānassatikathā

215. idāni yaṃ taṃ bhagavatā ‘‘ayampi khau, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassatisamādhi bhāvitau bahulīkatau santau caiva paṇītau ca asaicanakau ca sukhau ca vihārau, uppan‍nuppan‍nai ca pāpakai akusalai dhammai ṭhānasau antaradhāpaiti vūpasamaitī’’ti aivaṃ pasaṃsitvā –

‘‘kathaṃ bhāvitau ca, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassatisamādhi kathaṃ bahulīkatau santau caiva paṇītau ca asaicanakau ca sukhau ca vihārau, uppan‍nuppan‍nai ca pāpakai akusalai dhammai ṭhānasau antaradhāpaiti vūpasamaiti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau  vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā, sau satauva assasati satau passasati| dīghaṃ vā assasantau dīghaṃ assasāmīti pajānāti| dīghaṃ vā passasantau…pai॰… rassaṃ vā assasantau…pai॰… rassaṃ vā passasantau rassaṃ passasāmīti pajānāti| sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmīti sikkhati| sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmīti sikkhati| passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ assasissāmīti sikkhati| passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ passasissāmīti sikkhati| pītipaṭisaṃvaidī… sukhapaṭisaṃvaidī… cittasaṅkhārapaṭisaṃvaidī… passambhayaṃ cittasaṅkhāraṃ… cittapaṭisaṃvaidī… abhippamaudayaṃ cittaṃ… samādahaṃ cittaṃ… vimaucayaṃ cittaṃ  … anic‍cānupassī… virāgānupassī… niraudhānupassī| paṭinissaggānupassī assasissāmīti sikkhati| paṭinissaggānupassī passasissāmīti sikkhatī’’ti –

aivaṃ sauḷasavatthukaṃ ānāpānassatikammaṭṭhānaṃ niddiṭṭhaṃ| tassa bhāvanānayau anuppattau| sau pana yasmā pāḷivaṇṇanānusārainaiva vuc‍camānau sabbākāraparipūrau hauti| tasmā ayamaittha pāḷivaṇṇanāpubbaṅgamau niddaisau|

216. kathaṃ bhāvitau ca, bhikkhavai, ānāpānassati samādhīti aittha tāva kathanti ānāpānassatisamādhibhāvanaṃ nānappakāratau vitthāraitukamyatāpucchā| bhāvitau ca bhikkhavai ānāpānassatisamādhīti nānappakāratau vitthāraitukamyatāya puṭṭhadhammanidassanaṃ| kathaṃ bahulīkatau…pai॰… vūpasamaitīti aitthāpi aisaiva nayau| tattha bhāvitauti uppāditau vaḍḍhitau vā| ānāpānassatisamādhīti ānāpānapariggāhikāya satiyā saddhiṃ sampayuttau samādhi| ānāpānassatiyaṃ vā samādhi ānāpānassatisamādhi| bahulīkatauti punappunaṃ katau| santaucaiva paṇītau cāti santau caiva paṇītau caiva| ubhayattha aiva saddaina niyamau vaiditabbau| kiṃ vuttaṃ hauti? ayañhi yathā asubhakammaṭṭhānaṃ kaivalaṃ paṭivaidhavasaina santañ‍ca paṇītañ‍ca, auḷārikārammaṇattā pana paṭik‍kūlārammaṇattā ca ārammaṇavasaina naiva santaṃ na paṇītaṃ, na aivaṃ kainaci pariyāyaina asantau vā apaṇītau vā, atha khau ārammaṇasantatāyapi santau vūpasantau nibbutau, paṭivaidhasaṅkhātaaṅgasantatāyapi| ārammaṇapaṇītatāyapi paṇītau atittikarau, aṅgapaṇītatāyapīti| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘santau caiva paṇītau cā’’ti|

asaicanakau  ca sukhau ca vihārauti aittha pana nāssa saicananti asaicanakau, anāsittakau abbaukiṇṇau pāṭiyaik‍kau āvaiṇikau| natthi aittha parikammaina vā upacāraina vā santatā| ādisaman‍nāhāratau pabhuti attanau sabhāvainaiva santau ca paṇītau cāti atthau| kaici pana asaicanakauti anāsittakau aujavantau sabhāvainaiva  madhurauti vadanti| aivaṃ ayaṃ asaicanakau ca, appitappitakkhaṇai kāyikacaitasikasukhapaṭilābhāya saṃvattanatau sukhau ca vihārauti vaiditabbau| uppan‍nuppan‍naiti avikkhambhitai avikkhambhitai| pāpakaiti lāmakai| akusalai dhammaiti akausal‍lasambhūtai dhammai| ṭhānasau antaradhāpaitīti khaṇainaiva antaradhāpaiti vikkhambhaiti| vūpasamaitīti suṭṭhu upasamaiti| nibbaidhabhāgiyattā vā anupubbaina ariyamaggavuddhippattau samucchindati, paṭippassambhaitīti vuttaṃ hauti|

ayaṃ panaittha saṅkhaipatthau| bhikkhavai, kaina pakāraina kainākāraina kaina vidhinā bhāvitau ānāpānassatisamādhi kaina pakāraina bahulīkatau santau caiva…pai॰… vūpasamaitīti|

217. idāni tamatthaṃ vitthāraintau ‘‘idha, bhikkhavai’’tiādimāha| tattha idha bhikkhavai bhikkhūti bhikkhavai, imasmiṃ sāsanai bhikkhu| ayañhi aittha idhasaddau sabbappakāraānāpānassatisamādhinibbattakassa puggalassa san‍nissayabhūtasāsanaparidīpanau añ‍ñasāsanassa tathābhāvapaṭisaidhanau ca| vuttañhaitaṃ – idhaiva, bhikkhavai, samaṇau…pai॰… suñ‍ñā parappavādā samaṇaibhi añ‍ñaihī’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.139)| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘imasmiṃ sāsanai bhikkhū’’ti|

arañ‍ñagatau vā…pai॰… suñ‍ñāgāragatau vāti idamassa ānāpānassatisamādhibhāvanānurūpasaināsanapariggahaparidīpanaṃ| imassa hi bhikkhunau dīgharattaṃ rūpādīsu ārammaṇaisu anuvisaṭaṃ cittaṃ ānāpānassatisamādhiārammaṇaṃ abhiruhituṃ na icchati, kūṭagauṇayuttarathau viya uppathamaiva dhāvati| tasmā saiyyathāpi nāma gaupau kūṭadhainuyā sabbaṃ khīraṃ pivitvā vaḍḍhitaṃ kūṭavacchaṃ damaitukāmau dhainutau apanaitvā aikamantai mahantaṃ thambhaṃ nikhaṇitvā tattha yauttaina bandhaiyya, athassa sau vacchau itau citau ca vipphanditvā palāyituṃ asak‍kauntau tamaiva thambhaṃ upanisīdaiyya vā upanipaj‍jaiyya vā, aivamaiva imināpi bhikkhunā dīgharattaṃ rūpārammaṇādirasapānavaḍḍhitaṃ duṭṭhacittaṃ damaitukāmaina rūpādiārammaṇatau apanaitvā arañ‍ñaṃ vā…pai॰… suñ‍ñāgāraṃ vā pavaisaitvā tattha assāsapassāsathambhai satiyauttaina bandhitabbaṃ| aivamassa  taṃ cittaṃ itau citau ca vipphanditvāpi pubbai āciṇṇārammaṇaṃ alabhamānaṃ satiyauttaṃ chinditvā palāyituṃ  asak‍kauntaṃ tamaivārammaṇaṃ upacārappanāvasaina upanisīdati caiva upanipaj‍jati ca| taināhu paurāṇā –

‘‘yathā thambhai nibandhaiyya, vacchaṃ damaṃ narau idha|

bandhaiyyaivaṃ sakaṃ cittaṃ, satiyārammaṇai daḷha’’nti|| (pārā॰ aṭṭha॰ 2.165; dī॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 3.374; ma॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.107)|

aivamassaitaṃ saināsanaṃ bhāvanānurūpaṃ hauti| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘idamassa ānāpānassatisamādhibhāvanānurūpasaināsanapariggahaparidīpana’’nti|

atha vā yasmā idaṃ kammaṭṭhānappabhaidai muddhabhūtaṃ sabbañ‍ñubuddhapac‍caikabuddhabuddhasāvakānaṃ visaisādhigamadiṭṭhadhammasukhavihārapadaṭṭhānaṃ ānāpānassatikammaṭṭhānaṃ itthipurisahatthiassādisaddasamākulaṃ gāmantaṃ aparic‍cajitvā na sukaraṃ bhāvaituṃ, saddakaṇṭakattā jhānassa| agāmakai pana arañ‍ñai sukaraṃ yaugāvacaraina idaṃ kammaṭṭhānaṃ pariggahaitvā ānāpānacatutthajjhānaṃ nibbattaitvā tadaiva pādakaṃ katvā saṅkhārai sammasitvā aggaphalaṃ arahattaṃ sampāpuṇituṃ| tasmāssa anurūpasaināsanaṃ dassaintau bhagavā ‘‘arañ‍ñagatau vā’’tiādimāha|

vatthuvij‍jācariyau viya hi bhagavā, sau yathā vatthuvij‍jācariyau nagarabhūmiṃ passitvā suṭṭhu upaparikkhitvā ‘‘aittha nagaraṃ māpaithā’’ti upadisati, sautthinā ca nagarai niṭṭhitai rājakulatau mahāsak‍kāraṃ labhati, aivamaiva yaugāvacarassa anurūpasaināsanaṃ upaparikkhitvā ‘‘aittha kammaṭṭhānaṃ anuyuñ‍jitabba’’nti upadisati, tatau tattha kammaṭṭhānaṃ anuyuttaina yauginā kamaina arahattai pattai ‘‘sammāsambuddhau vata sau bhagavā’’ti mahantaṃ sak‍kāraṃ labhati|

ayaṃ pana bhikkhu dīpisadisauti vuc‍cati| yathā hi mahādīpirājā arañ‍ñai tiṇagahanaṃ vā vanagahanaṃ vā pabbatagahanaṃ vā nissāya nilīyitvā vanamahiṃsagaukaṇṇasūkarādayau migai gaṇhāti, aivamaiva ayaṃ arañ‍ñādīsu kammaṭṭhānaṃ anuyuñ‍jantau bhikkhu yathāk‍kamaina sautāpattisakadāgāmianāgāmiarahattamaggai caiva ariyaphalañ‍ca gaṇhatīti vaiditabbau| taināhu paurāṇā –

‘‘yathāpi  dīpikau nāma, nilīyitvā gaṇhatī migai|

tathaivāyaṃ buddhaputtau, yuttayaugau vipassakau|

arañ‍ñaṃ pavisitvāna, gaṇhāti phalamuttama’’nti|| (pārā॰ aṭṭha॰ 2.165; dī॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 2.374; ma॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.107)|

tainassa parak‍kamajavayauggabhūmiṃ arañ‍ñasaināsanaṃ dassaintau bhagavā ‘‘arañ‍ñagatau vā’’tiādimāha|

218. tattha arañ‍ñagatauti ‘‘arañ‍ñanti nikkhamitvā bahi indakhīlā sabbamaitaṃ arañ‍ña’’nti (vibha॰ 529) ca, ‘‘ārañ‍ñakaṃ nāma saināsanaṃ pañ‍cadhanusatikaṃ pacchima’’nti (pārā॰ 654) ca aivaṃ vuttalakkhaṇaisu arañ‍ñaisu yaṃkiñ‍ci pavivaikasukhaṃ arañ‍ñaṃ gatau| rukkhamūlagatauti rukkhasamīpaṃ gatau| suñ‍ñāgāragatauti suñ‍ñaṃ vivittaukāsaṃ gatau| aittha ca ṭhapaitvā arañ‍ñañ‍ca rukkhamūlañ‍ca avasaisasattavidhasaināsanagataupi suñ‍ñāgāragatauti vattuṃ vaṭṭati|

aivamassa ututtayānukūlaṃ dhātucariyānukūlañ‍ca ānāpānassatibhāvanānurūpaṃ saināsanaṃ upadisitvā alīnānuddhac‍capakkhikaṃ santaṃ iriyāpathaṃ upadisantau nisīdatīti āha| athassa nisaj‍jāya daḷhabhāvaṃ assāsapassāsānaṃ pavattanasukhataṃ ārammaṇapariggahūpāyañ‍ca dassaintau pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvātiādimāha| tattha pal‍laṅkanti samantatau ūrubaddhāsanaṃ| ābhujitvāti bandhitvā| ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāyāti uparimasarīraṃ ujukaṃ ṭhapaitvā| aṭṭhārasapiṭṭhikaṇṭakai kauṭiyā kauṭiṃ paṭipādaitvā| aivañhi nisīdantassa cammamaṃsanhārūni na paṇamanti| athassa yā taisaṃ paṇamanappac‍cayā khaṇai khaṇai vaidanā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ, tā na uppaj‍janti| tāsu anuppaj‍jamānāsu cittaṃ aikaggaṃ hauti, kammaṭṭhānaṃ na paripatati, vuddhiṃ phātiṃ upagacchati| parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvāti kammaṭṭhānābhimukhaṃ satiṃ ṭhapayitvā| atha vā parīti pariggahaṭṭhau| mukhanti niyyānaṭṭhau| satīti upaṭṭhānaṭṭhau| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘parimukhaṃ sati’’nti aivaṃ paṭisambhidāyaṃ (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.164) vuttanayainapaittha atthau daṭṭhabbau| tatrāyaṃ saṅkhaipau, pariggahitaniyyānaṃ satiṃ katvāti|

219. sau  satauva assasati satau passasatīti sau bhikkhu aivaṃ nisīditvā aivañ‍ca satiṃ  upaṭṭhapaitvā taṃ satiṃ avijahantau satau aiva assasati satau passasati, sataukārī hautīti vuttaṃ hauti| idāni yaihākāraihi sataukārī hauti, tai dassaituṃ dīghaṃ vā assasantautiādimāha| vuttañhaitaṃ paṭisambhidāyaṃ ‘‘sau satauva assasati satau passasatī’’ti aitassaiva vibhaṅgai –

‘‘bāttiṃsāya ākāraihi satau kārī hauti| dīghaṃ assāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau sati upaṭṭhitā hauti| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina satau kārī hauti| dīghaṃ passāsavasaina…pai॰… paṭinissaggānupassī assāsavasaina| paṭinissaggānupassī passāsavasaina cittassa aikaggataṃ avikkhaipaṃ pajānatau sati upaṭṭhitā hauti| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina satau kārī hautī’’ti (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.165)|

tattha dīghaṃ vā assasantauti dīghaṃ vā assāsaṃ pavattayantau| assāsauti bahi nikkhamanavātau| passāsauti antau pavisanavātauti vinayaṭṭhakathāyaṃ vuttaṃ| suttantaṭṭhakathāsu pana uppaṭipāṭiyā āgataṃ| tattha sabbaisampi gabbhasaiyyakānaṃ mātukucchitau nikkhamanakālai paṭhamaṃ abbhantaravātau bahi nikkhamati| pacchā bāhiravātau sukhumarajaṃ gahaitvā abbhantaraṃ pavisantau tāluṃ āhac‍ca nibbāyati| aivaṃ tāva assāsapassāsā vaiditabbā|

yā pana taisaṃ dīgharassatā, sā addhānavasaina vaiditabbā| yathā hi aukāsaddhānaṃ pharitvā ṭhitaṃ udakaṃ vā vālikā vā ‘‘dīghamudakaṃ dīghā vālikā, rassamudakaṃ rassā vālikā’’ti vuc‍cati, aivaṃ cuṇṇavicuṇṇāpi assāsapassāsā hatthisarīrai ca ahisarīrai ca taisaṃ attabhāvasaṅkhātaṃ dīghaṃ addhānaṃ saṇikaṃ pūraitvā saṇikamaiva nikkhamanti| tasmā dīghāti vuc‍canti| sunakhasasādīnaṃ attabhāvasaṅkhātaṃ rassaṃ addhānaṃ sīghaṃ pūraitvā sīghamaiva nikkhamanti, tasmā rassāti vuc‍canti| manussaisu pana kaici hatthiahiādayau viya kāladdhānavasaina dīghaṃ assasanti ca passasanti ca| kaici sunakhasasādayau viya rassaṃ, tasmā taisaṃ kālavasaina dīghamaddhānaṃ  nikkhamantā ca pavisantā ca tai ‘‘dīghā’’ ittaramaddhānaṃ nikkhamantā ca pavisantā ca ‘‘rassā’’ti vaiditabbā|

tatrāyaṃ  bhikkhu navahākāraihi dīghaṃ assasantau passasantau ca ‘‘dīghaṃ assasāmi, passasāmī’’ti pajānāti| aivaṃ pajānatau cassa aikainākāraina kāyānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānabhāvanā sampaj‍jatīti vaiditabbā| yathāha paṭisambhidāyaṃ (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.166) –

‘‘kathaṃ dīghaṃ assasantau dīghaṃ assasāmīti pajānāti| dīghaṃ passasantau dīghaṃ passasāmīti pajānāti| dīghaṃ assāsaṃ addhānasaṅkhātai assasati| dīghaṃ passāsaṃ addhānasaṅkhātai passasati| dīghaṃ assāsapassāsaṃ addhānasaṅkhātai assasatipi passasatipi| dīghaṃ assāsapassāsaṃ addhānasaṅkhātai assasataupi passasataupi chandau uppaj‍jati| chandavasaina tatau sukhumataraṃ dīghaṃ assāsaṃ addhānasaṅkhātai assasati| chandavasaina tatau sukhumataraṃ dīghaṃ passāsaṃ…pai॰… dīghaṃ assāsapassāsaṃ addhānasaṅkhātai assasatipi passasatipi| chandavasaina tatau sukhumataraṃ dīghaṃ assāsapassāsaṃ addhānasaṅkhātai assasataupi passasataupi pāmauj‍jaṃ uppaj‍jati| pāmauj‍javasaina tatau sukhumataraṃ dīghaṃ assāsaṃ addhānasaṅkhātai assasati| pāmauj‍javasaina tatau sukhumataraṃ dīghaṃ passāsaṃ…pai॰… dīghaṃ assāsapassāsaṃ addhānasaṅkhātai assasatipi passasatipi| pāmauj‍javasaina tatau sukhumataraṃ dīghaṃ assāsapassāsaṃ addhānasaṅkhātai assasataupi passasataupi dīghaṃ assāsapassāsā cittaṃ vivattati, upaikkhā saṇṭhāti| imaihi navahi ākāraihi dīghaṃ assāsapassāsā kāyau| upaṭṭhānaṃ sati| anupassanā ñāṇaṃ| kāyau upaṭṭhānaṃ, nau sati| sati upaṭṭhānañ‍caiva sati ca| tāya satiyā taina ñāṇaina taṃ kāyaṃ anupassati| taina vuc‍cati kāyai kāyānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānabhāvanā’’ti|

aisa  nayau rassapadaipi| ayaṃ pana visaisau, yathā aittha ‘‘dīghaṃ assāsaṃ addhānasaṅkhātai’’ti vuttaṃ, aivamidha ‘‘rassaṃ assāsaṃ ittarasaṅkhātai assasatī’’ti āgataṃ| tasmā rassavasaina yāva ‘‘taina vuc‍cati kāyai kāyānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānabhāvanā’’ti, tāva yaujaitabbaṃ|

aivaṃ ayaṃ addhānavasaina ittaravasaina ca imaihākāraihi assāsapassāsai pajānantau dīghaṃ vā assasantau dīghaṃ assasāmīti pajānāti…pai॰… rassaṃ vā passasantau rassaṃ passasāmīti pajānātīti vaiditabbau| aivaṃ pajānatau cassa –

dīghau  rassau ca assāsau,

passāsaupi ca tādisau|

cattārau vaṇṇā vattanti,

nāsikaggaiva bhikkhunauti|| (pārā॰ aṭṭha॰ 2.165)|

220. sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmi…pai॰… passasissāmīti sikkhatīti sakalassa assāsakāyassa ādimajjhapariyausānaṃ viditaṃ karauntau pākaṭaṃ karauntau assasissāmīti sikkhati| sakalassa passāsakāyassa ādimajjhapariyausānaṃ viditaṃ karauntau pākaṭaṃ karauntau passasissāmīti sikkhati| aivaṃ viditaṃ karauntau pākaṭaṃ karauntau ñāṇasampayuttacittaina assasati caiva passasati ca| tasmā ‘‘assasissāmi passasissāmī’’ti sikkhatīti vuc‍cati| aikassa hi bhikkhunau cuṇṇavicuṇṇavisaṭai assāsakāyai passāsakāyai vā ādi pākaṭau hauti, na majjhapariyausānaṃ| sau ādimaiva pariggahaituṃ sak‍kauti, majjhapariyausānai kilamati| aikassa majjhaṃ pākaṭaṃ hauti, na ādipariyausānaṃ| aikassa pariyausānaṃ pākaṭaṃ hauti, na ādimajjhaṃ| sau pariyausānaṃyaiva pariggahaituṃ sak‍kauti, ādimajjhai kilamati| aikassa sabbampi pākaṭaṃ hauti, sau sabbampi pariggahaituṃ sak‍kauti, na katthaci kilamati, tādisaina bhavitabbanti dassaintau āha – ‘‘sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmīti…pai॰… passasissāmīti sikkhatī’’ti|

tattha  sikkhatīti aivaṃ ghaṭati vāyamati| yau vā tathābhūtassa saṃvarau, ayamaittha adhisīlasikkhā| yau tathābhūtassa samādhi, ayaṃ adhicittasikkhā| yā tathābhūtassa pañ‍ñā, ayaṃ adhipañ‍ñāsikkhāti imā tissau sikkhāyau tasmiṃ ārammaṇai tāya satiyā taina manasikāraina sikkhati āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarautīti aivamaittha atthau daṭṭhabbau|

tattha yasmā purimanayai kaivalaṃ assasitabbaṃ passasitabbamaiva, na ca añ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍ci kātabbaṃ| itau paṭṭhāya pana ñāṇuppādanādīsu yaugau karaṇīyau| tasmā tattha assasāmīti pajānāti passasāmīti pajānātic‍caiva vattamānakālavasaina pāḷiṃ vatvā itau paṭṭhāya kattabbassa ñāṇuppādanādinau ākārassa dassanatthaṃ sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmītiādinā nayaina anāgatavacanavasaina pāḷi āraupitāti vaiditabbā|

passambhayaṃ  kāyasaṅkhāraṃ assasissāmīti…pai॰… passasissāmīti sikkhatīti auḷārikaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ passambhaintau paṭippassambhaintau niraudhaintau vūpasamaintau assasissāmi passasissāmīti sikkhati| tatra aivaṃ auḷārikasukhumatā ca passaddhi ca vaiditabbā| imassa hi bhikkhunau pubbai apariggahitakālai kāyau ca cittañ‍ca sadarathā haunti auḷārikā| kāyacittānaṃ auḷārikattai avūpasantai assāsapassāsāpi auḷārikā haunti, balavatarā hutvā pavattanti, nāsikā nappahauti, mukhaina assasantaupi passasantaupi tiṭṭhati| yadā panassa kāyaupi cittampi pariggahitā haunti, tadā tai santā haunti vūpasantā| taisu vūpasantaisu assāsapassāsā sukhumā hutvā pavattanti, ‘‘atthi nu khau natthī’’ti vicaitabbatākārappattā haunti|

saiyyathāpi purisassa dhāvitvā, pabbatā vā aurauhitvā, mahābhāraṃ vā sīsatau auraupaitvā ṭhitassa auḷārikā assāsapassāsā haunti, nāsikā nappahauti, mukhaina assasantaupi passasantaupi tiṭṭhati| yadā panaisa taṃ parissamaṃ vinaudaitvā nhatvā ca pivitvā ca al‍lasāṭakaṃ hadayai katvā sītāya chāyāya nipan‍nau hauti, athassa tai assāsapassāsā  sukhumā haunti ‘‘atthi nu khau natthī’’ti vicaitabbatākārappattā, aivamaiva imassa bhikkhunau pubbai apariggahitakālai kāyau ca…pai॰… vicaitabbatākārappattā haunti| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hissa pubbai apariggahitakālai ‘‘auḷārikauḷārikai kāyasaṅkhārai passambhaimī’’ti ābhaugasaman‍nāhāramanasikārapac‍cavaikkhaṇā natthi, pariggahitakālai pana atthi| tainassa apariggahitakālatau pariggahitakālai kāyasaṅkhārau sukhumau hauti| taināhu paurāṇā –

‘‘sāraddhai kāyai cittai ca, adhimattaṃ pavattati|

asāraddhamhi kāyamhi, sukhumaṃ sampavattatī’’ti|| (pārā॰ aṭṭha॰ 2.165)|

221. pariggahaipi auḷārikau, paṭhamajjhānupacārai sukhumau| tasmimpi auḷārikau, paṭhamajjhānai sukhumau| paṭhamajjhānai ca dutiyajjhānupacārai ca auḷārikau, dutiyajjhānai sukhumau| dutiyajjhānai ca tatiyajjhānupacārai ca auḷārikau, tatiyajjhānai sukhumau| tatiyajjhānai ca catutthajjhānupacārai ca auḷārikau, catutthajjhānai atisukhumau appavattimaiva pāpuṇātīti| idaṃ tāva dīghabhāṇakasaṃyuttabhāṇakānaṃ mataṃ|

majjhimabhāṇakā  pana paṭhamajjhānai auḷārikau, dutiyajjhānupacārai sukhumauti aivaṃ haiṭṭhimahaiṭṭhimajjhānatau uparūparijjhānupacāraipi sukhumataramicchanti| sabbaisañ‍ñaiva pana mataina apariggahitakālai pavattakāyasaṅkhārau pariggahitakālai paṭippassambhati| pariggahitakālai pavattakāyasaṅkhārau paṭhamajjhānupacārai…pai॰… catutthajjhānupacārai pavattakāyasaṅkhārau catutthajjhānai paṭippassambhati| ayaṃ tāva samathai nayau|

vipassanāyaṃ pana apariggahai pavattau kāyasaṅkhārau auḷārikau, mahābhūtapariggahai sukhumau| saupi auḷārikau, upādārūpapariggahai sukhumau| saupi auḷārikau, sakalarūpapariggahai sukhumau| saupi auḷārikau, arūpapariggahai sukhumau| saupi auḷārikau, rūpārūpapariggahai sukhumau| saupi auḷārikau, pac‍cayapariggahai sukhumau| saupi auḷārikau, sappac‍cayanāmarūpapariggahai sukhumau| saupi auḷārikau, lakkhaṇārammaṇikavipassanāya sukhumau| saupi dubbalavipassanāya auḷārikau, balavavipassanāya sukhumau| tattha pubbai vuttanayainaiva purimassa purimassa pacchimaina  pacchimaina paṭippassaddhi vaiditabbā| aivamaittha auḷārikasukhumatā ca passaddhi ca vaiditabbā|

paṭisambhidāyaṃ (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.171) panassa saddhiṃ caudanāsaudhanāhi aivamatthau vuttau –
